The name of the method in the specified entrypoint assembly and type to hook and load Chainloader from.=指定入口程序集和类型中要挂钩的方法名称；将通过该方法加载 Chainloader。
The name of the type in the entrypoint assembly to search for the entrypoint method.=入口程序集内的类型名称，用于在其中查找入口方法。
This can be used by developers to inspect and debug preloader patchers.=开发者可用此选项检查和调试预加载器补丁程序。
This can be used to be able to load patched assemblies into debuggers like dnSpy.=可用此选项将修补后的程序集加载到 dnSpy 等调试器中。
This can be used with debuggers like dnSpy to install breakpoints into patched assemblies before they are loaded.=可配合 dnSpy 等调试器，在修补后的程序集加载前为其设置断点。
This can fix loading issues in some games that attempt to prevent BepInEx from being loaded.=可修复某些试图阻止 BepInEx 加载的游戏所出现的加载问题。
This setting should only be used for development purposes (e.g. debugging in dnSpy). Other code might override this setting.=此设置仅供开发使用（例如在 dnSpy 中调试）。其他代码可能会覆盖此设置。
This should always be true, unless you cannot start the game due to a Harmony related issue (such as running .NET Standard runtime) or you know what you're doing.=此项通常应保持为 true；只有在 Harmony 相关问题（例如使用 .NET Standard 运行时）导致游戏无法启动，或你明确了解此选项时才更改。
Use this only if you know what this option means, as it can affect functionality of some older plugins.=此选项可能影响部分旧版插件的功能，仅在明确了解其含义时使用。
Which log leves are saved to the disk log output.=指定保存到磁盘日志文件的日志级别。

// BetterScaling.KoikatsuSunshine.dll
Scaled TNO ({0}) initialising {1} children!=已缩放的 TNO（{0}）正在初始化 {1} 个子级！
Scale folders=缩放文件夹
Makes it possible to scale folders. You need to save/reload the scene after changing this.=允许缩放文件夹。更改此项后需要保存并重新载入场景。
Logaritchmic scaling=对数缩放
The bigger the scale, the faster it scales! And the smaller the scale, the slower it goes. Allows better control across all scales.=缩放值越大，缩放越快；缩放值越小，变化越慢。可以在任意缩放范围内获得更好的控制。
Prevent zero-scale=防止零缩放
Makes it impossible to scale anything to 0 on any axis, because that makes objects disappear. The minimum scale on any axis is one one millionth. Does not prevent negative scales.=禁止在任何轴向上将物体缩放到 0，因为那样会使物体消失。任一轴向的最小缩放为百万分之一。不会阻止负值缩放。
Logging=日志
Enable plugin=启用插件
REQUIRES RESTART! Enable/disable the plugin entirely. DO NOT save / modify current scene after changing this setting!=需要重启！完全启用/停用此插件。更改此设置后请勿保存/修改当前场景！
Hierarchy scaling=层级缩放
REQUIRES RESTART! Enable toggling of hierarchy scaling. Mark objects in the Workspace window. Marked objects will scale their direct children by their own scale. DO NOT save / modify current scene after changing this setting! WARNING: Toggling this off (default is ON) might break compatibility with scenes created with this setting turned ON.=需要重启！启用层级缩放开关。在工作区窗口中标记物件，被标记的物件会按自身缩放来缩放其直接子级。更改此设置后请勿保存/修改当前场景！警告：关闭此项（默认开启）可能会破坏与开启此设置时创建的场景的兼容性。
Auto-adjust scales=自动调整缩放
Auto-adjust item scaling when toggling hierarchy-scaling or parenting to/releasing an item from a hierarchy-scaled object, in order to maintain a constant apparent size.=切换层级缩放，或将物品设为层级缩放物件的子级/从中解除时，自动调整物品缩放，以保持看起来的大小不变。
Disable Folder Scale Warning=停用文件夹缩放警告

Reading description file from =正在读取说明文件：
1 - Load value of key Answer from TextScenario.Vars. If doesn't exist, get a default value.=1 - 从 TextScenario.Vars 读取键 Answer 的值。不存在时取默认值。
2 - Apply Formula to the value from dictionary and Value.=2 - 用字典中的值和 Value 执行 Formula。
3 - Store the result of the Formula back to the dictionary under the key Answer.=3 - 将 Formula 的结果存回字典中键 Answer 下。
The Formula can be: 0 \= assign, 1 \= add, 2 \= subtract, 3 \= multiply, 4 \= divide (seems to be buggy? need to set this manually in adv command list?) Other variables can be used for all parameters.=Formula 可为：0 \= 赋值，1 \= 加，2 \= 减，3 \= 乘，4 \= 除（似乎有 bug？需要在 adv 命令列表里手动设置？）所有参数都可以使用其他变量。
Set the result of Lerp of floats A and B at point T to variable of key Answer.=将浮点数 A 和 B 在 T 点的 Lerp 结果存入键 Answer 的变量。
Other variables can be used for all parameters (use their keys instead of numbers then).=所有参数都可以使用其他变量（此时用它们的键代替数字）。
Set the result of LerpAngle of floats A and B at point T to variable of key Answer.=将浮点数 A 和 B 在 T 点的 LerpAngle 结果存入键 Answer 的变量。
Set the result of InverseLerp of floats A and B at point T to variable of key Answer.=将浮点数 A 和 B 在 T 点的 InverseLerp 结果存入键 Answer 的变量。
Store result of string.Format("Format", Args) to variable of key Variable. Args is a comma separated list of arguments.=将 string.Format("Format", Args) 的结果存入键 Variable 的变量。Args 是以逗号分隔的参数列表。
Based on variables Left and Right, jump to Tag under either True or False. Center specifies the comparison type:  "\=\=" (0)=根据变量 Left 和 Right，跳转到 True 或 False 下的 Tag。Center 指定比较方式：  "\=\=" (0)
"check" (6): check if a variable with key Left exists.="check" (6)：检查键为 Left 的变量是否存在。
Play a voice clip on character number No. Personality and Pitch can be left empty to use the character's values. Must be set to Multi and be used before a Text command to work.=在编号为 No 的角色上播放语音。Personality 和 Pitch 可留空以使用角色自身的值。必须设为 Multi 并在 Text 命令之前使用才有效。
Voices are stored in abdata\\sound\\data\\pcm\\c00 (00 \= id of the personality). Inside that, different folders correspond to different game modes (adm is generic sounds, adv is spoken lines, h is h scene stuff, namelist is nicknames). You can preview the audio clips and see their names in SB3U.=语音存放在 abdata\\sound\\data\\pcm\\c00（00 \= 性格 ID）。其中不同的文件夹对应不同的游戏模式（adm 为通用音效，adv 为台词，h 为 H 场景相关，namelist 为昵称）。可以在 SB3U 中试听音频并查看其名称。

True by default, if set to false user can move the camera freely during the ADV scene.=默认为 True，设为 false 时用户可以在 ADV 场景中自由移动镜头。

The heroine has to be passed in the arguments when starting the ADV scene. Heroine IDs are calculated like this: (index * -1) - 2=开始 ADV 场景时必须在参数中传入该女主角。女主角 ID 的计算方式：(index * -1) - 2

State: name of the animation state on the character's animator (e.g. Stand_00_00)=State：角色动画器中的动画状态名（如 Stand_00_00）
Setting rest of the arguments will load a different RuntimeAnimatorController into the character's animator. By default animator at abdata\\adv\\motion\\controller\\adv\\00.unity3d > cf_adv_00_00 is used, so check it for available clips. You can preview the clips in SB3U.=设置其余参数会将另一个 RuntimeAnimatorController 加载到角色的动画器中。默认使用 abdata\\adv\\motion\\controller\\adv\\00.unity3d > cf_adv_00_00 中的动画器，可在其中查看可用的片段。可以在 SB3U 中预览片段。

Disabled nodes will output FALSE, no matter the input=禁用的节点无论输入如何都输出 FALSE
Select all downstream nodes of the selected nodes by pressing T (Tree)=按 T（树）选择选中节点的所有下游节点

INPUTS turn on/off according to the clothing state they represent=输入节点根据其代表的服装状态开关
ACCESSORY INPUTS turn on/off according to the accessory slot they represent=饰品输入节点根据其代表的饰品栏位开关
Add ACCESSORY INPUTS by clicking the button in the accessory UI=在饰品界面中点击按钮可添加饰品输入节点
OUTPUTS control the according accessory slot=输出节点控制对应的饰品栏位
Add OUTPUTs by clicking the button in the accessory UI=在饰品界面中点击按钮可添加输出节点
NOT-GATES output the opposite of their input=非门输出与输入相反的值
AND-GATES turn on if BOTH inputs are on=与门在两个输入都为开时打开
OR-GATES turn on if ONE OR BOTH inputs are on=或门在一个或两个输入为开时打开
XOR-GATES turn on if EXACTLY ONE input is on=异或门在恰好一个输入为开时打开
Add these via the menu on the right=通过右侧菜单添加
You can connect them to however many nodes you want them to control=可将其连接到任意多个要控制的节点
They have an integer state as indicated by their label, you can cycle this state via the < and > buttons=它们有一个整数状态（见其标签），可通过 < 和 > 按钮循环切换
Right click the output handle of the node to specify active outputs for the current state=右键单击节点的输出端，可指定当前状态下激活的输出
If they receive no input or an ON input, they will output as normal, however, on an OFF input, they will only output OFF=若没有输入或输入为开，则正常输出；若输入为关，则只输出关
HAND PATTERN is on if the specified hand is set to the specified pattern=手部模式：指定的手设为指定模式时为开
EYE PATTERN is on if the eyes are set to the specified pattern=眼部模式：眼睛设为指定模式时为开
EYE THRESHOLD is on if the eye are MORE or LESS OR EQUALLY open compared to the specified threshold=眼部阈值：眼睛睁开程度大于、或小于等于指定阈值时为开
MOUTH PATTERN is on if the moth is set to the specified pattern=嘴部模式：嘴设为指定模式时为开
MOUTH THRESHOLD is on if the mouth is MORE or LESS OR EQUALLY open compared to the specified theshold=嘴部阈值：嘴张开程度大于、或小于等于指定阈值时为开
EYEBROW PATTERN is on if the eyesbrows are set to the specified pattern=眉毛模式：眉毛设为指定模式时为开
Feature is experimental, no guarantees!!=功能为实验性质，不作任何保证！！
Tries to convert Accessory State Sync data saved in the card to a ANAL graph=尝试将角色卡中保存的 Accessory State Sync 数据转换为 ANAL 图
Only Accessories with ONE or TWO connected clothing slots are supported=仅支持连接了一个或两个服装栏位的饰品
The generated nodes will not be sorted properly and overlap=生成的节点不会正确排列，并会互相重叠

Accessories slot is not enough! Discard =饰品栏位不足！丢弃
When performing coordinate changes in Maker, you may encounter an error here. =在角色编辑器中更换服装时，这里可能会出错。
The implementation of changing coordinates optionally is to read and apply the coordinate to the dummy first, and then pick up the wanted parts. =选择性更换服装的实现方式是先将服装读取并应用到替身上，再挑出需要的部分。
The design consideration of KKSAPI is that ""only one character will loaded in Maker"", which causes plugins that rely on KKSAPI to fail to initialize the dummy. =KKSAPI 的设计前提是“角色编辑器中只会读取一个角色”，这会导致依赖 KKSAPI 的插件无法初始化替身。
If you encounter errors, please try to install IllusionFixes.NullChecks, it can easily bypass the situation. =如果遇到错误，请尝试安装 IllusionFixes.NullChecks，它可以轻松绕过这种情况。
Since the dummy does not use other unsupported plugin data, their initialization failed does not affect the result of the coordinate change.=由于替身不使用其他不受支持的插件数据，它们初始化失败不会影响更换服装的结果。

// KKS_Fix_CameraTarget.dll
Unlock cursor when Cam center is off=镜头中心关闭时解锁光标
By default cursor will be unlocked when the camera center point is hidden. This plugin stops that from happening, so the cursor is locked by default. Turn this on to restore the original behavior.=默认情况下，隐藏镜头中心点时会解锁光标。本插件阻止这种情况，使光标默认锁定。开启此项可恢复原本的行为。
Changes take effect after studio resteart.=更改在重启 Studio 后生效。

// KKS_Fix_MainGameOptimizations.dll
Main Game Optimizations {{A}}\n...=主游戏优化 {{A}}\n...
Main Game Optimizations {{A}}=主游戏优化 {{A}}
Didn't find target=没有找到目标
Tweaks=微调
Pause dynamic bones outside range=暂停范围外的动态骨骼
Async clothes loading=异步载入衣服
Preload characters on initial load=初次载入时预载入角色
Forces all characters to load during initial load into school mode. Slightly longer loading time but eliminates large stutters when unseen characters enter current map.=初次载入学校模式时强制载入所有角色。载入时间略长，但可以消除未出现的角色进入当前地图时的大幅卡顿。
Throttle chara blend shape updates=限制角色形态键更新
Reduces the amount of unnecessary blend shape updates. Performance improvement in main game, especially with over 20 characters in one room.=减少不必要的形态键更新。可提升本篇性能，在一个房间里有超过 20 个角色时尤其明显。
Throttle dynamic bone updates=限制动态骨骼更新
Pause dynamic bones outside camera view=暂停镜头视野外的动态骨骼
Character.chaControls not found=未找到 Character.chaControls
Spread loading of clothes in school roam mode over multiple frames. Greatly reduces seemingly random stutters when characters change clothes somewhere in the world.=在校园自由行动模式下，将服装的读取分摊到多帧中。可大幅减少角色在世界某处换衣服时看似随机的卡顿。
Warning: In rare cases can cause some visual glitches like 2 coordinates loaded at once.=警告：极少数情况下可能导致画面故障，例如同时读取了 2 套服装。
Stops dynamic bone physics in roaming mode for characters that are far away or not visible. Performance improvement in main game, especially with over 20 characters.=在自由行动模式下，停止远处或不可见角色的动态骨骼物理。可提升本篇游戏的性能，角色超过 20 个时尤其明显。
Warning: In rare cases can cause some physics glitches.=警告：极少数情况下可能导致物理故障。
Stops dynamic bone physics in roaming mode for characters that are further than this many units away from player (1 unit is roughly 1m).=在自由行动模式下，停止与玩家距离超过此单位数的角色的动态骨骼物理（1 单位约为 1 米）。
Lower value will greatly increase FPS, but skirts might look glitchy as characters move away.=数值越低帧率越高，但角色走远时裙子可能显得异常。
Needs 'Throttle dynamic bone updates' to be enabled.=需要启用“限制动态骨骼更新”。
Stops dynamic bone physics in roaming mode for characters that are outside of the camera view (e.g. behind or to the side). Improves performance, but can cause the bones to visibly jerk into place as camera rotates to show the character.=在自由行动模式下，停止镜头视野外（如背后或侧面）角色的动态骨骼物理。可提升性能，但镜头转向角色时骨骼可能明显地跳回原位。

This setting prevents the character's limbs from being readjusted to match body proportions. It can fix weirdly bent limbs on characters that use ABMX sliders, but will break Stiletto if it's installed.=此设置可防止角色四肢为匹配身体比例而被重新调整。可修复使用 ABMX 滑块的角色四肢弯曲怪异的问题，但如果安装了 Stiletto 会导致其失效。

Performance improvement in maker if there are many new items.=新物品很多时可提升编辑器性能。
Turn off the New! mark on items in character maker that weren't used yet.=关闭角色编辑器中未使用过的物品上的 New! 标记。

Major load time reduction and performance improvement in character maker. Eliminates lag when switching tabs.=大幅缩短角色编辑器的读取时间并提升性能。消除切换标签页时的卡顿。
Can cause some compatibility issues with other plugins.=可能与其他插件产生兼容性问题。
Automatically scroll maker lists to show their selected items in view (usually after loading a character card).=自动滚动编辑器列表，使所选物品显示在视野内（通常在读取角色卡之后）。

// KKS_Fix_ResourceUnloadOptimizations.dll
Resource Unload Optimizations {{A}}=资源卸载优化 {{A}}
Resource Unload Optimizations {{A}}\n...=资源卸载优化 {{A}}\n...
Disable Resource Unload=停用资源卸载
Disables all resource unloading. Requires large amounts of RAM or will likely crash your game.=停用所有资源卸载。需要大量内存，否则很可能导致游戏崩溃。
Optimize Memory Usage=优化内存使用
Use more memory (if available) in order to load the game faster and reduce random stutter.=使用更多内存（如果可用）以加快游戏载入并减少随机卡顿。
Optimize Memory Threshold=优化内存阈值
Minimum amount of memory to be used before resource unloading will run.=开始卸载资源前需要使用的最低内存量。
Optimize Memory Threshold During Load=载入期间的优化内存阈值
Minimum amount of memory to be used during load before resource unloading will run (should be lower than 'Optimize Memory Threshold').=载入期间开始卸载资源前需要使用的最低内存量（应低于“优化内存阈值”）。

// KKS_ForceHighPoly.dll
Force High Poly {{A}}\n...=强制高模 {{A}}\n...
Force High Poly {{A}}=强制高模 {{A}}
Force High Poly Models=强制使用高多边形模型
High poly mode=高多边形模式
Whether or not to load high poly models in the main game roaming mode (has no effect outside of that). Improves quality of characters and fixes some modded items not appearing.=是否在本篇游戏自由行动模式下读取高面数模型（在其他模式下无效）。可提升角色质量，并修复部分模组物品不显示的问题。
May require exiting to main menu to take effect.=可能需要退回主菜单后才能生效。
None: Original behavior - Some characters may throw errors and walk around with glitched clothing.=无：原本的行为 —— 部分角色可能报错，并穿着显示异常的服装走动。
Partial: Use high poly models only if necessary. Higher resource usage and longer load times when using some modded clothes.=部分：仅在必要时使用高面数模型。使用某些模组服装时资源占用更高、读取时间更长。
Full: Always use high poly models. Best quality but very high resource usage. At least 8GB of RAM is recommended with ~10 characters. Load times can be 3x longer.=完全：总是使用高面数模型。质量最好，但资源占用非常高。约 10 个角色时建议至少 8GB 内存。读取时间可能延长到 3 倍。
Original setting kept due to external dependency, no longer used.=因外部依赖而保留的原设置，已不再使用。
Use the 'Force High Poly Models' setting instead.=请改用“强制高面数模型”设置。
Partial=按需启用

Adjust the horizontal offset of the texture. It can move the texture left or right.=调整纹理的水平偏移，可将纹理向左或向右移动。

Adjust the horizontal scale of the texture. Values greater than 1 make the texture appear smaller horizontally, values less than 1 make it appear larger horizontally.=调整纹理的水平缩放。大于 1 时纹理在水平方向上显得更小，小于 1 时显得更大。

Overlay export/open folder=叠加层导出/打开文件夹
The value needs to be a valid full path to an existing folder. Default folder will be used if the value is invalid. Exported overlays will be saved there, and by default open overlay dialog will show this directory.=该值必须是已存在文件夹的有效完整路径。值无效时使用默认文件夹。导出的叠加层会保存在那里，打开叠加层对话框时也默认显示该目录。
Compress overlay textures in RAM=在内存中压缩叠加纹理
Reduces RAM usage to about 1/4th at the cost of lower quality. Use when loading lots of characters with overlays if you're running out of memory.=以降低画质为代价，将内存占用减少到约 1/4。载入大量带叠加层的角色且内存不足时使用。
Texture size limit=纹理尺寸上限
If an overlay has a higher resolution than the base image, allow the finished texture to be this much larger than the base image size. Original will force the same resolution as the original texture, OriginalX2 will allow sizes up to twice as large. Increases quality at the cost of higher memory usage, turn off if you're running out of memory. If using Unlimited, consider enabling texture compression. Changes take effect after a scene reload.=若叠加层分辨率高于底图，允许最终纹理比底图大出多少。Original 强制与原始纹理分辨率相同，OriginalX2 允许最大到两倍。可提高画质，但会占用更多内存，内存不足时请关闭。若使用 Unlimited，建议启用纹理压缩。重新载入场景后生效。

// KKS_PerspectiveX.dll
PerspectiveX {{A}}\n...=第一人称视角（PerspectiveX） {{A}}\n...
PerspectiveX {{A}}=第一人称视角（PerspectiveX） {{A}}
Toggle POV=切换 POV
Previous POV character=上一个 POV 角色
Switch the POV to the previous character while POV is active.=POV 启用时，将视角切换到上一个角色。
Next POV character=下一个 POV 角色
Switch the POV to the next character while POV is active.=POV 启用时，将视角切换到下一个角色。
Toggle FPS mouse look=切换 FPS 鼠标视角
Optional hands-free mode: locks and hides the cursor so the mouse looks around directly, like an FPS. Press again to get the cursor back. The normal mode is dragging with the left mouse button.=可选的免按键模式：锁定并隐藏光标，鼠标直接控制视角，类似 FPS 游戏。再按一次即可恢复光标。普通模式下是按住鼠标左键拖动。
Roll camera left=镜头向左倾斜
Hold to tilt the camera counterclockwise.=按住使镜头逆时针倾斜。
Roll camera right=镜头向右倾斜
Hold to tilt the camera clockwise.=按住使镜头顺时针倾斜。
Reset camera roll=重置镜头倾斜
Reset the manual camera tilt back to level.=将手动调整的镜头倾斜恢复水平。
Lock camera in place=锁定镜头位置
Freeze the camera where it is so it stops following the head, e.g. during caress animations that throw the head around. Looking around still works. Press again to unlock.=将镜头固定在当前位置，不再跟随头部，例如在头部大幅晃动的爱抚动画中。仍然可以环顾四周。再按一次解锁。
Toggle presets panel=开关预设面板
Show/hide the in-game panel with view presets and saved views. In Studio the panel can also be opened with the PerspectiveX button in the bottom-left toolbar.=显示/隐藏游戏内的视角预设和已保存视角面板。在工作室中也可以通过左下角工具栏的 PerspectiveX 按钮打开该面板。
Save the current POV view (FOV, look direction, tilt, camera offsets) into this slot. Slots are kept between game sessions.=将当前 POV 视角（视野、视线方向、倾斜、镜头偏移）保存到此栏位。栏位在游戏重启后依然保留。
Recall the view saved in this slot.=调用此栏位中保存的视角。
Saved views=已保存的视角
Field of view=视野
Vertical FOV. Can also be adjusted with the scroll wheel while in POV.=垂直视野。在 POV 中也可以用鼠标滚轮调整。
Scroll wheel adjusts FOV=滚轮调整视野
Turn off if you keep changing the FOV by accident while scrolling in POV.=如果在 POV 中滚动时总是误改视野，请关闭此项。
View presets=视角预设
Custom presets=自定义预设
Mouse sensitivity=鼠标灵敏度
Comfort=舒适度
Position smoothing=位置平滑
Animation sway=动画摇晃
Pitch limit=俯仰限制
How far up/down you can look, in degrees.=可以向上/向下看的角度，单位为度。
Forward offset=前向偏移
Camera distance in front of the eyes, in meters. Raise it if you can see the inside of the head.=镜头在眼睛前方的距离，单位为米。若能看到头部内侧，请调高此值。
Up offset=向上偏移
Vertical camera offset from the eye line, in meters.=镜头相对视线的垂直偏移，单位为米。
Near clip plane=近裁剪面
Lower values let very close geometry render without being cut off.=数值越低，非常近的几何体越不会被裁掉。
Hide character head=隐藏角色头部
Hide the POV character's head (incl. hair and head accessories) so nothing clips into the view.=隐藏 POV 角色的头部（包括头发和头部饰品），避免穿模挡住视野。
Use POV during free-roam=自由行动时使用 POV
OFF by default. When you walk around the map the game already has its own first-person view (press Space) that this mod clashes with - with both on, the built-in view hides the body while this one hides the head, so the character can vanish and the camera gets jittery. While this is off, Backspace does nothing during free-roam (it still works in H-scenes and Studio). Turn it on to use PerspectiveX here anyway: it takes over and suppresses the built-in Space view, but it's less reliable than in H/Studio - if the character disappears or the view glitches, turn this back off and use the game's own Space camera instead.=默认关闭。在地图上走动时，游戏本身已有第一人称视角（按空格键），会与此模组冲突：两者同时开启时，内置视角隐藏身体、此模组隐藏头部，角色可能消失，镜头也会抖动。关闭此项时，自由行动中按 Backspace 无效（在H场景和工作室中仍可使用）。若仍想在此使用 PerspectiveX，可开启此项：它会接管并屏蔽内置的空格视角，但不如在H场景/工作室中稳定。如果角色消失或画面异常，请关闭此项，改用游戏自带的空格视角。
Align camera with body=镜头与身体对齐
Tilt the camera to follow the character's body orientation, so the view isn't kept artificially level when they're lying on their side or leaning. Fine-tune with the roll keys.=让镜头随角色身体朝向倾斜，角色侧躺或倾身时视角不会被强行保持水平。可用倾斜键微调。
Cozy (60)=舒适 (60)
Natural (90)=自然 (90)
Action (110)=动作 (110)
Type a name and save the current FOV, smoothing and camera offsets as your own preset.=输入名称，将当前的视野、平滑和镜头偏移保存为自己的预设。
Scene repair=场景修复
Versions up to 1.3.2 could save a hidden head into the scene file if you saved while in POV. This puts it back - save the scene afterwards.=1.3.2 及更早版本在 POV 中保存场景时，可能把隐藏的头部存入场景文件。此功能可将其恢复，之后请保存场景。
Saved automatically by the save-view-slot shortcuts, no need to edit by hand.=由保存视角栏位快捷键自动保存，无需手动编辑。
One-click setups for FOV, position smoothing and forward offset. Pick one, then fine-tune the sliders if you feel like it.=一键设置视野、位置平滑和前向偏移。选一个，然后可以按需微调滑块。
Managed from the in-game presets panel, no need to edit by hand.=在游戏内的预设面板中管理，无需手动编辑。
PerspectiveX POV is off during free-roam - it clashes with the game's own first-person view (Space). Turn on "Use POV during free-roam" in the plugin settings to use it here.=自由行动时 PerspectiveX 的 POV 处于关闭状态，因为它会与游戏自带的第一人称视角（空格键）冲突。要在这里使用，请在插件设置中开启“自由行动时使用 POV”。
Camera unlocked=镜头已解锁
Camera locked in place=镜头已锁定位置
Can't enter POV: no main camera found=无法进入 POV：找不到主镜头
Can't enter POV: no suitable character found=无法进入 POV：找不到合适的角色

Heads up: free-roam POV fights the game's own Space view and can make your character invisible or the camera glitchy. If it misbehaves, turn it back off and use Space instead.=注意：自由行动 POV 会与游戏自带的空格视角冲突，可能导致角色隐形或镜头异常。如果出现问题，请关闭此项，改用空格视角。
) to bring it back.=）即可恢复。

How much head motion from animations is filtered out of the camera position. 0 \= camera rigidly locked to the head, 1 \= very smooth and floaty.=从镜头位置中过滤掉多少来自动画的头部晃动。0 \= 镜头牢牢锁定在头部，1 \= 非常平滑、轻飘。
How much of the head animation's rotation leaks into your view. 0 \= perfectly stable FPS camera, 1 \= fully follow the animation (dizzy!). Roll/tilt is always removed.=头部动画的旋转有多少会传到你的视角中。0 \= 完全稳定的第一人称镜头，1 \= 完全跟随动画（会晕！）。翻滚/倾斜始终会被去除。

// KKS_PoseTools.dll

Load Skirt FK=载入裙子 FK

Enable pregnancy progression=启用怀孕进程
If on, belly will progressively get bigger. No other effects.=开启后，肚子会逐渐变大，没有其他影响。
If off, the character can't get pregnant and current pregnancy will stop progressing.=关闭后，角色无法怀孕，当前的怀孕也会停止进展。
How likely this character is to get pregnant.=此角色怀孕的可能性。
Week of pregnancy=怀孕周数
The only way for male characters to get pregnant is to manually set this slider above 0.=男性角色只能通过手动将此滑块设为大于 0 来怀孕。
If the character is pregnant when added to the game, the pregnancy will continue from this point.=如果角色加入游戏时已怀孕，怀孕会从此处继续。
Menstruation schedule=月经周期
More risky=更多危险日
Always safe=始终安全日
Always risky=始终危险日
Changes how many risky days the character has in a cycle. Default is more safe days than risky days.=更改角色每个周期中危险日的天数。默认安全日多于危险日。
Always lactates=始终泌乳
Makes the character always have milk, even when not pregnant.=使角色即使未怀孕也始终有乳汁。
Pregnancy=怀孕
Fertility=生育能力
Enable conception=启用受孕
Allows characters to get pregnant from vaginal sex. Doesn't affect already pregnant characters.=允许角色通过阴道性交怀孕。不影响已怀孕的角色。
Enable anal conception=启用肛交受孕
Allows characters to get pregnant from anal sex. Doesn't affect already pregnant characters.=允许角色通过肛交怀孕。不影响已怀孕的角色。
Show pregnancy icon early=提前显示怀孕图标
Use custom safe/risky icons in H Scenes=在H场景中使用自定义安全日/危险日图标
Inflation=腹部膨胀
Enable inflation=启用腹部膨胀
Turn on the inflation effect.=开启腹部膨胀效果。
Open clothes at max inflation=膨胀到最大时撑开衣服
If clothes are fully on, open them when inflation reaches the max value (they 'burst' open).=如果衣服完全穿着，膨胀达到最大值时会被撑开（“爆开”）。
Cum count until full=撑满所需射精次数
How many times you have to let out inside to reach the maximum belly size.=需要在里面射几次才能使肚子达到最大尺寸。
Lactation=泌乳
Enable lactation=启用泌乳
Enable the lactation effect. For the effect to work the character has to be pregnant, or the override setting has to be enabled.=启用泌乳效果。角色必须已怀孕，或启用了覆盖设置，效果才会生效。
Time to fully refill=完全补满所需时间
How many minutes it takes to fully refill the milk. 0 is always fully refilled.=乳汁完全补满所需的分钟数。0 表示始终是满的。
Force max milk capacity=强制最大乳汁容量
If enabled, all characters will lactate and have full capacity. If off, capacity depends on the pregnancy progress.=启用后，所有角色都会泌乳且容量全满。关闭时，容量取决于怀孕进度。
Pregnancy progression speed=怀孕进展速度
Minimum fertility level=最低生育能力
When to reveal Risky/Safe/Pregnant status=何时显示危险日/安全日/怀孕状态
Inflation speed modifier=腹部膨胀速度倍率
How quickly the belly will inflate/deflate compared to normal (1x, 2x, 3x as fast).=肚子膨胀/收缩的速度相对于正常速度的倍数（1x、2x、3x）。
Pregnancy week=怀孕周数
This character didn't tell you their risky day schedule.=此角色还没有告诉你她的危险日周期。
Become closer to learn it!=关系更亲密后就能知道了！
Become close lovers to learn it!=成为亲密的恋人后就能知道了！
All schedules are forced to be hidden by the "When to reveal status" setting.=由于“何时显示状态”设置，所有周期都被强制隐藏。
This character is on a maternal leave and will not appear until it is over.=此角色正在休产假，结束前不会出现。
Consider using a rubber next time!=下次考虑戴上避孕套吧！
The character's body will slowly change.=角色的身体会慢慢变化。
The character's body will slowly change, and at the end they will temporarily leave.=角色的身体会慢慢变化，最后会暂时离开。
This character is on a risky day, be careful!=此角色今天是危险日，小心！
This character is on a safe day, have fun!=此角色今天是安全日，尽情享受吧！
Forecast for this week:=本周预报：
It's always safe!=始终是安全日！
It's always risky!=始终是危险日！
Risky=危险日
Safe=安全日
Adding particles to heroine: =正在为女主角添加粒子：
Patching =正在修补
How much faster does the in-game pregnancy progresses than the standard 40 weeks. It also reduces the time characters leave school for after birth.=游戏内怀孕进度比标准的 40 周快多少。也会缩短角色分娩后离校的时间。
x1 is 40 weeks, x2 is 20 weeks, x4 is 10 weeks, etc.=x1 为 40 周，x2 为 20 周，x4 为 10 周，以此类推。
If a character has a lower fertility level than this set, this level will be used instead. =如果角色的受孕几率低于此设置，则改用此设置。
0 - The value saved in the character card is used (30% by default)=0 - 使用角色卡中保存的数值（默认 30%）
30%, 50%, 75%, 100% - If the character card's saved value is lower, it will be raised to this level in HScenes.=30%、50%、75%、100% - 如果角色卡保存的数值较低，在 H 场景中会提高到此数值。
By default pregnancy status icon in class roster is shown after a few days or weeks (the character had a chance to do the test or noticed something is wrong).=默认情况下，班级名册中的怀孕状态图标会在几天或几周后显示（角色有机会验孕或察觉到异常）。
Turning this on will always make the icon show up at the end of the current day.=开启此项后，图标总会在当天结束时显示。
Always: You can immediately see the status of all characters.=总是：可以立即看到所有角色的状态。
Normal: You can only see status of characters you are reasonably close with.=普通：只能看到与你比较亲近的角色的状态。
Only Girlfriend: You can only see status of your girlfriends (other requirements might also apply).=仅女朋友：只能看到你女朋友的状态（可能还有其他条件）。
Never: The status is always shown as Unknown.=从不：状态总是显示为未知。
Replaces the standard safe/risky indicators with custom indicators that can also show pregnancy and unknown status. If the status is unknown you will have to listen for the voice cues instead.=用自定义指示图标替换标准的安全/危险指示图标，自定义图标还能显示怀孕和未知状态。状态未知时，需要通过语音提示来判断。

Enable Pregnancy+=启用 Pregnancy+
If disabled, you won't see any pregnant effects.=若停用，将不会看到任何怀孕效果。
Applies a preset belly shape to the character, so you don't have to deal with sliders.=为角色应用预设的肚子形状，无需手动调整滑块。
The equivalent to number of weeks pregnant for Pregnancy+.=相当于 Pregnancy+ 中的怀孕周数。
Multiplies the base inflation size by this value.=将基础膨胀尺寸乘以此值。
Make the front of the belly more or less round=使肚子前部更圆或更平
Moves the belly sphere up and down.=上下移动肚子球体。
Moves the belly sphere forward and back.=前后移动肚子球体。
Stretch the belly wider in the X direction.=在 X 方向上将肚子拉宽。
Stretch the belly taller in the Y direction.=在 Y 方向上将肚子拉高。
Shift the front of the belly up and down.=上下移动肚子前部。
Shift the front of the belly forward and back.=前后移动肚子前部。
Taper the sides of the belly in at the top and out at the bottom.  Makes an egg like shape.=使肚子两侧上窄下宽，形成蛋形。
Taper the front of the belly in at the top and out at the bottom.  Gives the belly an angle at the front.=使肚子前部上窄下宽，让前部形成角度。

// KKS_StudioTransformOrientation.dll
Child object transform orientation=子物件变换方向
Relative to Parent=相对于父级

// KKS_StudioWindowResize.dll
Expand=扩展
Amount of height expansion=高度扩展量
How much to expand the lists by. Arbitrary units. 0 is roughly the same as default. 0.5 fills almost the entire screen under default UI scale. only use values above 0.5 if you change UI scale with another plugin. Needs a studio restart to apply.=列表扩展的程度（任意单位）。0 大致与默认相同。在默认界面缩放下，0.5 几乎会占满整个屏幕。只有用其他插件更改了界面缩放时，才使用大于 0.5 的值。需要重启工作室才能生效。

// KKS_TimelineFlowControl.dll
Flow Control=流程控制
Flow command=流程命令
Condition=条件
node not found or empty=未找到节点或节点为空
DO (=执行 (
Flow Command Editor=流程命令编辑器
You can edit this later by clicking the "Use Current Value" button in Keyframe editor. Changes are applied instantly as you type.=之后可以在关键帧编辑器中点击“Use Current Value”按钮进行编辑。输入时更改会立即生效。
Undo changes=撤销更改
This plugin is mainly used for making animation loops and reusing pieces of animations. This allows a short timeline track to have a much longer playback, to avoid having to copy keyframes many times over, to easily change the amount of loop-overs if the pacing is off, and more.=此插件主要用于制作动画循环和复用动画片段。可以让较短的 Timeline 轨道播放更长时间，避免多次复制关键帧，在节奏不对时轻松修改循环次数，等等。
You can edit existing Flow Control keyframes by selecting the keyframe and pressing the "Use current value" button. These keyframes are not interpolated, commands are executed as the playback cursor passes over them.=选中已有的流程控制关键帧并点击“Use current value”按钮即可编辑。这些关键帧不会插值，播放光标经过时会执行其中的命令。
To create a simple loop that runs for 5 repetitions, add the following commands in order: SetValueToVariable count 0, MakeLabel loop, AddValueToVariable count 1, JumpToLabel loop if count LessThan 6=要创建一个重复 5 次的简单循环，请按顺序添加以下命令：SetValueToVariable count 0、MakeLabel loop、AddValueToVariable count 1、JumpToLabel loop if count LessThan 6
This does nothing.=此命令不执行任何操作。
Unsupported Command=不支持的命令
Unsupported Condition=不支持的条件
Time | Label name=时间 | 标签名
Variable name | Value=变量名 | 值
Clear all values=清除所有值
Command to be executed when timeline playback passes over this keyframe. Graph editor and other features related to interpolation have no effect on this, only keyframe position.=Timeline 播放经过此关键帧时要执行的命令。曲线编辑器等与插值相关的功能对其无效，只有关键帧位置有影响。
Time in seconds as seen on the left side of the main Timeline window. The value can be a variable name.=以秒为单位的时间，与 Timeline 主窗口左侧显示的一致。该值可以是变量名。
Playback cursor will be moved to the position of the current keyframe + this relative time value. The value can be a variable name.=播放光标会移动到当前关键帧位置加上此相对时间的位置。该值可以是变量名。
Value to modify the specified variable with. Can be a number or a variable name.=用于修改指定变量的值。可以是数字或变量名。
Condition attached to this command. The condition is checked every time before executing the above command during playback. If the condition ends up false, the above command won't be executed.=附加在此命令上的条件。播放期间每次执行上述命令前都会检查条件；若条件为假，则不会执行上述命令。
This command will always be executed during playback.=播放期间始终执行此命令。
You can add a condition if you want this command to only work some of the time. This can be used to, for example, make a loop that runs only a couple of times.=如果希望此命令只在部分情况下生效，可以添加条件。例如可以用来制作只运行几次的循环。
Var=变量
Error reading FlowCommand from XML: =从 XML 读取 FlowCommand 出错：
Offending XML: =出错的 XML：
Flow Command Editor - editing keyframe at =流程命令编辑器 - 正在编辑位于以下时间的关键帧：
This command will move playback back to the position of the last Jump command that was executed, and resume from there.=此命令会将播放位置移回上一次执行的 Jump 命令的位置，并从那里继续。
For example, this can be used to create a short animation clip that can be later used multiple times simply by jumping to its start (most likely a label).=例如，可以用来制作一段短动画，之后只需跳转到其开头（通常是标签）即可多次使用。
Command type: =命令类型：
Label Name: =标签名：
Name of the label. Labels can be created with the MakeLabel command, and then jumped to with the JumpToLabel command.=标签的名称。标签可用 MakeLabel 命令创建，再用 JumpToLabel 命令跳转。
Jumping to a label immediately moves playback cursor over the first MakeLabel keyframe with that label name that satisfies its condition (if MakeLabel has a condition that fails, the search continues). If no valid labels are found the jump is ignored.=跳转到标签时，播放光标会立即移到第一个具有该标签名且满足其条件的 MakeLabel 关键帧（如果 MakeLabel 的条件不满足，则继续搜索）。如果找不到有效的标签，则忽略跳转。
Playback time in seconds: =播放时间（秒）：
Relative time in seconds (+/-): =相对时间（秒，+/-）：
Variable Name: =变量名：
Name of the variable. Variables can be used in conditions and as values of most commands.=变量的名称。变量可用于条件，也可作为大多数命令的值。
Variables don't have to be defined before use, every variable has a value of 0 by default.=变量无需事先定义，每个变量默认值为 0。
Variable values are NOT reset when stopping playback! If you need to reset a variable, add commands at the start to set it to 0.=停止播放时变量值不会重置！如果需要重置变量，请在开头添加将其设为 0 的命令。
Variable name or any text: =变量名或任意文本：
Text to show in the top left corner of the game (and write to log).=显示在游戏左上角的文本（并写入日志）。
If the text is a valid label name, the value of the variable with that name will be shown.=如果文本是有效的变量名，则会显示该变量的值。
Condition: =条件：
Left parameter: =左参数：
Can be either a decimal number or a name of a variable.=可以是小数，也可以是变量名。
The condition is evaluated as: 'Left Parameter' Condition 'Right Parameter'=条件的判断方式为：“左参数” 条件 “右参数”
For example: variableName Equals 69  - This will only run the command if value of variable 'variableName' is equal to the number 69.=例如：variableName Equals 69 —— 只有当变量“variableName”的值等于数字 69 时才执行该命令。
Right parameter: =右参数：

Advanced Bone Sliders - =高级骨骼滑块 -
Character: =角色：
Bones adjusted with yellow ABMX sliders in maker tabs are automatically added to this window.=在编辑器标签页中用黄色 ABMX 滑块调整过的骨骼会自动添加到此窗口。
Hover over bones in the list below to see notes (only some bones have them). Bones commented with "ANIMCOR" are used by animation correction system, adjusting them can cause weird effects during gameplay.=将鼠标悬停在下方列表中的骨骼上可查看说明（仅部分骨骼有说明）。标注为“ANIMCOR”的骨骼用于动画校正系统，调整它们可能会在游戏中造成奇怪的效果。
BL \= Bone length. It's the distance between given bone and its parent. In some cases (like cf_j_spine02/03) effect is pretty obvious, in some not so much. Offset and rotation controls can be used instead, but the effect may be a bit different during animations.effects and glitches. If the BL slider is grayed out it means it has no effect on that bone.=BL \= 骨骼长度，即该骨骼与其父级之间的距离。有些情况下（如 cf_j_spine02/03）效果很明显，有些则不太明显。也可以改用偏移和旋转控件，但在动画中的效果可能略有不同。如果 BL 滑块显示为灰色，说明它对该骨骼无效。
Adjusting any bone also affects all of its children. To get a sense of what adjusting one bone will do, just look at its children.=调整任何骨骼也会影响它的全部子级。想知道调整某根骨骼会有什么效果，看看它的子级即可。
\u0023 cf_j_neck affects both neck and head since cf_j_head is parented to it, while cf_s_neck only affects the neck itself.=\u0023 cf_j_neck 同时影响颈部和头部，因为 cf_j_head 是它的子级；而 cf_s_neck 只影响颈部本身。
\u0023 cf_d_bust01_L affects entire left breast, cf_s_bust01_L only affects root part of it.=\u0023 cf_d_bust01_L 影响整个左胸，cf_s_bust01_L 只影响其根部。
Dont be too afraid to move and rotate bones around, sometimes this is the only way to make things look good (broad shoulders for example)=不要太害怕移动和旋转骨骼，有时这是让效果好看的唯一方法（例如宽肩）。
If adjusting cf_d_* / j_s_* / cf_s_* bones seem to do the same thing, use cf_s_* - they are least likely to cause issues.=如果调整 cf_d_* / j_s_* / cf_s_* 骨骼效果似乎相同，请使用 cf_s_*，它们最不容易出问题。
Things to keep in mind:=注意事项：
- Uneven XYZ scaling of bones with animated child bones (whole arm/leg, torso, cf_j_neck, finger roots) will produce deformities during animations.=- 对带有动画子骨骼的骨骼（整条手臂/腿、躯干、cf_j_neck、手指根部）进行不均匀的 XYZ 缩放，会在动画中产生变形。
- Rotating cf_j_* bones of the body is a bad idea - those are joits used in animation (face bones are usually fine). They may still prove useful in Studio for tweaking static poses.=- 旋转身体的 cf_j_* 骨骼不是好主意，它们是动画使用的关节（脸部骨骼通常没问题）。不过在 Studio 中调整静态姿势时可能仍然有用。
- To fix face Scale sliders are generally safe to use with no major glitches. Length, offset and rotation sliders can cause glitches and misplaced body parts if used on some bones.=- 脸部的缩放滑块一般可以放心使用，不会有严重故障。长度、偏移和旋转滑块用在某些骨骼上可能导致故障和身体部位错位。
- glithces just reload the character. Body shape glithes (breasts drifting out of place, scewed pelvis etc.) can often only be fixed by restarting character maker.=- 出现故障时重新读取角色即可。身体形状的故障（胸部偏离原位、骨盆歪斜等）往往只能通过重启角色编辑器来修复。

This will let you set different slider values for each coordinate (outfit slot).=可以为每套服装（服装栏位）分别设置滑块数值。
Modifiers set as per-coordinate are saved to coordinate cards (outfit cards) and later loaded from them (they are added to existing modifiers).=按服装设置的修改器会保存到服装卡中，之后再从中读取（会添加到已有的修改器上）。
Disabling the option will cause all coordinates to use current slider values.=禁用此选项会使所有服装都使用当前滑块数值。

on top =置顶
Copy data of this modifier to clipboard so it can be pasted into another modifier, or into any text editor to hand-edit or save for later.=将此修改器的数据复制到剪贴板，以便粘贴到其他修改器，或粘贴到任意文本编辑器中手动编辑或留作备用。
If the modifier is per-coordinate, data for all coordinates is copied.=如果修改器是按服装设置的，会复制所有服装的数据。
Hold this bone's scale/position/rotation constant in world space even when parent bones are modified.=即使父级骨骼被修改，也保持此骨骼在世界空间中的缩放/位置/旋转不变。
It's best to use a T-pose or pause the animation to avoid character's movement causing held bone to drift.=最好使用 T 字姿势或暂停动画，以免角色的动作导致被保持的骨骼漂移。
Warning: Holding scale can be inconsistent and unreliable, especially when parent bones have rotations.=警告：保持缩放可能不一致且不可靠，尤其是父级骨骼带有旋转时。
This effect only applies while the Advanced Window is open.=此效果仅在高级窗口打开时生效。
Expand maker customization options with new yellow sliders, including new categories.=在编辑器中加入新的黄色滑块（包括新分类），扩展自定义选项。
This setting doesn't affect loading and using cards that use ABMX sliders. Advanced window can still be used as normal.=此设置不影响读取和使用含 ABMX 滑块的角色卡。高级窗口仍可正常使用。
The setting takes effect after maker restart. Has no effect on advanced window.=此设置在重启编辑器后生效。对高级窗口无影响。
When enabled, all yellow scale sliders in maker are split into XYZ sliders (one for each direction). Cards made with this option will automatically enable it for relevant sliders.=启用后，编辑器中所有黄色缩放滑块都会拆分为 XYZ 滑块（每个方向一个）。用此选项制作的角色卡会自动为相关滑块启用该功能。
Note that using uneven scaling on some parts can skew the model in some animations, especially in H mode.=注意，对某些部位进行不均匀缩放可能使模型在某些动画中扭曲，尤其是在 H 模式中。
The setting takes effect immediately while inside maker. Has no effect on advanced window.=在编辑器中，此设置会立即生效。对高级窗口无影响。
Maximum values of all yellow sliders in maker are increased twofold. Can cause even more horrifying results. Recommended to only enable when working on furries and superdeformed charas.=编辑器中所有黄色滑块的最大值提高为两倍。可能产生更加骇人的效果。建议仅在制作兽人或 Q 版角色时启用。

<i>Find any Studio animation, apply it to your selected characters, and organize the list to your liking.</i>=<i>查找任意工作室动画，应用到选中的角色，并按你的喜好整理列表。</i>
The Anim Browser lists every animation Studio knows about in a category tree with thumbnails. Click an animation to apply it to the character(s) you have selected in Studio. You can also tidy the list with <b>renames</b>, bundle related animations into one <b>card</b>, and <b>merge</b> categories or whole groups so navigation is faster.=动画浏览器以带缩略图的分类树列出工作室已知的所有动画。点击动画即可应用到你在工作室中选中的角色。你还可以通过<b>重命名</b>整理列表，把相关动画打包成一张<b>卡片</b>，或<b>合并</b>分类乃至整个分组，让浏览更快捷。

Three steps to apply your first animation:=三步应用你的第一个动画：
1. In the Studio workspace tree, <b>select the character(s)</b> you want to animate.=1. 在工作室的工作区树中<b>选择要做动画的角色</b>。
2. In the Anim Browser, click a <b>sub-category</b> on the left to list its animations.=2. 在动画浏览器中点击左侧的<b>子分类</b>，列出其中的动画。
3. <b>Click an animation</b> — it is applied to every selected character at once.=3. <b>点击动画</b>，它会同时应用到每个选中的角色。
<b>Nothing happens when I click?</b>=<b>点击后没有反应？</b>
You almost certainly have no character selected in Studio, or the selected object is a prop / accessory rather than a character. The character row in the top bar shows who is targeted.=几乎可以肯定是你没有在工作室中选中角色，或者选中的是道具/饰品而不是角色。顶栏的角色行会显示目标是谁。
<b>Opening and closing the window</b>=<b>打开和关闭窗口</b>
• Toggle the browser with its <b>toolbar icon</b> on the Studio left toolbar.=• 用工作室左侧工具栏上的<b>工具栏图标</b>开关浏览器。
• <b>−</b> minimises to a small <b>AB</b> chip (drag it around; click to restore).=• <b>−</b> 会最小化为一个小的 <b>AB</b> 标签（可拖动；点击即可恢复）。
• <b>×</b> closes the browser.=• <b>×</b> 关闭浏览器。
• Drag the title bar to move; drag the <b>◢</b> corner to resize.=• 拖动标题栏可移动；拖动 <b>◢</b> 角可调整大小。
← Overview=← 概览
<b>The category tree (left)</b>=<b>分类树（左侧）</b>
• Shows animation <b>groups</b> and their <b>sub-categories</b>. Only sub-categories hold animations.=• 显示动画<b>分组</b>及其<b>子分类</b>。只有子分类包含动画。
• Click the <b>► / ▼</b> arrow to expand or collapse a group.=• 点击 <b>► / ▼</b> 箭头可展开或折叠分组。
• Click a <b>sub-category</b> to show its animations on the right.=• 点击<b>子分类</b>可在右侧显示其中的动画。
• <b>↻</b> reloads the catalog (after adding mods / animations).=• <b>↻</b> 重新载入目录（添加模组/动画之后使用）。
• Groups start collapsed the first time the catalog loads.=• 首次载入目录时，分组默认处于折叠状态。
<b>Grid vs list view</b>=<b>网格视图与列表视图</b>
The <b>Grid / List</b> button (top bar) switches between a thumbnail grid and a compact text list. Grid shows a preview per animation; List is quicker to scan when you know the names. Thumbnail size is set by <b>Card size</b> in Options.=顶栏的 <b>网格 / 列表</b> 按钮可以在缩略图网格和紧凑文本列表之间切换。网格为每个动画显示预览；知道名称时，用列表浏览更快。缩略图大小由选项中的<b>卡片大小</b>决定。
Type in the search box to filter animations by name. Matching animations are highlighted and sub-categories whose name matches are shown too. Clear the box to see everything again.=在搜索框中输入文字可按名称筛选动画。匹配的动画会高亮显示，名称匹配的子分类也会显示。清空搜索框即可重新显示全部内容。
← Getting started=← 入门
Clicking an animation applies it to <b>every</b> character selected in Studio.=点击动画会将其应用到工作室中选中的<b>每一个</b>角色。
<b>Grouped cards</b>=<b>分组卡片</b>
Some tiles are <b>grouped cards</b> that bundle related animations under one preview. Small buttons on the card pick which one to apply:=有些图块是<b>分组卡片</b>，在一个预览下打包了相关动画。可以用卡片上的小按钮选择要应用哪一个：
<b>m / f</b> (and <b>m2, f2</b>…) — the male / female version, or each participant in a paired animation.=<b>m / f</b>（以及 <b>m2、f2</b>…）：男性 / 女性版本，或配对动画中的各个参与者。
<b>in / loop / out</b> — the intro, looping, or outro part of a sequence.=<b>in / loop / out</b>：序列中的开头、循环或结尾部分。
<b>1 / 2 / 3</b>… — numbered variants when there is no gender or phase to label.=<b>1 / 2 / 3</b>…：没有性别或阶段可标注时的编号变体。
Grouped cards are created by you (see <b>Grouping animations</b>) or proposed automatically when you merge categories.=分组卡片由你创建（见<b>动画分组</b>），或在合并分类时自动提议。
<b>Selecting without applying</b>=<b>只选择不应用</b>
Each card has a small <b>checkbox</b> in the corner — use it to select animations (for grouping) without applying them.=每张卡片角落都有一个小<b>勾选框</b>，用它可以选择动画（用于分组）而不应用。
← Browsing & search=← 浏览与搜索
Open the <b>Controls</b> panel (top bar) to adjust the animation currently playing on the selected character(s). It only shows content once a character with an animation is selected.=打开顶栏的<b>控制</b>面板，可以调整选中角色当前播放的动画。只有选中正在播放动画的角色后才会显示内容。
<b>Speed</b> — slider or type a value to slow down / speed up playback.=<b>速度</b>：拖动滑块或输入数值来减慢/加快播放。
<b>Pause / Play</b> and <b>Time</b> — freeze playback and scrub to a specific moment.=<b>暂停 / 播放</b>与<b>时间</b>：冻结播放并拖动到指定时刻。
<b>Force loop</b> — keep a one-shot animation repeating.=<b>强制循环</b>：让单次播放的动画重复播放。
<b>Restart animation</b> / <b>Restart all in scene</b> — replay from the beginning.=<b>重新播放动画</b> / <b>场景内全部重新播放</b>：从头开始重放。
<b>Show items</b> — reveal the individual animations loaded on the character.=<b>显示道具</b>：显示角色身上载入的各个动画。
<b>Docked or floating</b>=<b>停靠或浮动</b>
The Controls panel can be docked beside the main window or floated as an independent window. The floating panel stays usable even when the main Anim Browser window is closed, so you can keep just the playback controls on screen.=控制面板可以停靠在主窗口旁边，也可以作为独立窗口浮动。即使关闭动画浏览器主窗口，浮动面板仍可使用，因此可以只在屏幕上保留播放控制。
<b>Grouping controls by proximity</b>=<b>按距离合并控制</b>
When several characters share an animation, their controls are merged into one box. The Options toggle <b>Group controls by proximity</b> only merges characters that are physically close in the scene.=多个角色共用一个动画时，它们的控制会合并到一个框中。选项中的<b>按距离合并控制</b>开关只会合并场景中实际靠近的角色。
← Applying animations=← 应用动画
The character section in the top bar shows who an animation will be applied to — the character(s) you have selected in Studio. Props and accessories are ignored.=顶栏的角色区域显示动画将应用到谁，也就是你在工作室中选中的角色。道具和饰品会被忽略。
<b>Priority list (Characters panel)</b>=<b>优先级列表（角色面板）</b>
• Reorder with the arrows; the top entry is filled first.=• 用箭头调整顺序；最上面的条目最先填充。
• The list is remembered between sessions.=• 列表会在会话之间保留。
← Playback controls=← 播放控制
Bundle several related animations into a single tile so the grid is less cluttered and variants are one click apart.=把几个相关动画打包成一个图块，让网格不那么杂乱，切换变体也只需点一下。
1. Tick the <b>checkbox</b> on two or more animation cards.=1. 勾选两张或更多动画卡片上的<b>勾选框</b>。
2. Click <b>Group selected…</b> (panel below the grid).=2. 点击<b>将选中项分组…</b>（位于网格下方的面板）。
3. The <b>review panel</b> opens — confirm the bundle and adjust each animation's role.=3. <b>审阅面板</b>会打开，确认打包内容并调整每个动画的角色。
4. Click <b>Confirm</b>. The animations now share one card with role buttons.=4. 点击<b>确认</b>。这些动画现在共用一张带角色按钮的卡片。
<b>Roles on the card</b>=<b>卡片上的角色</b>
In the review you set each animation's <b>gender</b> (m / f / m2 / f2…) and <b>phase</b> (in / loop / out). Those become the buttons on the finished card. The browser guesses roles from the names; you only fix what it got wrong.=在审阅中设置每个动画的<b>性别</b>（m / f / m2 / f2…）和<b>阶段</b>（in / loop / out），它们会成为完成后卡片上的按钮。浏览器会根据名称猜测角色，你只需修正猜错的部分。
Select a grouped card and click <b>Ungroup</b> to split it back into separate animations. The animations themselves are never deleted.=选中一张分组卡片并点击<b>取消分组</b>，即可将其拆回独立的动画。动画本身永远不会被删除。
Merging reshapes the <b>category tree</b> so related content lives together. Nothing is ever deleted — merges are a display layer you can always undo. Select tree nodes (Ctrl+click for several), then use the buttons under the tree.=合并会重新组织<b>分类树</b>，让相关内容放在一起。不会删除任何内容，合并只是一个显示层，随时可以撤销。选择树节点（按住 Ctrl 点击可多选），然后使用树下方的按钮。
<b>Merge categories</b> (sub-categories of one group)=<b>合并分类</b>（同一分组的子分类）
• Select two or more sub-categories of the <b>same group</b>, then <b>Merge categories…</b>.=• 选择<b>同一分组</b>中的两个或更多子分类，然后点击<b>合并分类…</b>。
• Select an existing merged entry plus another sub-category to <b>extend</b> the merge.=• 选择一个已合并的条目和另一个子分类，即可<b>扩展</b>合并。
• If the button is greyed out, hover it — it explains why (for example the sub-categories live in different top-level groups, which needs a group merge first).=• 如果按钮是灰色的，将鼠标悬停在上面会显示原因（例如子分类位于不同的顶级分组中，需要先合并分组）。
<b>Merge groups</b> (whole top-level groups)=<b>合并分组</b>（整个顶级分组）
• Select two or more <b>groups</b>, then <b>Merge groups…</b>. Sub-categories with the same name are lined up together automatically.=• 选择两个或更多<b>分组</b>，然后点击<b>合并分组…</b>。名称相同的子分类会自动对齐。
• Select a merged group plus another group and use <b>Add to group merge…</b> to add it without starting over.=• 选择一个已合并的分组和另一个分组，使用<b>加入分组合并…</b>即可添加，无需从头开始。
<b>Joining & splitting sub-categories</b> (inside a group merge)=<b>合并与拆分子分类</b>（在分组合并内）
• <b>Join subcategories…</b> — select sub-categories from the merged groups that should be one entry even though their names differ (e.g. "Cowgirl" and "Cow girl").=• <b>合并子分类…</b>：从已合并的分组中选择名称不同但应该合为一个条目的子分类（例如“Cowgirl”和“Cow girl”）。
• <b>Split subcategories</b> — on a joined entry, separates the parts again (they stay inside the merged group).=• <b>拆分子分类</b>：对已合并的条目使用，可重新拆开（它们仍保留在合并后的分组中）。
• Two sub-categories that belong to the <b>same</b> source group are combined as a real category merge instead — same result: one entry.=• 属于<b>同一</b>来源分组的两个子分类会按真正的分类合并处理，结果相同：一个条目。
<b>Undoing merges</b>=<b>撤销合并</b>
• <b>Unmerge</b> — removes a merge and restores the original groups / categories.=• <b>取消合并</b>：移除合并并恢复原来的分组 / 分类。
• <b>Unmerge subcategory</b> — pulls a single sub-category back out of a group merge; the rest stays merged.=• <b>取消合并子分类</b>：把单个子分类从分组合并中移出，其余部分保持合并。
• <b>Dissolve all groups</b> (Options) — resets every merge and group you have made.=• <b>解散所有分组</b>（选项）：重置你创建的所有合并和分组。
Select any node (or a card / animation) and use <b>Rename…</b> to give it a friendlier label. Names are remembered and only change what you see; the original is never lost.=选择任意节点（或卡片/动画）并使用<b>重命名…</b>，即可给它起一个更易懂的名字。名称会被记住，而且只改变显示内容，原名不会丢失。
Every merge opens the <b>review panel</b> first, so you can check the result before it is applied. Cancel discards it with no changes.=每次合并都会先打开<b>审阅面板</b>，你可以在应用前检查结果。点击取消则放弃，不做任何更改。
← Grouping animations=← 动画分组
↑ Overview=↑ 概览
Whenever you group animations or merge categories / groups, a <b>review panel</b> docks to the right so you can check and tweak the result <i>before</i> anything is applied.=每次分组动画或合并分类/分组时，右侧都会停靠一个<b>审阅面板</b>，让你在应用<i>之前</i>检查并调整结果。
<b>Gender / phase buttons</b> — fix each animation's role (m / f / m2…, and in / loop / out). The browser pre-fills its best guess from the names.=<b>性别 / 阶段按钮</b>：修正每个动画的角色（m / f / m2…，以及 in / loop / out）。浏览器会根据名称预先填入最可能的猜测。
<b>Skip</b> — keep one animation at its original category instead of grouping it.=<b>跳过</b>：让某个动画保留在原分类，而不加入分组。
<b>As singles</b> — show these animations individually inside the merged category, without bundling them into a card.=<b>作为单项</b>：在合并后的分类中逐个显示这些动画，而不打包成卡片。
<b>Confirm</b> — apply everything at once.=<b>确认</b>：一次性应用全部更改。
<b>Cancel</b> — discard the whole review; nothing is changed.=<b>取消</b>：放弃整个审阅，不做任何更改。
<b>Empty review?</b>=<b>审阅面板是空的？</b>
An empty review just means no animations could be auto-bundled into cards — the merge / join itself is still valid. Click <b>Confirm</b> and the categories are combined; you simply get no grouped cards.=审阅为空只说明没有动画能被自动打包成卡片，合并/合并子分类本身仍然有效。点击<b>确认</b>后分类会被合并，只是不会生成分组卡片。
<b>Headings (sections)</b>=<b>标题（分区）</b>
Proposed cards are grouped under headings by their original sub-category. Click a heading to collapse it while reviewing a long list.=提议的卡片会按原来的子分类分组在各个标题下。审阅长列表时，点击标题可将其折叠。
← Merging categories & groups=← 合并分类与分组
<b>UI scale</b> — enlarge the whole browser (text, buttons, cards) for 4K / high-DPI screens.=<b>界面缩放</b>：为 4K / 高 DPI 屏幕放大整个浏览器（文字、按钮、卡片）。
<b>Card size</b> — thumbnail width in the grid.=<b>卡片大小</b>：网格中缩略图的宽度。
<b>Hide non-Studio animation lists</b> — hide odd <i>Group 101 / Category 2018</i> entries that come from H-scene-only lists. On by default; turn off to see everything.=<b>隐藏非工作室动画列表</b>：隐藏来自仅限 H 场景列表的奇怪条目，例如 <i>Group 101 / Category 2018</i>。默认开启；关闭后可看到全部内容。
<b>Group controls by proximity</b> — only merge playback controls for characters that are close together in the scene.=<b>按距离合并控制</b>：只合并场景中彼此靠近的角色的播放控制。
<b>Keyboard shortcuts</b> — read-only overview; assign keys in BepInEx Configuration Manager under <b>Anim Browser · Keyboard shortcuts</b>.=<b>键盘快捷键</b>：只读概览；请在 BepInEx Configuration Manager 的 <b>Anim Browser · Keyboard shortcuts</b> 下分配按键。
<b>Dissolve all groups</b> — remove every display group and tree merge you have created (reset).=<b>解散所有分组</b>：移除你创建的所有显示分组和树合并（重置）。
<b>Where settings are stored</b>=<b>设置的保存位置</b>
• <b>anim_browser_options.json</b> — window size/position, view mode, panel widths, card size, UI preferences.=• <b>anim_browser_options.json</b>：窗口大小/位置、视图模式、面板宽度、卡片大小、界面偏好。
• <b>anim_browser_groups.json</b> — your renames, grouped cards, and category / group merges.=• <b>anim_browser_groups.json</b>：你的重命名、分组卡片以及分类/分组合并。
Both are plain files; keep a backup before editing by hand.=两者都是普通文件；手动编辑前请先备份。
← The review panel=← 审阅面板
<b>Clicking an animation does nothing</b> — select a character in Studio first; props / accessories are ignored.=<b>点击动画没有反应</b>：请先在工作室中选择角色；道具/饰品会被忽略。
<b>The list is empty / missing entries</b> — clear the search box; check <b>Hide non-Studio animation lists</b> in Options; press <b>↻</b> to reload the catalog.=<b>列表为空/缺少条目</b>：清空搜索框；检查选项中的<b>隐藏非工作室动画列表</b>；按 <b>↻</b> 重新载入目录。
<b>A merge button is greyed out</b> — hover it for the reason (e.g. cross-group merge needs a group merge first).=<b>合并按钮是灰色的</b>：将鼠标悬停在上面查看原因（例如跨分组合并需要先合并分组）。
<b>The review panel is empty</b> — normal when no cards can be auto-built; click <b>Confirm</b> and the merge still applies.=<b>审阅面板是空的</b>：无法自动生成卡片时属于正常现象；点击<b>确认</b>后合并仍会生效。
<b>I want to start over</b> — <b>Dissolve all groups</b> in Options removes all your merges and grouped cards.=<b>我想从头开始</b>：选项中的<b>解散所有分组</b>会移除你所有的合并和分组卡片。
<b>Wiki pages missing</b> — is HS2Wiki installed? Restart Studio after installing it.=<b>缺少 Wiki 页面</b>：安装了 HS2Wiki 吗？安装后请重启工作室。

Separate the subcategory buckets that were joined here; they stay inside the merged group.=拆开在此合并的子分类，它们仍保留在合并后的分组中。

A quick guide to everything the Anim Browser can do. Keep it open while you explore.=动画浏览器全部功能的快速指南。可以在探索时保持打开。
Close help=关闭帮助
Merge categories…=合并分类…
Join subcategories…=合并子分类…
Merge the selected subcategory buckets inside the group merge into one.=将分组合并中选中的子分类合并为一个。
Combine the selected sub-categories into one node (including already-merged categories).=将选中的子分类合并为一个节点（包括已合并的分类）。
These subcategories live in different top-level groups. Merge the parent groups first, then join their subcategories inside the merged group.=这些子分类位于不同的顶级分组中。请先合并父分组，再在合并后的分组内合并它们的子分类。
Add to group merge…=加入分组合并…
Add the selected group(s) to the already-merged group.=将选中的分组加入已合并的分组。
Merge groups…=合并分组…
Combine the selected groups, matching sub-categories by name.=合并选中的分组，并按名称匹配子分类。
Select two or more raw groups, or one merged group plus the raw groups to add to it.=请选择两个或更多原始分组，或选择一个已合并分组加上要加入它的原始分组。
Capture missing only…=只截取缺失的…
Capture in progress — use the on-screen box.=正在截取，请使用屏幕上的框。
UI=界面

Controls=控制
Preview skeleton: dumps bone names, card paths and joint resolution for offline stick-figure tuning. Includes an embedded-rig test (no scene characters are created or modified).=预览骨架：导出骨骼名称、卡片路径和关节解析结果，用于离线调整火柴人模型。包含内置骨架测试（不会创建或修改场景中的角色）。

Motion=动作

Show controls pane=显示控制面板
Loading animation catalog…=正在载入动画目录…
No animations in catalog.=目录中没有动画。
No animations match the search.=没有符合搜索的动画。
Catalog=目录
Loading animation catalog in the background=正在后台载入动画目录
Building display tree in the background=正在后台构建显示树
Caching animation lists in the background=正在后台缓存动画列表
Keep at original category; do not group this animation.=保留在原分类，不将此动画分组。
Include this animation in the group again.=重新将此动画加入分组。
Skip all=全部跳过
Keep every animation at its original category; do not create this group.=所有动画保留在原分类，不创建此分组。
As singles=作为单项
Do not group these animations, but show them separately inside the merged category.=不对这些动画分组，而是在合并后的分类中单独显示。
Restore group=恢复分组
Include this proposal in the merge again as a grouped card.=重新将此提案作为分组卡片加入合并。
Click section headers to collapse. Skip keeps an animation at its original category. As singles moves animations into the merged category without grouping them. Gender button: slot number → m → f. Phase button: none → in → loop → out.=点击分区标题可折叠。“跳过”会让动画保留在原分类。“作为单项”会把动画移入合并后的分类但不分组。性别按钮：栏位编号 → 男 → 女。阶段按钮：无 → 进入 → 循环 → 退出。
Help ▶=帮助 ▶
Hide the help panel=隐藏帮助面板
Help=帮助
Show a guide to every Anim Browser feature=显示动画浏览器全部功能的指南
What is the Anim Browser?=什么是动画浏览器？
A browser for every Studio animation. Find an animation, click it, and it is applied to the character(s) you have selected in Studio. You can also tidy the list up by renaming, grouping and merging categories so it is easier to navigate.=用于浏览工作室所有动画的浏览器。找到动画并点击，就会应用到你在工作室中选中的角色上。你还可以通过重命名、分组和合并分类来整理列表，让浏览更方便。
• Select one or more characters in the Studio workspace tree first.=• 请先在工作室的工作区树中选择一个或多个角色。
• Pick a sub-category on the left, then click an animation to apply it.=• 在左侧选择一个子分类，然后点击动画即可应用。
• If nothing happens when you click an animation, you probably have no character selected.=• 如果点击动画后没有反应，可能是没有选中角色。
Category tree (left side)=分类树（左侧）
Shows animation groups and their sub-categories. Only sub-categories contain animations.=显示动画分组及其子分类。只有子分类包含动画。
• Click the arrow (► / ▼) to expand or collapse a group.=• 点击箭头（► / ▼）可展开或折叠分组。
• Click a sub-category to show its animations on the right.=• 点击子分类可在右侧显示其中的动画。
• ↻ at the top reloads the catalog if animations were added or changed.=• 如果添加或更改了动画，可点击顶部的 ↻ 重新载入目录。
• Groups are collapsed by default the first time the catalog loads.=• 首次载入目录时，分组默认处于折叠状态。
Grid and list views=网格视图与列表视图
The Grid button (top bar) switches between a thumbnail grid and a compact text list.=顶栏的“网格”按钮可以在缩略图网格和紧凑文本列表之间切换。
• Grid shows a preview image per animation; List is faster to scan when you know the names.=• 网格为每个动画显示预览图；知道名称时，用列表浏览更快。
• Card size in the Options panel changes how large the grid thumbnails are.=• 选项面板中的卡片大小决定网格缩略图的大小。
Type in the search box to filter animations by name. Matching animations are highlighted; sub-categories whose name matches are shown too. Clear the box to see everything again.=在搜索框中输入文字可按名称筛选动画。匹配的动画会高亮显示，名称匹配的子分类也会显示。清空搜索框即可重新显示全部内容。
Applying animations & grouped cards=应用动画与分组卡片
Clicking an animation applies it to every selected character.=点击动画会将其应用到每个选中的角色。
Some cards are 'grouped' and bundle related animations under one tile. Small buttons on the card let you pick which one to apply:=有些卡片是“分组”卡片，把相关动画打包在一个图块中。可以用卡片上的小按钮选择要应用哪一个：
Open the Controls panel (top bar) to adjust the animation currently playing on the selected character(s). It only appears once a character with an animation is selected.=打开顶栏的“控制”面板，可以调整选中角色当前播放的动画。只有选中正在播放动画的角色时才会出现。
• Speed: slider or type a value to slow down / speed up.=• 速度：拖动滑块或输入数值来减速或加速。
• Pause / Play and Time: freeze playback and scrub to a specific moment.=• 暂停 / 播放与时间：冻结播放并拖动到指定时刻。
• Force loop: keep a one-shot animation repeating.=• 强制循环：让单次播放的动画重复播放。
• Restart animation / Restart all in scene: replay from the beginning.=• 重新播放动画 / 场景内全部重新播放：从头开始重放。
• The Controls panel can be docked next to the window or floated freely; the floating panel stays usable even when the main window is closed.=• 控制面板可以停靠在窗口旁边，也可以自由浮动；即使关闭主窗口，浮动面板仍可使用。
The character section in the top bar shows who the animation will be applied to.=顶栏的角色区域显示动画将应用到哪些角色。
Give friendlier names to anything: select a sub-category, group, card or animation and use Rename… (in the tree action bar or the panel below the grid). Names are remembered and only change what you see — the original is never lost.=可以给任何东西起更易懂的名称：选中子分类、分组、卡片或动画，然后使用“重命名…”（位于树操作栏或网格下方的面板）。名称会被记住，而且只改变显示内容，原名不会丢失。
Grouping animations into one card=将动画分组为一张卡片
Select two or more animations (use the checkbox on each card), then 'Group selected…'. A review panel opens where you can confirm the bundle and adjust each animation's role (gender / phase). Use 'Ungroup' to split a card back into separate animations.=选择两个或更多动画（使用每张卡片上的勾选框），然后点击“将选中项分组…”。会打开审阅面板，你可以确认打包内容并调整每个动画的角色（性别 / 阶段）。使用“取消分组”可以把卡片重新拆分为独立的动画。
Merging categories=合并分类
Combine sub-categories that belong together into a single entry.=将属于同一类的子分类合并为一个条目。
• Select two or more sub-categories of the same group, then 'Merge categories…'.=• 选择同一分组中的两个或更多子分类，然后点击“合并分类…”。
• Select a merged entry plus another sub-category to add it to the merge.=• 选择一个已合并的条目和另一个子分类，即可把它加入合并。
• If the button is greyed out, hover it: it explains why (e.g. the sub-categories are in different top-level groups).=• 如果按钮是灰色的，将鼠标悬停在上面会显示原因（例如子分类位于不同的顶级分组中）。
Merging groups=合并分组
Combine whole top-level groups; matching sub-categories (by name) are lined up automatically.=合并整个顶级分组；名称相同的子分类会自动对齐。
• Select two or more groups, then 'Merge groups…'.=• 选择两个或更多分组，然后点击“合并分组…”。
• Select an existing merged group plus another group and use 'Add to group merge…' to extend it without starting over.=• 选择一个已合并的分组和另一个分组，使用“加入分组合并…”即可扩展，无需从头开始。
Joining & splitting sub-categories=合并与拆分子分类
Inside a merged group, sub-categories with different names can be joined into one.=在合并后的分组中，名称不同的子分类也可以合并为一个。
• Select the sub-categories (across the merged groups), then 'Join subcategories…'.=• 选择子分类（可跨合并的分组），然后点击“合并子分类…”。
• 'Split subcategories' on a joined entry separates them again.=• 对已合并的条目使用“拆分子分类”可以重新拆开。
Undoing merges=撤销合并
• 'Unmerge' removes a merge and restores the original groups / categories.=• “取消合并”会移除合并并恢复原来的分组 / 分类。
• 'Unmerge subcategory' pulls a single sub-category back out of a group merge.=• “取消合并子分类”会把单个子分类从分组合并中移出。
Nothing is deleted — unmerging always brings the original catalog entries back.=不会删除任何内容，取消合并后总能恢复原来的目录条目。
Whenever you merge or group, a review panel opens so you can check the result before applying.=每次合并或分组时都会打开审阅面板，你可以在应用前检查结果。
• Adjust each animation's gender and phase with the small buttons.=• 使用小按钮调整每个动画的性别和阶段。
• Skip keeps a single animation at its original place instead of grouping it.=• “跳过”会让单个动画保留在原位置，而不加入分组。
• As singles shows animations individually inside the merged category without bundling them.=• “作为单项”会在合并后的分类中逐个显示动画，而不打包。
• Confirm applies everything at once; Cancel discards it with no changes made.=• “确认”一次性应用全部更改；“取消”则放弃，不做任何改动。
Thumbnails & hover preview=缩略图与悬停预览
• Options → Thumbnails: capture all listed or missing-only; uses the same green-frame overlay as Pose Browser.=• 选项 → 缩略图：截取列表中的全部或仅缺失的缩略图；使用与姿势浏览器相同的绿框叠加层。
• Select cards with checkboxes, then Capture thumbnail(s)… in the content action bar.=• 用勾选框选择卡片，然后在内容操作栏中点击“截取缩略图…”。
• Options → Hover animation preview (HS2): toggle preview and set camera angle, rotation speed, and pitch.=• 选项 → 悬停动画预览（HS2）：开关预览，并设置镜头角度、旋转速度和俯仰。
Options panel=选项面板
• UI scale: enlarge the whole browser for high-resolution screens.=• 界面缩放：为高分辨率屏幕放大整个浏览器。
• Card size: thumbnail size in the grid.=• 卡片大小：网格中缩略图的大小。
• Hover animation preview: live stick-figure in the hovered card thumbnail (HS2 grid; works without scene characters).=• 悬停动画预览：在鼠标悬停的卡片缩略图中实时显示火柴人（HS2 网格；无需场景角色）。
• Thumbnails: capture screen PNGs for the current sub-category or selected cards.=• 缩略图：为当前子分类或选中的卡片截取屏幕 PNG。
• Options → Debug → Dump preview skeleton data: writes embedded rig + joint diagnostics to BepInEx config (share the file for stick-figure tuning).=• 选项 → 调试 → 导出预览骨架数据：将内置骨架和关节诊断信息写入 BepInEx 配置（分享该文件可用于调整火柴人）。
• Hide non-Studio animation lists: hide odd 'Group 101 / Category 2018' entries that come from H-scene-only lists. On by default.=• 隐藏非工作室动画列表：隐藏来自仅限 H 场景列表的奇怪条目，例如“Group 101 / Category 2018”。默认开启。
• Group controls by proximity: only merge playback controls for characters that are close together (3.5 units).=• 按距离合并控制：只合并彼此靠近（3.5 单位以内）的角色的播放控制。
• Keyboard shortcuts: overview of keys (assigned in BepInEx configuration).=• 键盘快捷键：按键概览（在 BepInEx 配置中分配）。
• Dissolve all groups: reset every group and merge you have made.=• 解散所有分组：重置你创建的所有分组与合并。
Window tips=窗口提示
• Drag the title bar to move; drag the ◢ corner to resize.=• 拖动标题栏可移动；拖动 ◢ 角可调整大小。
• − minimises to a small 'AB' chip you can drag and click to restore; × closes the browser.=• − 会最小化为一个小的“AB”标签，可拖动，点击即可恢复；× 关闭浏览器。
• Panels (Controls, Characters, Options, Help, Review) dock to the right and follow the window.=• 各面板（控制、角色、选项、帮助、审阅）停靠在右侧并跟随窗口。
• Japanese animation names are translated automatically when a translator is available.=• 有可用的翻译器时，日文动画名称会自动翻译。

Hover animation preview=悬停动画预览
Show a live stick-figure preview in the hovered card thumbnail (grid view). Uses embedded skeleton data — no scene character required.=在鼠标悬停的卡片缩略图中实时显示火柴人预览（网格视图）。使用内置骨架数据，无需场景角色。
Preview camera angle=预览镜头角度
How the preview camera frames the stick figures. Only one mode is active at a time.=预览镜头如何取景火柴人。同一时间只有一种模式生效。
Rotation speed=旋转速度
How fast the camera orbits the figures in the rotating modes.=旋转模式下镜头环绕人物的速度。
Camera pitch (up/down)=镜头俯仰（上/下）
Iteration pan duration=迭代平移时长
Standard previews=标准预览
Camera orbit for single animations and multi-character (gender) groups.=单个动画和多角色（性别）分组使用的镜头环绕方式。
Iteration groups (1, 2, 3…)=迭代分组（1、2、3…）
Camera orbit used only when previewing numbered-slot groups, where the camera also pans left-right between the figures. Set independently from the standard previews.=仅在预览编号栏位分组时使用的镜头环绕方式，此时镜头还会在人物之间左右平移。与标准预览分开设置。
Full frontal (0°)=正面（0°）
Straight-on front view.=正前方视角。
Front-side (45°)=前侧（45°）
45° front-side view.=45° 前侧视角。
Side (90°)=侧面（90°）
90° side view.=90° 侧面视角。
Rotating=旋转
Continuously orbits the figures.=持续环绕人物。
Orbits continuously but pauses 2 s at 0°, 45° and 90°.=持续环绕，但在 0°、45° 和 90° 处各停顿 2 秒。
Dump preview skeleton data…=导出预览骨架数据…
Filter the tree and grid by animation/category name.=按动画/分类名称筛选树和网格。
Treat search text as a regular expression (case-insensitive).=将搜索文本视为正则表达式（不区分大小写）。
Clear search=清除搜索
Select a subcategory to view animations.=选择一个子分类以查看动画。

Capture a screen thumbnail of each listed animation (applied to the selected scene character) to show on cards when not hovering. Saved under UserData/com.hs2.sandbox/anim_thumbnails. Frame the capture box over the character, then Capture / Auto-capture.=为列表中的每个动画截取屏幕缩略图（应用到选中的场景角色），在未悬停时显示在卡片上。保存在 UserData/com.hs2.sandbox/anim_thumbnails 下。将截取框框住角色，然后点击“截取”/“自动截取”。
• m / f (and m2, f2…): the male / female version, or each participant.=• m / f（以及 m2、f2…）：男性 / 女性版本，或各个参与者。
• in / loop / out: the intro, looping, or outro part of a sequence.=• in / loop / out：序列中的开头、循环或结尾部分。
• 1 / 2 / 3…: numbered variants when there is no gender or phase.=• 1 / 2 / 3…：没有性别或阶段时的编号变体。
Grid cards can show captured PNG thumbnails (UserData/com.hs2.sandbox/anim_thumbnails) or, on HS2, a live stick-figure hover preview while the cursor is on the card.=网格卡片可以显示截取的 PNG 缩略图（UserData/com.hs2.sandbox/anim_thumbnails），在 HS2 中还可以在光标悬停于卡片上时实时显示火柴人预览。
• Preview camera angle / rotation speed / pitch: framing for hover preview (HS2).=• 预览镜头角度 / 旋转速度 / 俯仰：悬停预览的取景（HS2）。
Remove animation groups/categories that are not registered in Studio's category tree (dicAGroupCategory). These often appear as "Group 101" / "Category 2018" and come from H-scene-only lists loaded via dicAnimeLoadInfo. Named abdata Studio animations are kept. On by default.=移除未在工作室分类树（dicAGroupCategory）中注册的动画分组/分类。它们通常显示为“Group 101”/“Category 2018”，来自通过 dicAnimeLoadInfo 载入的仅限 H 场景列表。有名称的 abdata 工作室动画会被保留。默认开启。

Open the full manual in <b>HS2Wiki</b> (<b>F3</b> by default). These buttons open the wiki window on the selected page:=在 <b>HS2Wiki</b> 中打开完整手册（默认按 <b>F3</b>）。这些按钮会在选定页面打开 Wiki 窗口：
<i>Browse, tag, and manage Studio pose files under UserData/studio/pose.</i>=<i>浏览、标记和管理 UserData/studio/pose 下的工作室姿势文件。</i>
<b>Recent releases:</b> <b>Pose stash</b> — temporary FK/IK clipboard with docked or floating window. <b>Pose Browser 5.0.0</b> adds <b>Pose items</b> — register workspace props per pose, load with position/rotation/scale toggles and optional free placement (<b>pose_items.tsv</b> v5). <b>3.2+</b> — group relative positions and object-scale layout. <b>3.0.0</b> — pose groups, tag include/exclude, multi-character apply. <b>2.0.0</b> — Full/List/Mini layouts, Sort, ★ Favorites, keyboard shortcuts. v2+ ZIP import/export — see pages below.=<b>近期版本：</b><b>姿势暂存</b>：临时的 FK/IK 剪贴板，带可停靠或浮动的窗口。<b>Pose Browser 5.0.0</b> 新增<b>姿势物品</b>：为每个姿势登记工作区道具，载入时可开关位置/旋转/缩放，并可选择自由放置（<b>pose_items.tsv</b> v5）。<b>3.2+</b>：分组相对位置和物件缩放布局。<b>3.0.0</b>：姿势分组、标签包含/排除、多角色应用。<b>2.0.0</b>：完整/列表/迷你布局、排序、★ 收藏、键盘快捷键。v2+ ZIP 导入/导出，见下方页面。
