<b>In-window help</b>: open the Pose Browser, then use the <b>Help</b> button for a short on-screen reference.=<b>窗口内帮助</b>：打开姿势浏览器，然后使用<b>帮助</b>按钮查看简短的屏幕参考。
The left pane mirrors folders under <b>UserData/studio/pose</b>. Use <b>↻</b> to rescan the tree after changes outside the browser.=左侧窗格对应 <b>UserData/studio/pose</b> 下的文件夹。在浏览器外做了更改后，用 <b>↻</b> 重新扫描文件夹树。
<b>All poses</b> — recursive listing from the pose root (every supported pose in subfolders).=<b>全部姿势</b>：从姿势根目录递归列出（子文件夹中所有支持的姿势）。
<b>★ Favorites</b> — every favorited pose library-wide (virtual view; <b>Save Pose</b> while this row is active writes into the pose root).=<b>★ 收藏</b>：整个姿势库中所有收藏的姿势（虚拟视图；此行处于活动状态时，<b>保存姿势</b>会写入姿势根目录）。
<b>Root only</b> — files directly in the pose root, no subfolders.=<b>仅根目录</b>：直接位于姿势根目录中的文件，不含子文件夹。
<b>Folder rows</b> — click the name to show only that folder (non-recursive). Use ►/▼ to expand or collapse.=<b>文件夹行</b>：点击名称只显示该文件夹（不递归）。用 ►/▼ 展开或折叠。
<b>Footer actions</b> (under the tree)=<b>底部操作</b>（位于文件夹树下方）
• <b>Library root</b>: <b>New folder…</b>; in <b>Full</b> layout, <b>Export library tree…</b> saves a v2 branch ZIP of the entire library tree.=• <b>姿势库根目录</b>：<b>新建文件夹…</b>；在<b>完整</b>布局中，<b>导出姿势库树…</b>会把整个姿势库树保存为 v2 分支 ZIP。
• <b>Selected folder</b>: <b>Rename…</b>, <b>New folder…</b>, <b>Delete folder…</b> (only if the folder is empty). In <b>Full</b> layout, <b>Export branch…</b> exports that subtree as a v2 ZIP.=• <b>选中的文件夹</b>：<b>重命名…</b>、<b>新建文件夹…</b>、<b>删除文件夹…</b>（仅限空文件夹）。在<b>完整</b>布局中，<b>导出分支…</b>会把该子树导出为 v2 ZIP。
• During <b>Move</b>/<b>Copy</b>/<b>import</b>, <b>Apply</b> / <b>Cancel</b> appear at the <i>top</i> of the footer after you pick a destination.=• 在<b>移动</b>/<b>复制</b>/<b>导入</b>时，选择目标后，<b>应用</b> / <b>取消</b>会出现在底部区域的<i>顶部</i>。
Filters apply to the <i>current folder view</i>, <i>All poses</i>, or <i>★ Favorites</i>, then affect the grid and counts. Use <b>Sort</b> on the top bar for order (Name, file dates, Last used).=筛选作用于<i>当前文件夹视图</i>、<i>全部姿势</i>或<i>★ 收藏</i>，进而影响网格和计数。用顶栏的<b>排序</b>设置顺序（名称、文件日期、最近使用）。
<b>Search</b> — filters by display name / path. Toggle <b>.*</b> for case-insensitive regex; invalid patterns show an error line under the bar.=<b>搜索</b>：按显示名称/路径筛选。切换 <b>.*</b> 可使用不区分大小写的正则表达式；无效的表达式会在搜索栏下方显示错误。
<b>★</b> — show only poses marked favorite (see Tags / Fav Selected).=<b>★</b>：只显示标记为收藏的姿势（见“标签”/“收藏所选”）。
<b>AND / OR</b> — combines active tag filters: every selected tag must match (AND) or any one (OR).=<b>AND / OR</b>：组合当前生效的标签筛选，必须匹配所有选中标签（AND）或任意一个（OR）。
<b>Tags (n)</b> — docked <b>Tag filter</b> window: click each tag to cycle <b>neutral → + include → − exclude</b>. <b>AND / OR</b> is inside the tag window for include rules.=<b>标签 (n)</b>：停靠的<b>标签筛选</b>窗口，点击各个标签可循环切换<b>中立 → + 包含 → − 排除</b>。包含规则的 <b>AND / OR</b> 位于标签窗口中。
<b>Exclude</b> — hides ungrouped poses with excluded tags; grouped segments stay visible with excluded members <b>dimmed</b> (red tag text on cards).=<b>排除</b>：隐藏带有排除标签的未分组姿势；分组区段仍然可见，其中被排除的成员会<b>变暗</b>（卡片上的标签文字为红色）。
← Folders & library=← 文件夹与姿势库
<b>Card</b> — thumbnail, name, optional tag line. Checkbox (corner) toggles selection without affecting the pose.=<b>卡片</b>：缩略图、名称、可选的标签行。角落的勾选框可切换选择而不影响姿势。
<b>Left-click</b> (thumbnail) — single select and <b>apply pose</b> to selected Studio character(s).=<b>左键点击</b>（缩略图）：单选并将<b>姿势应用</b>到工作室中选中的角色。
<b>Ctrl+click</b> — toggle item in the selection.=<b>Ctrl+点击</b>：在选择中切换该项目。
<b>Shift+click</b> — range-select within the filtered list (from last anchor).=<b>Shift+点击</b>：在筛选后的列表中范围选择（从上一个锚点开始）。
<b>Right-click</b> — apply this pose <i>without</i> changing the current selection.=<b>右键点击</b>：应用此姿势，<i>但不</i>改变当前选择。
<b>Pagination</b> — when <i>max items per page</i> is set in Options, use ◀ ▶ and the page label. Column count grows with window width (see Options).=<b>分页</b>：在选项中设置了<i>每页最大项目数</i>时，使用 ◀ ▶ 和页码标签。列数会随窗口宽度增加（见选项）。
<b>Import preview</b> — after <b>Import…</b>, cards from the ZIP appear in the grid; thumbnail click toggles whether a pose is checked (checkbox + Ctrl/Shift behave like normal). You are not browsing disk files until you cancel or finish the import.=<b>导入预览</b>：点击<b>导入…</b>后，ZIP 中的卡片会出现在网格中；点击缩略图可切换是否勾选该姿势（勾选框和 Ctrl/Shift 的行为与平常相同）。在取消或完成导入之前，你浏览的都不是磁盘上的文件。
<b>Pose groups</b> — members of a group appear inside a bordered segment with a header row (▦ name, optional group tags). See <b>Pose groups</b> for selection vs group entity, filters, and export.=<b>姿势分组</b>：分组成员显示在带边框的区段中，带有标题行（▦ 名称、可选的分组标签）。关于选择与分组实体、筛选和导出，请参阅<b>姿势分组</b>。
← Search & filters=← 搜索与筛选
<b>Import…</b> is always on the top bar. The <b>selection bar</b> actions below need at least one selected <i>library</i> pose (import preview uses its own bottom-bar hints).=<b>导入…</b>始终位于顶栏。下方<b>选择栏</b>中的操作至少需要选中一个<i>姿势库</i>中的姿势（导入预览使用自己的底栏提示）。
<b>Import…</b> (top bar) — read a v2 pose pack <b>.zip</b>; see <b>Import & export (ZIP)</b> for the workflow.=<b>导入…</b>（顶栏）：读取 v2 姿势包 <b>.zip</b>；流程见<b>导入与导出（ZIP）</b>。
<b>Save Pose</b> (top bar) — writes the current character pose into the <i>active save folder</i>: selected folder, pose root when <b>All poses</b> or <b>★ Favorites</b> is active.=<b>保存姿势</b>（顶栏）：将当前角色的姿势写入<i>当前保存文件夹</i>，即选中的文件夹；当<b>全部姿势</b>或<b>★ 收藏</b>处于活动状态时则为姿势根目录。
<b>Update Pose</b> (one selected) — overwrite file from the scene; choose keeping or regenerating the thumbnail.=<b>更新姿势</b>（选中一个时）：用场景中的姿势覆盖文件；可选择保留或重新生成缩略图。
<b>Rename…</b> — optional rename of file to match display name.=<b>重命名…</b>：可选择同时重命名文件，使其与显示名称一致。
<b>Grouping</b> — <b>Group…</b> (2+ ungrouped poses), <b>Ungroup</b>. Group header selection shows a separate bar: rename, tags, export, apply, save/clear positions. See <b>Pose groups</b>.=<b>分组</b>：<b>分组…</b>（两个或更多未分组的姿势）、<b>取消分组</b>。选中分组标题时会显示另一个操作栏：重命名、标签、导出、应用、保存/清除位置。见<b>姿势分组</b>。
<b>Items</b> (exactly one pose selected) — register Studio workspace items for that pose; see <b>Pose items</b>.=<b>物品</b>（恰好选中一个姿势时）：为该姿势登记工作室工作区物品；见<b>姿势物品</b>。
<b>Tag Selected</b> — tag window in <b>assign</b> mode for <i>pose</i> tags on all selected items.=<b>为选中项添加标签</b>：以<b>分配</b>模式打开标签窗口，为所有选中项目设置<i>姿势</i>标签。
<b>Fav Selected</b> — toggle favorite flag (★ filter).=<b>收藏所选</b>：切换收藏标记（★ 筛选）。
<b>Export…</b> — writes selected on-disk poses to a v2 <b>.zip</b> (embedded tags/favorites metadata).=<b>导出…</b>：将选中的磁盘姿势写入 v2 <b>.zip</b>（内嵌标签/收藏元数据）。
<b>Move… / Copy…</b> — pick destination in the <b>Folders</b> tree (<b>Root only</b> or a folder; highlighted), then <b>Apply</b> or <b>Cancel</b> in the folder footer. The grid does not reload while picking, so your selection is preserved. <b>New folder…</b> still works. Tags move with paths when applicable.=<b>移动… / 复制…</b>：在<b>文件夹</b>树中选择目标（<b>仅根目录</b>或某个文件夹，会高亮显示），然后在文件夹底部区域点击<b>应用</b>或<b>取消</b>。选择期间网格不会重新载入，因此你的选择会被保留。<b>新建文件夹…</b>仍然可用。适用时标签会随路径一起移动。
<b>Delete…</b> — copies into <b>!_AutoBackup</b> then removes files; confirmations are required.=<b>删除…</b>：先复制到 <b>!_AutoBackup</b> 再删除文件；需要确认。
<b>Character</b> row — <b>Chars</b> opens priority lists; with multiple poses or one full group, <b>Apply to characters…</b> maps poses onto Studio-selected characters. See <b>Multi-character apply</b>.=<b>角色</b>行：<b>角色</b>按钮打开优先级列表；选中多个姿势或一个完整分组时，<b>应用到角色…</b>会把姿势分配到工作室中选中的角色。见<b>多角色应用</b>。
← Grid & selection=← 网格与选择
Link Studio <b>workspace items</b> (props, accessories, etc.) to a <b>single library pose</b>. When you apply that pose and load its items, layout is restored relative to one character — including optional parenting to a body-part row in the workspace tree.=将工作室<b>工作区物品</b>（道具、饰品等）关联到<b>单个姿势库姿势</b>。应用该姿势并载入其物品时，布局会相对于一个角色恢复，包括可选地将其设为工作区树中某个身体部位行的子级。
<b>Open the pane</b>=<b>打开窗格</b>
1. Select <b>exactly one</b> pose in the library grid (not import preview).=1. 在姿势库网格中<b>恰好选择一个</b>姿势（不能是导入预览）。
2. Click <b>Items</b> in the bottom selection bar.=2. 点击底部选择栏中的<b>物品</b>。
3. The <b>Items</b> pane docks beside Help / Options / Tags (same chain as other side panels).=3. <b>物品</b>窗格会停靠在帮助 / 选项 / 标签旁边（与其他侧面板相同的排列）。
<b>Add items to a pose</b>=<b>为姿势添加物品</b>
• Select <b>one character</b> in Studio (extra selected objects are ignored for add/load).=• 在工作室中选择<b>一个角色</b>（添加/载入时会忽略额外选中的对象）。
• Select one or more <b>workspace items</b> (tree row and/or item guide).=• 选择一个或多个<b>工作区物品</b>（树中的行和/或物品辅助物件）。
• The line above <b>Add selected item(s)</b> lists names that will be registered (<b>Will add: …</b>).=• <b>添加选中的物品</b>上方的一行会列出将要登记的名称（<b>将添加：…</b>）。
• Each entry stores catalog ids, bundle paths (for respawn after restart), transform layout, optional body-part attach path, and Studio attach offsets when parented.=• 每个条目会保存目录 ID、资源包路径（用于重启后重新生成）、变换布局、可选的身体部位附着路径，以及设置父级时的工作室附着偏移。
<b>Stored list (per pose)</b>=<b>已保存列表（每个姿势）</b>
<b>☑</b> — include row in <b>Load Selection</b> (unchecked rows are skipped).=<b>☑</b>：将该行包含在<b>载入选中项</b>中（未勾选的行会被跳过）。
<b>Name button</b> — load that one entry immediately.=<b>名称按钮</b>：立即载入该条目。
<b>✎</b> — rename the display label (saved in <b>pose_items.tsv</b>).=<b>✎</b>：重命名显示标签（保存在 <b>pose_items.tsv</b> 中）。
<b>X</b> — remove the entry from this pose.=<b>X</b>：从此姿势中移除该条目。
<b>Bold name</b> — same catalog item is currently selected in Studio (still a button).=<b>粗体名称</b>：相同的目录物品当前在工作室中处于选中状态（仍然是按钮）。
<b>Load options</b>=<b>载入选项</b>
<b>Position</b> / <b>Rotation</b> / <b>Scale</b> — turn off to keep the spawned item’s default on that axis.=<b>位置</b> / <b>旋转</b> / <b>缩放</b>：关闭后，生成的物品在该方面保持默认值。
<b>Load as free</b> — do not reparent in the workspace tree even when the item was saved on a body part; world layout still follows the character (scaled with current object scale / body height).=<b>自由载入</b>：即使物品保存时附着在身体部位上，也不在工作区树中重新设置父级；世界布局仍跟随角色（按当前物件缩放/身高缩放）。
<b>Load Selection</b> — checked rows only. <b>Load All</b> — every stored row.=<b>载入选中项</b>：只载入勾选的行。<b>全部载入</b>：载入所有已保存的行。
<b>Layout & scaling</b>=<b>布局与缩放</b>
<b>Data file</b>=<b>数据文件</b>
<b>pose_items.tsv</b> (v5) under <b>BepInEx/config/com.hs2.sandbox/</b>, keyed by pose path relative to the library. Move/rename pose files update keys through the browser like tags.=<b>BepInEx/config/com.hs2.sandbox/</b> 下的 <b>pose_items.tsv</b>（v5），以相对于姿势库的姿势路径为键。通过浏览器移动/重命名姿势文件时，键会像标签一样更新。
← Pose files & actions=← 姿势文件与操作
A <b>pose group</b> is a named set of library poses that stay together in the grid, share optional <b>group tags</b>, and can be exported/imported inside v5 ZIP packs (v2–v4 packs still import; layout fields optional). Membership, optional <b>relative offsets</b>, and <b>body heights per pose</b> are stored in <b>pose_groups.tsv</b> (config folder), keyed by pose file paths.=<b>姿势分组</b>是一组命名的姿势库姿势，它们在网格中保持在一起，可共享<b>分组标签</b>，并可在 v5 ZIP 包中导出/导入（v2–v4 包仍可导入；布局字段可选）。成员关系、可选的<b>相对偏移</b>和<b>每个姿势的身高</b>保存在 <b>pose_groups.tsv</b>（配置文件夹）中，以姿势文件路径为键。
<b>Creating and editing groups</b>=<b>创建和编辑分组</b>
1. Select <b>two or more ungrouped</b> poses in the bottom bar (checkboxes or thumbnail selection).=1. 在底栏中选择<b>两个或更多未分组的</b>姿势（勾选框或点击缩略图选择）。
2. Click <b>Group…</b>, enter a name, confirm.=2. 点击<b>分组…</b>，输入名称并确认。
3. To add poses later, select ungrouped poses + at least one member of the target group, then <b>Group…</b> again (merges into the existing group when applicable).=3. 之后要添加姿势时，选择未分组的姿势和目标分组中至少一个成员，然后再次点击<b>分组…</b>（适用时会合并到现有分组）。
4. <b>Ungroup</b> removes selected poses from their groups (does not delete pose files).=4. <b>取消分组</b>会将选中的姿势移出其分组（不会删除姿势文件）。
5. <b>Rename…</b> / <b>Tags…</b> / <b>Export…</b> appear on the <b>group action bar</b> when the group header is selected as a group entity.=5. 将分组标题作为分组实体选中时，<b>分组操作栏</b>上会出现<b>重命名…</b> / <b>标签…</b> / <b>导出…</b>。
<b>Relative positions (save & apply)</b>=<b>相对位置（保存与应用）</b>
Optional layout when a group is applied multi-character. Everything is keyed by <b>pose path</b> and <b>assignment order</b> from <b>Chars</b> priority (grid display order) — not by which Studio character card you used at save time.=分组以多角色方式应用时可选的布局。所有内容都以<b>姿势路径</b>和来自<b>角色</b>优先级的<b>分配顺序</b>（网格显示顺序）为键，而不是以保存时使用的工作室角色为键。
<b>Anchor (first pose)</b>=<b>锚点（第一个姿势）</b>
• The <b>first pose</b> in display order is the anchor.=• 显示顺序中的<b>第一个姿势</b>是锚点。
• At save: anchor world position + body height are recorded on that pose path.=• 保存时：在该姿势路径上记录锚点的世界位置和身高。
• At apply: anchor character is <b>not moved</b> by layout; other characters use <b>anchor position + anchor rotation × saved local offset</b> (orbit with anchor).=• 应用时：锚点角色<b>不会</b>因布局而移动；其他角色使用<b>锚点位置 + 锚点旋转 × 保存的局部偏移</b>（随锚点环绕）。
<b>Requirements to enable Save positions…</b>=<b>启用“保存位置…”的条件</b>
• Not during import preview.=• 不在导入预览期间。
• The group was the <b>last</b> thing you applied with <b>Apply to characters…</b> — no other pose applied since.=• 该分组是你用<b>应用到角色…</b>应用的<b>最后一个</b>内容，此后没有应用过其他姿势。
• Studio selection: <b>exactly as many characters as poses</b> in the group.=• 工作室中选择的角色数：<b>与分组中的姿势数完全相同</b>。
• <b>One-to-one gender match</b> via <b>Chars</b> lists and pose tags (see <b>Multi-character apply</b>).=• 通过<b>角色</b>列表和姿势标签实现<b>一对一的性别匹配</b>（见<b>多角色应用</b>）。
<b>Workflow — save</b>=<b>流程：保存</b>
1. Set up <b>Chars</b> and pose tags if needed.=1. 如有需要，先设置好<b>角色</b>列表和姿势标签。
2. Select characters in Studio → apply group → arrange scene.=2. 在工作室中选择角色 → 应用分组 → 布置场景。
3. <b>Save positions…</b> stores per other pose: local <b>offset</b> and <b>rotation</b> vs anchor, plus <b>body height</b> on every pose path.=3. <b>保存位置…</b>会为每个非锚点姿势保存相对锚点的局部<b>偏移</b>和<b>旋转</b>，并在每个姿势路径上保存<b>身高</b>。
<b>Workflow — apply</b>=<b>流程：应用</b>
Same apply path and assignment order. After poses are applied:=使用相同的应用路径和分配顺序。应用姿势后：
• <b>Apply relative positions</b> (global) — <b>anchor + rotated offset</b> (orbits with anchor) and relative rotation on each non-anchor pose.=• <b>套用相对位置</b>（全局）：<b>锚点 + 旋转后的偏移</b>（随锚点环绕），以及每个非锚点姿势的相对旋转。
• <b>Adjust for body height</b> (global; requires relative positions) — same full offset, but <b>offset.y</b> is scaled from saved vs current body-height ratios on each pose path (no hardcoded world multiplier).=• <b>根据身高调整</b>（全局；需要相对位置）：偏移完整保留，但 <b>offset.y</b> 会根据每个姿势路径上保存时与当前的身高比例进行缩放（不使用写死的世界倍率）。
• <b>Clear positions</b> — removes offsets and heights for that group.=• <b>清除位置</b>：移除该分组的偏移和身高。
<b>Group thumbnails</b>=<b>分组缩略图</b>
Capture preview images for <b>every pose in a group</b> while characters stay posed together in the scene.=在角色于场景中保持组合姿势的同时，为<b>分组中的每个姿势</b>截取预览图。
<b>When Group thumbnails… is enabled</b>=<b>“分组缩略图…”何时可用</b>
• Select the <b>group header</b> (group entity, not individual member checkboxes).=• 选中<b>分组标题</b>（分组实体，而不是单个成员的勾选框）。
• Select <b>exactly as many characters in Studio as poses</b> in the group.=• 在工作室中选择的<b>角色数与分组中的姿势数完全相同</b>。
• Characters must match pose genders and <b>Chars</b> priority (same one-to-one rules as multi-character apply).=• 角色必须符合姿势性别和<b>角色</b>优先级（与多角色应用相同的一对一规则）。
• Not during import preview; not while another capture overlay is open.=• 不在导入预览期间；没有打开其他截取叠加层时。
<b>What happens</b>=<b>执行过程</b>
1. All group poses are applied at once (including relative layout when <b>Apply relative positions</b> / height / object-scale toggles are on — same as <b>Apply to characters…</b>).=1. 一次性应用分组中的所有姿势（开启<b>套用相对位置</b> / 身高 / 物件缩放开关时也包括相对布局，与<b>应用到角色…</b>相同）。
2. The capture overlay opens; frame the shot with the green square (drag / resize).=2. 打开截取叠加层；用绿色方框取景（拖动/调整大小）。
3. For each pose in display order, characters on <b>other</b> poses use Studio <b>simple color</b> (monocolor) so the thumbnail highlights one character.=3. 按显示顺序处理每个姿势时，<b>其他</b>姿势上的角色会使用工作室的<b>单色</b>显示，让缩略图突出一个角色。
4. <b>Capture</b> writes that pose's PNG; <b>Skip</b> skips it; <b>Auto-capture</b> finishes the rest automatically; <b>Cancel</b> exits and restores normal rendering.=4. <b>截取</b>会写入该姿势的 PNG；<b>跳过</b>会跳过它；<b>自动截取</b>会自动完成剩余部分；<b>取消</b>会退出并恢复正常渲染。
Example: two-pose group, characters A and B — A gets pose 1, B gets pose 2. While capturing pose 1, B is monocolor; while capturing pose 2, A is monocolor.=示例：两个姿势的分组，角色 A 和 B，A 分配姿势 1，B 分配姿势 2。截取姿势 1 时 B 为单色；截取姿势 2 时 A 为单色。
<b>Two kinds of selection</b>=<b>两种选择方式</b>
<b>Group entity</b> — click the <b>group header</b> (▦ row). Highlights the segment; shows the group bar (rename, tags, export, apply, <b>Group thumbnails…</b>, save/clear positions, <b>Apply relative positions</b>, <b>Adjust for body height</b>). Ctrl+click toggles group entities; Shift+click range-selects group headers in the filtered list.=<b>分组实体</b>：点击<b>分组标题</b>（▦ 行）。会高亮该区段并显示分组操作栏（重命名、标签、导出、应用、<b>分组缩略图…</b>、保存/清除位置、<b>套用相对位置</b>、<b>根据身高调整</b>）。Ctrl+点击可切换分组实体；Shift+点击可在筛选后的列表中范围选择分组标题。
<b>Pose members</b> — checkboxes / thumbnail clicks on cards inside the segment. Used for move, copy, delete, tag selected, partial export, etc.=<b>姿势成员</b>：区段内卡片上的勾选框/缩略图点击。用于移动、复制、删除、为选中项添加标签、部分导出等。
During <b>import preview</b>, clicking the group header toggles <b>all member checkboxes</b> for import (not group-entity mode).=在<b>导入预览</b>期间，点击分组标题会切换导入时的<b>所有成员勾选框</b>（而不是分组实体模式）。
<b>Group tags vs pose tags</b>=<b>分组标签与姿势标签</b>
<b>Grid layout</b>=<b>网格布局</b>
Grouped poses render in a bordered block. Large groups may continue on the next row (continuation header without repeating tags). Sort order applies to <b>groups as blocks</b> and to ungrouped poses.=分组的姿势会显示在带边框的区块中。较大的分组可能延续到下一行（延续标题不会重复标签）。排序会把<b>分组作为整块</b>参与排序，也作用于未分组的姿势。
<b>Move / copy / delete</b>=<b>移动 / 复制 / 删除</b>
<b>Import / export (ZIP v5)</b>=<b>导入 / 导出（ZIP v5）</b>
v3+ packs include <b>groups[]</b> in <b>metadata.json</b>; v4 adds <b>memberRelativeOffsets</b>; v5 adds <b>memberBodyHeights</b> (parallel to members). On import preview, groups appear as segments; layout imports when present. v2 packs without groups still import as flat poses.=v3 及以上的包在 <b>metadata.json</b> 中包含 <b>groups[]</b>；v4 新增 <b>memberRelativeOffsets</b>；v5 新增 <b>memberBodyHeights</b>（与成员一一对应）。导入预览时分组显示为区段；有布局数据时会一并导入。不含分组的 v2 包仍会作为平铺姿势导入。
The <b>pose stash</b> is a temporary clipboard for character FK/IK poses. It does <b>not</b> write to <b>UserData/studio/pose</b> and is separate from <b>History</b> (automatic undo snapshots per character).=<b>姿势暂存</b>是存放角色 FK/IK 姿势的临时剪贴板。它<b>不会</b>写入 <b>UserData/studio/pose</b>，也与<b>历史</b>（每个角色的自动撤销快照）相互独立。
<b>Opening and closing</b>=<b>打开和关闭</b>
<b>Stash</b> (top bar in <b>Full</b> layout; character row in <b>List</b> / <b>Mini</b>) — toggles the stash UI. If open (docked or floating), closes it. If closed, opens in the last mode you used.=<b>暂存</b>（<b>完整</b>布局中位于顶栏；<b>列表</b> / <b>迷你</b>布局中位于角色行）：开关暂存界面。若已打开（停靠或浮动）则关闭；若已关闭则以上次使用的模式打开。
<b>Docked</b> — side pane beside the browser (like <b>History</b>). Closes when the main Pose Browser window closes.=<b>停靠</b>：浏览器旁的侧面板（与<b>历史</b>类似）。姿势浏览器主窗口关闭时一并关闭。
<b>Floating</b> — use <b>Float</b> in the docked pane, or the hotkey <b>Toggle undocked pose stash</b>. Independent window: drag the title bar, resize with ◢. Stays open when the main browser is closed.=<b>浮动</b>：在停靠面板中点击<b>浮动</b>，或使用快捷键<b>切换独立姿势暂存面板</b>。独立窗口：拖动标题栏移动，用 ◢ 调整大小。主浏览器关闭后仍保持打开。
<b>Dock</b> on the floating window re-attaches the pane (closes the float if the browser is hidden). <b>×</b> closes only the floating stash.=浮动窗口上的<b>停靠</b>会重新附着面板（若浏览器已隐藏则关闭浮动窗口）。<b>×</b> 只关闭浮动的暂存窗口。
<b>Capture and apply</b>=<b>暂存与应用</b>
<b>Stash selected character</b> — requires <b>exactly one</b> character selected in Studio. Stores FK/IK pose data only (not world position/rotation).=<b>暂存选中的角色</b>：需要在工作室中<b>恰好选中一个</b>角色。只保存 FK/IK 姿势数据（不包括世界位置/旋转）。
Each entry shows <b>character name</b> and <b>timestamp</b> (newest first).=每个条目显示<b>角色名称</b>和<b>时间戳</b>（最新的在前）。
<b>Click an entry</b> — applies that pose to <b>every</b> character currently selected in Studio (one or many).=<b>点击条目</b>：将该姿势应用到工作室中当前选中的<b>每一个</b>角色（一个或多个）。
<b>Auto-delete after apply</b> — optional toggle; removes the entry after a successful apply.=<b>应用后自动删除</b>：可选开关；应用成功后移除该条目。
<b>x</b> on a row — confirm with <b>Yes</b> / <b>No</b>.=行上的 <b>x</b>：用<b>是</b> / <b>否</b>确认。
<b>Clear entire stash</b> — bottom of the window; confirm required.=<b>清空全部暂存</b>：位于窗口底部；需要确认。
<b>Keyboard shortcut</b>=<b>键盘快捷键</b>
Assign <b>Toggle undocked pose stash</b> in Configuration Manager → <b>Pose Browser · Keyboard shortcuts</b>. Opens or closes the <b>floating</b> stash only (works while Pose Browser is loaded, even if the main window is closed).=在 Configuration Manager → <b>Pose Browser · Keyboard shortcuts</b> 中分配<b>切换独立姿势暂存面板</b>。只打开或关闭<b>浮动</b>暂存窗口（只要姿势浏览器已载入就有效，即使主窗口已关闭）。
<b>Data files</b>=<b>数据文件</b>
← Multi-character apply=← 多角色应用
Use this workflow when a scene has <b>several characters</b> and you want to apply <b>multiple poses at once</b> (or an entire <b>group</b>) with predictable pairing — for example a male pose on the male character and a female pose on the female character.=当场景中有<b>多个角色</b>，并且你想以可预测的配对方式<b>一次应用多个姿势</b>（或整个<b>分组</b>）时使用此流程，例如把男性姿势应用到男性角色、女性姿势应用到女性角色。
<b>When the button appears</b>=<b>按钮何时出现</b>
<b>Apply to characters…</b> shows on the character row or group bar when:=在以下情况下，<b>应用到角色…</b>会显示在角色行或分组操作栏上：
• <b>Two or more</b> library poses are selected (checkboxes), or=• 选中了<b>两个或更多</b>姿势库姿势（勾选框），或
• Exactly <b>one group entity</b> is selected (group header) — all members in <b>display order</b> are used.=• 恰好选中了<b>一个分组实体</b>（分组标题），此时按<b>显示顺序</b>使用所有成员。
Not available during ZIP import preview. Requires at least one character selected in Studio.=ZIP 导入预览期间不可用。需要在工作室中至少选中一个角色。
<b>Setup — Chars window</b>=<b>设置：角色窗口</b>
1. Open <b>Chars</b> (docked pane, like Tags / Sort).=1. 打开<b>角色</b>（停靠面板，与标签 / 排序类似）。
2. <b>Load characters from scene</b> appends new characters (gender from scene). <b>Remove missing from scene</b> deletes orange (not found) slots.=2. <b>从场景载入角色</b>会追加新角色（性别取自场景）。<b>移除场景中缺失的角色</b>会删除橙色（未找到）的栏位。
5. The list is saved to <b>pose_browser_character_config.json</b> in the Sandbox config folder.=5. 列表会保存到 Sandbox 配置文件夹中的 <b>pose_browser_character_config.json</b>。
<b>Studio selection</b>=<b>工作室选择</b>
Select the characters you want to pose in Studio (workspace / gizmo). The character row shows count and names. Only <b>characters</b> count — props and accessories are ignored. Multi-apply uses <b>intersection</b> of Studio selection and your priority lists (plus unlisted selected characters at the end for untagged poses).=在工作室中（工作区/辅助物件）选择要摆姿势的角色。角色行会显示数量和名称。只有<b>角色</b>才算数，道具和饰品会被忽略。多角色应用使用工作室选择与优先级列表的<b>交集</b>（对于无标签的姿势，还会把未列出的已选角色排在最后）。
<b>How poses are assigned</b>=<b>姿势如何分配</b>
Processing follows the <b>pose list order</b> (grid display order for groups). Each character receives <b>at most one pose per apply</b> — later poses never overwrite an earlier assignment on the same character.=按<b>姿势列表顺序</b>处理（分组则按网格显示顺序）。每次应用时每个角色<b>最多得到一个姿势</b>，后面的姿势绝不会覆盖同一角色之前的分配。
<b>Male / Female pose tags</b> (case-insensitive, on the pose only)=<b>Male / Female 姿势标签</b>（不区分大小写，只看姿势上的标签）
• Pose tagged <b>Male</b> (not Female) → next available selected character marked <b>m</b> in the priority list.=• 标记为 <b>Male</b>（且不是 Female）的姿势 → 优先级列表中下一个可用的、标记为 <b>m</b> 的已选角色。
• Pose tagged <b>Female</b> → next available selected character marked <b>f</b> in list order.=• 标记为 <b>Female</b> 的姿势 → 按列表顺序下一个可用的、标记为 <b>f</b> 的已选角色。
• Characters not in the list (or wrong gender for a tagged pose) are skipped for that pose.=• 不在列表中的角色（或性别与带标签姿势不符的角色）会在该姿势中被跳过。
• If both Male and Female tags are present, or neither tag is present, the pose is treated as <b>untagged</b> (see below).=• 如果同时有 Male 和 Female 标签，或两者都没有，该姿势按<b>无标签</b>处理（见下文）。
<b>Untagged poses</b>=<b>无标签的姿势</b>
• Build priority order: top-to-bottom in <b>Chars</b>, then any selected characters not in the list.=• 确定优先顺序：先按<b>角色</b>列表从上到下，然后是不在列表中的已选角色。
• First pass: pose 1 → first free slot, pose 2 → second free slot, etc. Extra poses with no free character are <b>skipped</b>.=• 第一轮：姿势 1 → 第一个空闲栏位，姿势 2 → 第二个空闲栏位，以此类推。没有空闲角色的多余姿势会被<b>跳过</b>。
• Second pass: any selected character still without a pose gets a pose by cycling through the pose list (only if eligible for that pose’s gender tag).=• 第二轮：仍没有姿势的已选角色会循环取用姿势列表中的姿势（仅当符合该姿势的性别标签时）。
<b>Example workflows</b>=<b>流程示例</b>
<b>Couple (M + F tags)</b> — Group with two poses tagged Male and Female. Select both characters in Studio. Select the group header → <b>Apply to characters…</b>. Optionally save positions after arranging spacing, then re-apply the group later with layout restored.=<b>情侣（M + F 标签）</b>：分组中有两个分别标记为 Male 和 Female 的姿势。在工作室中选中两个角色。选中分组标题 → <b>应用到角色…</b>。调整好间距后可以保存位置，以后再次应用该分组时会恢复布局。
<b>Five generic poses, three characters</b> — Untagged poses; only the top three priority characters are posed; poses 4–5 skipped.=<b>五个通用姿势，三个角色</b>：无标签的姿势；只有优先级最高的三个角色会摆姿势；姿势 4–5 被跳过。
<b>Two poses, four characters</b> — After the first pass, two characters remain; second pass applies poses 1 and 2 again to them (different characters, not overwrites).=<b>两个姿势，四个角色</b>：第一轮后还剩两个角色；第二轮会把姿势 1 和 2 再应用到他们身上（是不同的角色，不会覆盖）。
<b>Single-pose apply</b> (left-click thumbnail) still applies one pose to <b>all</b> selected Studio characters at once — separate from multi-character mapping.=<b>单个姿势应用</b>（左键点击缩略图）仍会把一个姿势同时应用到工作室中选中的<b>所有</b>角色，与多角色分配是分开的。
← Pose groups=← 姿势分组
Pose Browser exchanges packs as <b>.zip</b> archives with <b>manifest.json</b>, <b>metadata.json</b>, and pose binaries under <b>poses/</b>. <b>v3</b> adds optional <b>groups[]</b> in metadata (names, group tags, member paths). The reader only accepts <b>stored</b> (uncompressed) ZIP entries—recompressing with Deflate breaks import.=姿势浏览器以 <b>.zip</b> 压缩包交换姿势包，其中包含 <b>manifest.json</b>、<b>metadata.json</b> 以及 <b>poses/</b> 下的姿势二进制文件。<b>v3</b> 在元数据中新增了可选的 <b>groups[]</b>（名称、分组标签、成员路径）。读取器只接受<b>仅存储</b>（未压缩）的 ZIP 条目，用 Deflate 重新压缩会导致导入失败。
1. Choose a pack file. A <b>preview grid</b> replaces the normal listing.=1. 选择一个姿势包文件。<b>预览网格</b>会取代普通列表。
2. Check poses to import (thumbnail toggles; or checkbox with Ctrl/Shift range). <b>Cancel import</b> in the bottom bar abandons the operation.=2. 勾选要导入的姿势（点击缩略图切换，或用勾选框配合 Ctrl/Shift 范围选择）。底栏中的<b>取消导入</b>会放弃此操作。
3. Pick a destination: click <b>Root only</b> or a folder name in <b>Folders</b> so the footer shows <b>Apply</b>/<b>Cancel</b> for the import.=3. 选择目标：在<b>文件夹</b>中点击<b>仅根目录</b>或某个文件夹名称，底部区域会显示导入用的<b>应用</b>/<b>取消</b>。
4. <b>Apply</b> writes files. <b>Tree branch</b> packs create one new subfolder (named in the manifest) under the folder you picked.=4. <b>应用</b>会写入文件。<b>树分支</b>包会在你选择的文件夹下新建一个子文件夹（名称由清单指定）。
<b>Export…</b> (selection bar)=<b>导出…</b>（选择栏）
Select poses that already live in your library, then export a flat v5 ZIP with tags/favorites and pose groups (when fully selected; offsets and body heights when saved) in metadata.=选择已在姿势库中的姿势，然后导出平铺的 v5 ZIP，元数据中包含标签/收藏和姿势分组（完整选中时；已保存时还包括偏移和身高）。
<b>Export branch…</b> / <b>Export library tree…</b>=<b>导出分支…</b> / <b>导出姿势库树…</b>
In <b>Full</b> layout, the folder footer offers <b>Export branch…</b> when a folder is selected, and <b>Export library tree…</b> at library root—hierarchical branch packs for sharing whole subtrees.=在<b>完整</b>布局中，选中文件夹时底部区域会提供<b>导出分支…</b>，在姿势库根目录时提供<b>导出姿势库树…</b>，用于以分层分支包分享整个子树。
Modders: full field reference and JSON examples — <b>Modules/PoseBrowser/POSE_ZIP_FORMAT.md</b> in the HS2-Sandbox repository.=模组作者：完整字段参考和 JSON 示例见 HS2-Sandbox 仓库中的 <b>Modules/PoseBrowser/POSE_ZIP_FORMAT.md</b>。
<b>Single-pose capture — Thumbnails…</b>=<b>单个姿势截取：缩略图…</b>
Select one or more library poses (member checkboxes), then click <b>Thumbnails…</b> in the bottom selection bar.=选择一个或多个姿势库姿势（成员勾选框），然后点击底部选择栏中的<b>缩略图…</b>。
• Each pose in the queue is applied to Studio-selected character(s) before its shot.=• 队列中的每个姿势在截取前都会应用到工作室中选中的角色。
• The overlay shows a draggable/resizable green frame over the scene.=• 叠加层会在场景上显示一个可拖动/调整大小的绿色框。
• <b>Capture</b> — save PNG into the pose file and advance.=• <b>截取</b>：将 PNG 保存到姿势文件中并继续下一个。
• <b>Skip</b> — advance without writing a file.=• <b>跳过</b>：不写入文件直接继续。
• <b>Auto-capture</b> — capture this pose, then all remaining poses automatically (pause: Options / BepInEx <b>Auto capture delay</b>).=• <b>自动截取</b>：截取此姿势后自动截取所有剩余姿势（间隔：选项 / BepInEx 中的 <b>Auto capture delay</b>）。
• <b>Cancel</b> — exit; files already captured stay saved.=• <b>取消</b>：退出；已截取的文件保持保存状态。
<b>Group capture — Group thumbnails…</b>=<b>分组截取：分组缩略图…</b>
When a <b>group entity</b> is selected (click the <b>▦ group header</b>), the group action bar offers <b>Group thumbnails…</b> to refresh every member thumbnail in one session.=选中<b>分组实体</b>（点击 <b>▦ 分组标题</b>）时，分组操作栏会提供<b>分组缩略图…</b>，可在一次会话中刷新所有成员的缩略图。
• <b>Exactly as many characters selected in Studio as poses</b> in the group.=• 在工作室中选择的<b>角色数与分组中的姿势数完全相同</b>。
• One-to-one assignment must succeed: <b>Male</b> / <b>Female</b> pose tags and the <b>Chars</b> priority list (see <b>Multi-character apply</b>).=• 一对一分配必须成功：取决于 <b>Male</b> / <b>Female</b> 姿势标签和<b>角色</b>优先级列表（见<b>多角色应用</b>）。
• Poses do <b>not</b> need to be applied beforehand — the capture run applies them.=• 姿势<b>不需要</b>事先应用，截取过程会自动应用。
• Disabled during ZIP import preview or while another capture overlay is active.=• ZIP 导入预览期间或有其他截取叠加层时不可用。
1. Select characters in Studio; select the group header.=1. 在工作室中选择角色；选中分组标题。
2. Click <b>Group thumbnails…</b>. All poses are applied at once using the same rules as <b>Apply to characters…</b> (including <b>Apply relative positions</b>, <b>Adjust for body height</b>, and <b>Adjust for object scale</b> when those toggles are on).=2. 点击<b>分组缩略图…</b>。按与<b>应用到角色…</b>相同的规则一次性应用所有姿势（开关开启时包括<b>套用相对位置</b>、<b>根据身高调整</b>和<b>根据物件缩放调整</b>）。
3. Frame the scene in the overlay.=3. 在叠加层中取景。
4. For each pose in <b>display order</b>, characters assigned to other poses render in Studio <b>simple color</b> (monocolor) so only the focus character shows full detail.=4. 按<b>显示顺序</b>处理每个姿势时，分配到其他姿势的角色会以工作室<b>单色</b>渲染，只有焦点角色显示完整细节。
5. Use <b>Capture</b>, <b>Skip</b>, <b>Auto-capture</b>, or <b>Cancel</b> as in single-pose capture. Status shows <b>Group thumb n / total</b>.=5. 与单个姿势截取一样使用<b>截取</b>、<b>跳过</b>、<b>自动截取</b>或<b>取消</b>。状态栏显示 <b>Group thumb n / total</b>。
<b>Example:</b> Group with poses 1 and 2; characters A and B assigned by priority. Pose 1 captures with B in monocolor; pose 2 captures with A in monocolor.=<b>示例：</b>分组中有姿势 1 和 2；角色 A 和 B 按优先级分配。截取姿势 1 时 B 为单色；截取姿势 2 时 A 为单色。
Missing thumbnails use a neutral placeholder until a file is loaded or regenerated.=缺失的缩略图会使用中性占位图，直到载入或重新生成文件为止。
← Import & export (ZIP)=← 导入与导出（ZIP）
<b>Options</b> side panel=<b>选项</b>侧面板
• <b>Card width slider</b> — minimum card width; the grid adds columns or stretches cards to fill the row.=• <b>卡片宽度滑块</b>：卡片的最小宽度；网格会增加列数或拉伸卡片来填满一行。
• <b>Apply stored relative positions when applying a group</b> — global layout toggle (see <b>Pose groups</b>).=• <b>应用分组时套用已保存的相对位置</b>：全局布局开关（见<b>姿势分组</b>）。
• <b>Adjust relative layout for body height (saved per pose)</b> — scales saved <b>offset.y</b> from body-height ratios; requires relative positions.=• <b>根据身高调整相对布局（按姿势保存）</b>：根据身高比例缩放保存的 <b>offset.y</b>；需要相对位置。
• <b>Select all filtered / Deselect all</b> — bulk selection in the current filtered list.=• <b>全选筛选结果 / 取消全选</b>：在当前筛选后的列表中批量选择。
• <b>Keyboard shortcuts</b> — read-only here; assign in Configuration Manager under <b>Pose Browser · Keyboard shortcuts</b> (next/previous pose; next/previous browse target; undo/redo; toggle undocked pose stash). Active while the browser is focused unless a text field holds keyboard focus.=• <b>键盘快捷键</b>：此处只读；请在 Configuration Manager 的 <b>Pose Browser · Keyboard shortcuts</b> 下分配（上一个/下一个姿势；上一个/下一个浏览目标；撤销/重做；切换独立姿势暂存面板）。浏览器获得焦点时生效，文本框占用键盘焦点时除外。
• <b>pose_browser_options.json</b> — layout tier (Full/List/Mini) with remembered window rects per mode, sort mode + direction, card width, items per page, floating stash window rect, docked-vs-float stash preference.=• <b>pose_browser_options.json</b>：布局级别（完整/列表/迷你）及各模式记住的窗口矩形、排序方式和方向、卡片宽度、每页项目数、浮动暂存窗口矩形、暂存停靠/浮动偏好。
• <b>pose_stash.json</b> — pose stash entries (FK/IK snapshots) and auto-delete-after-apply flag.=• <b>pose_stash.json</b>：姿势暂存条目（FK/IK 快照）和应用后自动删除标记。
• <b>pose_browser_history.json</b> — per-character pose history (undo/redo snapshots).=• <b>pose_browser_history.json</b>：每个角色的姿势历史（撤销/重做快照）。
• <b>pose_tags.tsv</b> — per-pose tags and favorites (atomic save).=• <b>pose_tags.tsv</b>：每个姿势的标签和收藏（原子保存）。
• <b>pose_groups.tsv</b> — group membership, tags, relative offsets, body heights per pose (v3 TSV).=• <b>pose_groups.tsv</b>：分组成员、标签、相对偏移、每个姿势的身高（v3 TSV）。
• <b>pose_items.tsv</b> — workspace items registered per pose (catalog paths, layout, attach data; v5 TSV).=• <b>pose_items.tsv</b>：每个姿势登记的工作区物品（目录路径、布局、附着数据；v5 TSV）。
• <b>pose_browser_character_config.json</b> — unified character priority list for multi-character apply.=• <b>pose_browser_character_config.json</b>：多角色应用使用的统一角色优先级列表。
• BepInEx <b>Pose Browser</b> section — <b>Card column width</b> and <b>Items per page</b> mirrored from Options.=• BepInEx 中的 <b>Pose Browser</b> 部分：与选项同步的 <b>Card column width</b> 和 <b>Items per page</b>。
← Thumbnails=← 缩略图
→ Tag storage (advanced)=→ 标签存储（高级）
Tags and favorites are stored in <b>pose_tags.tsv</b> under the Sandbox config folder. Keys are tied to pose paths relative to the library root.=标签和收藏保存在 Sandbox 配置文件夹下的 <b>pose_tags.tsv</b> 中。键与相对于姿势库根目录的姿势路径绑定。
A legacy <b>pose_tags.json</b> may be imported once if present. The TSV format is text-safe and avoids Unity JsonUtility pitfalls for nested data.=如果存在旧版的 <b>pose_tags.json</b>，会导入一次。TSV 格式对文本更安全，并可避免 Unity JsonUtility 处理嵌套数据时的问题。

How long the camera takes to glide from the first to the last figure in iteration-group previews (numbered slots 1, 2, 3…). It ping-pongs back and forth. Higher \= slower. Other previews are unaffected.=在迭代组预览（编号栏位 1、2、3…）中，镜头从第一个人物滑到最后一个人物所需的时间。会来回往返。数值越大越慢。不影响其他预览。

<size\=18><b>Pose Browser</b></size>=<size\=18><b>姿势浏览器</b></size>
<size\=17><b>Folders & library</b></size>=<size\=17><b>文件夹与姿势库</b></size>
<size\=17><b>Search & filters</b></size>=<size\=17><b>搜索与筛选</b></size>

• Saved <b>anchor-relative</b> position and rotation vs the character guide at save time.=• 保存时记录相对于角色控制器的<b>锚点相对</b>位置和旋转。
• On load, position and item scale adjust for the character’s current <b>Studio object scale</b> and <b>body height</b> (same ratio logic as group relative positions).=• 读取时，位置和道具缩放会根据角色当前的 <b>Studio 物体缩放</b>和<b>身高</b>进行调整（与组相对位置的比例逻辑相同）。
• Items saved on a <b>body part</b> store a workspace tree path and Studio <b>changeAmount</b> attach offsets (v5 TSV). Default load reparents via the tree; <b>Load as free</b> uses the same world layout without parenting.=• 保存在<b>身体部位</b>上的道具会记录工作区树路径和 Studio <b>changeAmount</b> 附着偏移（v5 TSV）。默认读取时通过树重新设置父级；<b>作为自由物体读取</b>则使用相同的世界布局但不设置父级。
• ⚠ <b>Yellow</b> banner — selected character does not have this pose applied (save/load still work). Orange ⚠ on a row — last load warning (e.g. body part not found).=• ⚠ <b>黄色</b>横幅 —— 所选角色未应用此姿势（仍可保存/读取）。行上的橙色 ⚠ —— 上次读取的警告（如找不到身体部位）。
<size\=17><b>Pose groups</b></size>=<size\=17><b>姿势组</b></size>
• <b>Group tags</b> — edited via <b>Group tags…</b>; used for <b>filtering</b> (include/exclude in the tag window) and shown on the group header.=• <b>组标签</b> —— 通过<b>组标签…</b>编辑；用于<b>筛选</b>（在标签窗口中包含/排除），并显示在组标题上。
• <b>Pose tags</b> — per-card tags (e.g. <b>Male</b> / <b>Female</b> for multi-character apply).=• <b>姿势标签</b> —— 每张卡片各自的标签（如用于多角色应用的<b>男</b> / <b>女</b>）。
• <b>Exclude</b> filters: ungrouped poses with an excluded tag are hidden; inside a visible group <b>all members stay</b> but cards with excluded pose tags are <b>dimmed</b> and tag names show in <b>red</b>.=• <b>排除</b>筛选：带有被排除标签的未分组姿势会被隐藏；在可见的组内，<b>所有成员保留</b>，但带有被排除姿势标签的卡片会<b>变暗</b>，标签名显示为<b>红色</b>。
• <b>Move…</b> / <b>Copy…</b> — works on ungrouped poses, or when exactly <b>one full group</b> is selected (all members). The whole group moves/copies together.=• <b>移动…</b> / <b>复制…</b> —— 适用于未分组的姿势，或恰好选中<b>一个完整组</b>（所有成员）时。整个组会一起移动/复制。
• <b>Delete…</b> — can remove group members or entire groups per confirmation.=• <b>删除…</b> —— 可按确认移除组成员或整个组。
• <b>Export…</b> — include group metadata (<b>memberRelativeOffsets</b>, <b>memberBodyHeights</b> when saved) when every member of a group is selected, or use <b>Export…</b> from the group bar.=• <b>导出…</b> —— 选中组的全部成员时会包含组元数据（<b>memberRelativeOffsets</b>，已保存时还有 <b>memberBodyHeights</b>），也可以在组栏中使用<b>导出…</b>。
<size\=17><b>Pose stash</b></size>=<size\=17><b>姿势暂存</b></size>
• <b>pose_stash.json</b> — stashed poses (base64 FK/IK blobs), auto-delete preference.=• <b>pose_stash.json</b> —— 暂存的姿势（base64 FK/IK 数据）、自动删除偏好。
• <b>pose_browser_options.json</b> — floating window rect and <b>docked vs float</b> preference.=• <b>pose_browser_options.json</b> —— 浮动窗口位置大小，以及<b>停靠还是浮动</b>的偏好。
<size\=17><b>Multi-character apply</b></size>=<size\=17><b>多角色应用</b></size>
3. Reorder with <b>↑</b> / <b>↓</b> — <b>top \= highest priority</b>.=3. 用 <b>↑</b> / <b>↓</b> 调整顺序 —— <b>最上方 \= 优先级最高</b>。
4. <b>m</b> / <b>f</b> toggles gender for that slot; <b>✕</b> removes it. <color\=#ffbb88>Orange</color> \= not in scene; <color\=#88ffaa>green</color> \= selected in Studio.=4. <b>m</b> / <b>f</b> 切换该栏位的性别；<b>✕</b> 将其移除。<color\=#ffbb88>橙色</color> \= 不在场景中；<color\=#88ffaa>绿色</color> \= 已在 Studio 中选中。

Pose Browser · Plugin compatibility=姿势浏览器 · 插件兼容
KKPE — save breast/butt gravity & force in poses=KKPE：在姿势中保存胸部/臀部重力和力度
When KKPE is installed, embed Advanced Mode breast and butt gravity/force into pose files on save/update (ExtendedSave block in the pose file). Editable in Pose Browser → Options → Plugin compatibility.=安装 KKPE 时，保存/更新姿势时将高级模式下胸部和臀部的重力/力度嵌入姿势文件（姿势文件中的 ExtendedSave 数据块）。可在“姿势浏览器 → 选项 → 插件兼容”中修改。
PoseBrowser: KKPE detected — breast/butt pose embedding enabled.=PoseBrowser：检测到 KKPE，已启用胸部/臀部姿势嵌入。
Pose Browser=姿势浏览器
Scales the entire Pose Browser UI (text, buttons, panels, cards). Useful on high-DPI / 4K displays where controls feel too small. Editable in Pose Browser → Options.=缩放整个姿势浏览器界面（文字、按钮、面板、卡片）。适用于控件显得过小的高 DPI / 4K 显示器。可在“姿势浏览器 → 选项”中修改。
Minimum width of each pose card column in the grid (full layout). Editable in Pose Browser → Options.=网格中每列姿势卡片的最小宽度（完整布局）。可在“姿势浏览器 → 选项”中修改。
Items per page (grid)=每页项目数（网格）
Maximum poses per page in the thumbnail grid; 0 shows all. Editable in Pose Browser → Options.=缩略图网格中每页最多显示的姿势数；0 表示全部显示。可在“姿势浏览器 → 选项”中修改。
Auto-capture delay (seconds)=自动截取延迟（秒）
Pause after applying each pose before taking a thumbnail during Auto-capture. Editable in Pose Browser → Options.=自动截取时，每次应用姿势后等待多久再截取缩略图。可在“姿势浏览器 → 选项”中修改。
History entries per character=每个角色的历史记录条数
Maximum pose history snapshots kept per character; oldest entries are removed. Editable in Pose Browser → Options.=每个角色最多保留的姿势历史快照数，超出时删除最旧的记录。可在“姿势浏览器 → 选项”中修改。
Compact hover thumbnail width (px)=紧凑模式悬停缩略图宽度（px）
Width of the thumbnail preview shown when hovering a pose in compact list view. Editable in Pose Browser → Options.=在紧凑列表视图中悬停姿势时显示的缩略图预览宽度。可在“姿势浏览器 → 选项”中修改。
Next pose=下一个姿势
Previous pose=上一个姿势
Toggle Pose Browser window=开关姿势浏览器窗口
Toggle minimize (PB chip)=切换最小化（PB 标签）
Undo pose change=撤销姿势更改
Redo pose change=重做姿势更改
Toggle undocked pose stash=切换独立姿势暂存面板
Freeze animation speed on apply=应用时冻结动画速度
When enabled, sets each affected character's animation speed to 0 after applying a pose from the browser or restoring a history entry. Editable in Pose Browser → Options.=启用后，从浏览器应用姿势或恢复历史记录后，会把每个受影响角色的动画速度设为 0。可在“姿势浏览器 → 选项”中修改。
Independent minimize chip position=独立的最小化标签位置
When enabled, the draggable PB chip and the main window each remember their own position across minimize/restore. Editable in Pose Browser → Options.=启用后，可拖动的 PB 标签和主窗口在最小化/恢复时各自记住自己的位置。可在“姿势浏览器 → 选项”中修改。
Pose Browser · Keyboard shortcuts=姿势浏览器 · 键盘快捷键
Active while Pose Browser is open. Uses BepInEx KeyboardShortcut (main key + optional modifiers in Configuration Manager). Leave unassigned (None) to disable. Browse keys match Folder ◀ / ▶ (root, folders depth-first, all poses, favorites).=姿势浏览器打开时生效。使用 BepInEx KeyboardShortcut（在 Configuration Manager 中设置主键和可选的修饰键）。保持未分配（None）即可停用。浏览键与 文件夹 ◀ / ▶ 一致（根目录、深度优先的文件夹、全部姿势、收藏）。
Next browse target (folder step)=下一个浏览目标（按文件夹）
Previous browse target (folder step)=上一个浏览目标（按文件夹）
Open or close the Pose Browser window (toolbar toggle stays in sync). Uses BepInEx KeyboardShortcut (main key + optional modifiers in Configuration Manager). Leave unassigned (None) to disable.=打开或关闭姿势浏览器窗口（工具栏开关保持同步）。使用 BepInEx KeyboardShortcut（在 Configuration Manager 中设置主键和可选的修饰键）。保持未分配（None）即可停用。
When the window is open: minimize to the draggable PB chip or restore from it. Uses BepInEx KeyboardShortcut (main key + optional modifiers in Configuration Manager). Leave unassigned (None) to disable.=窗口打开时：最小化为可拖动的 PB 标签，或从标签恢复。使用 BepInEx KeyboardShortcut（在 Configuration Manager 中设置主键和可选的修饰键）。保持未分配（None）即可停用。
Undo the last pose history step for Studio-selected characters. Active while Pose Browser is open. Uses BepInEx KeyboardShortcut (main key + optional modifiers in Configuration Manager). Leave unassigned (None) to disable. Browse keys match Folder ◀ / ▶ (root, folders depth-first, all poses, favorites).=撤销工作室中选中角色的上一步姿势历史。姿势浏览器打开时生效。使用 BepInEx KeyboardShortcut（在 Configuration Manager 中设置主键和可选的修饰键）。保持未分配（None）即可停用。浏览键与 文件夹 ◀ / ▶ 一致（根目录、深度优先的文件夹、全部姿势、收藏）。
Redo pose history for Studio-selected characters. Active while Pose Browser is open. Uses BepInEx KeyboardShortcut (main key + optional modifiers in Configuration Manager). Leave unassigned (None) to disable. Browse keys match Folder ◀ / ▶ (root, folders depth-first, all poses, favorites).=重做工作室中选中角色的姿势历史。姿势浏览器打开时生效。使用 BepInEx KeyboardShortcut（在 Configuration Manager 中设置主键和可选的修饰键）。保持未分配（None）即可停用。浏览键与 文件夹 ◀ / ▶ 一致（根目录、深度优先的文件夹、全部姿势、收藏）。
Open or close the floating pose stash window (undocked). Uses BepInEx KeyboardShortcut (main key + optional modifiers in Configuration Manager). Leave unassigned (None) to disable.=打开或关闭浮动的姿势暂存窗口（独立窗口）。使用 BepInEx KeyboardShortcut（在 Configuration Manager 中设置主键和可选的修饰键）。保持未分配（None）即可停用。

• <b>Group header</b> (▦ row) \= select the <b>group entity</b> (rename, tags, export, <b>Apply to characters…</b>, <b>Group thumbnails…</b>, save/clear <b>positions</b>). Card checkboxes \= individual poses.=• <b>组标题</b>（▦ 行）\= 选择<b>组实体</b>（重命名、标签、导出、<b>应用到角色…</b>、<b>组缩略图…</b>、保存/清除<b>位置</b>）。卡片复选框 \= 单个姿势。
• <b>Exclude</b> tag filters: hide ungrouped poses; grouped poses use <b>group + pose</b> tags. In a visible group, excluded tags dim cards (red tag text).=• <b>排除</b>标签筛选：隐藏未分组的姿势；分组的姿势使用<b>组 + 姿势</b>标签。在可见的组内，被排除的标签会使卡片变暗（标签文字为红色）。
• <b>Grouped poses</b> / <b>Thumbnails</b> in the tag filter panel cycle neutral → hide (−) → only (+), like per-tag filters.=• 标签筛选面板中的<b>分组姿势</b> / <b>缩略图</b>会循环切换中立 → 隐藏 (−) → 仅显示 (+)，与单个标签的筛选相同。
• Move/Copy one <b>full group</b> at a time. Data: <b>pose_groups.tsv</b> in Sandbox config.=• 每次只能移动/复制一个<b>完整组</b>。数据：Sandbox 配置中的 <b>pose_groups.tsv</b>。
Save character spacing and facing for a group and re-apply it with the group. The <b>first pose</b> in grid display order is the <b>anchor</b> (position and rotation reference). Every other pose stores a <b>local position offset</b> (in the anchor's frame), <b>relative rotation</b> (vs anchor), <b>maker body height</b>, and <b>Studio object scale</b> on that pose path. Persisted in <b>pose_groups.tsv</b> and v7 ZIP (<b>memberRelativeOffsets</b>, <b>memberRelativeRotations</b>, <b>memberBodyHeights</b>, <b>memberObjectScales</b>).=保存组内角色的间距和朝向，并在应用组时重新应用。网格显示顺序中的<b>第一个姿势</b>是<b>锚点</b>（位置和旋转的参照）。其他每个姿势会在该姿势路径上记录<b>局部位置偏移</b>（以锚点为坐标系）、<b>相对旋转</b>（相对于锚点）、<b>编辑器身高</b>和 <b>Studio 物体缩放</b>。保存在 <b>pose_groups.tsv</b> 和 v7 ZIP 中（<b>memberRelativeOffsets</b>、<b>memberRelativeRotations</b>、<b>memberBodyHeights</b>、<b>memberObjectScales</b>）。
<b>To save positions</b> (<b>Save positions…</b> on the group bar):=<b>保存位置</b>（组栏上的<b>保存位置…</b>）：
1. Set up <b>Chars</b> and pose <b>Male</b> / <b>Female</b> tags if needed.=1. 如有需要，设置<b>角色</b>和姿势的<b>男</b> / <b>女</b>标签。
2. Select <b>exactly as many characters as poses</b> in Studio.=2. 在 Studio 中选中<b>与姿势数量相同的角色</b>。
3. Apply the group (<b>Apply to characters…</b>) so poses map in <b>display order</b> (first pose → anchor character).=3. 应用该组（<b>应用到角色…</b>），使姿势按<b>显示顺序</b>对应（第一个姿势 → 锚点角色）。
4. Arrange characters, then <b>Save positions…</b> without applying another pose in between (see button tooltip if disabled).=4. 摆好角色后点击<b>保存位置…</b>，中间不要再应用其他姿势（按钮不可用时请看按钮提示）。
<b>To apply saved positions</b> — same apply path (same pose order and <b>Chars</b> priority). After poses are applied:=<b>应用已保存的位置</b> —— 应用方式相同（相同的姿势顺序和<b>角色</b>优先级）。姿势应用后：
• <b>Apply relative positions</b> (global; group bar or <b>Options</b>) — each non-anchor character moves to <b>anchor position + anchor rotation × saved offset</b> (orbits with the anchor) and rotates to <b>anchor rotation × saved relative rotation</b>. The anchor character is not moved.=• <b>应用相对位置</b>（全局；组栏或<b>选项</b>）—— 每个非锚点角色移动到<b>锚点位置 + 锚点旋转 × 已保存偏移</b>（随锚点环绕），并旋转到<b>锚点旋转 × 已保存相对旋转</b>。锚点角色不会移动。
• <b>Adjust for body height</b> (needs relative positions on) — still applies the full offset, but <b>offset.y</b> is scaled from saved vs current body-height ratios on each pose path (spread ratio when heights differed at save; otherwise anchor or averaged scale). No fixed meter constant.=• <b>按身高调整</b>（需开启相对位置）—— 仍应用完整偏移，但 <b>offset.y</b> 会按每个姿势路径上保存时与当前的身高比例缩放（保存时身高不同则用分摊比例，否则用锚点或平均缩放）。不使用固定的米制常数。
• <b>Adjust for object scale</b> (needs relative positions on) — scales saved offset <b>X/Y/Z</b> from saved vs current Studio object-scale ratios (same spread logic as body height). Relative rotation is unchanged.=• <b>按物体缩放调整</b>（需开启相对位置）—— 按保存时与当前的 Studio 物体缩放比例缩放已保存偏移的 <b>X/Y/Z</b>（与身高的分摊逻辑相同）。相对旋转不变。
• <b>Clear positions</b> — removes stored offsets, heights, scales, and rotations for that group.=• <b>清除位置</b> —— 移除该组已保存的偏移、身高、缩放和旋转。
<b>Thumbnails…</b> (selection bar, one or more poses checked) — capture preview PNGs for selected library poses. Each step applies that pose to Studio-selected character(s), then opens the capture overlay: drag/resize the green frame, <b>Capture</b>, <b>Skip</b>, <b>Auto-capture</b> (remaining poses after a configurable pause — Options / BepInEx), or <b>Cancel</b>.=<b>缩略图…</b>（选择栏，勾选一个或多个姿势）—— 为所选的库中姿势截取预览 PNG。每一步会将该姿势应用到 Studio 中所选的角色，然后打开截图叠加层：拖动/调整绿色框的大小，<b>截取</b>、<b>跳过</b>、<b>自动截取</b>（在可配置的暂停后处理剩余姿势 —— 选项 / BepInEx），或<b>取消</b>。
<b>Group thumbnails…</b> (group entity bar when the <b>group header</b> is selected) — capture one thumbnail per pose in the group while all characters stay posed together:=<b>组缩略图…</b>（选中<b>组标题</b>时的组实体栏）—— 在所有角色保持一起摆姿势的状态下，为组内每个姿势截取一张缩略图：
• Requires <b>exactly as many Studio characters as poses</b>, with the same one-to-one gender / <b>Chars</b> assignment as multi-character apply (poses need not be applied beforehand).=• 需要 <b>Studio 中的角色数量与姿势数量相同</b>，且性别 / <b>角色</b>的一对一分配与多角色应用相同（姿势无需事先应用）。
• On start: applies <b>all</b> group poses at once (same path as <b>Apply to characters…</b>, including <b>Apply relative positions</b> / height / object-scale toggles when enabled).=• 开始时：一次性应用组内<b>所有</b>姿势（与<b>应用到角色…</b>的方式相同，启用时也包括<b>应用相对位置</b> / 身高 / 物体缩放开关）。
• Overlay: frame the whole scene once. For each pose in display order, characters assigned to <b>other</b> poses render in Studio <b>simple color</b> (monocolor) so only the focus character shows full detail.=• 叠加层：整个场景只需框一次。按显示顺序处理每个姿势时，分配给<b>其他</b>姿势的角色以 Studio <b>单色</b>显示，只有当前焦点角色显示完整细节。
• <b>Capture</b> saves that pose's PNG and advances; <b>Skip</b> leaves that pose unchanged; <b>Auto-capture</b> chains the rest; <b>Cancel</b> restores all characters from monocolor. Not available during import preview.=• <b>截取</b>保存该姿势的 PNG 并前进；<b>跳过</b>保持该姿势不变；<b>自动截取</b>连续处理其余姿势；<b>取消</b>将所有角色从单色恢复。导入预览期间不可用。
• <b>Chars</b> (top bar) opens the <b>priority list</b> (top \= first). <b>Load characters from scene</b>, <b>Remove missing from scene</b>, ↑↓ reorder, <b>m</b>/<b>f</b> gender button, ✕ remove. Saved in <b>pose_browser_character_config.json</b>.=• <b>角色</b>（顶栏）打开<b>优先级列表</b>（最上方 \= 第一个）。<b>从场景读取角色</b>、<b>移除场景中不存在的角色</b>、↑↓ 调整顺序、<b>m</b>/<b>f</b> 性别按钮、✕ 移除。保存在 <b>pose_browser_character_config.json</b> 中。
• <b>Apply to characters…</b> when <b>2+ poses</b> selected, or <b>one group header</b> selected. Select characters in Studio first.=• 选中 <b>2 个以上姿势</b>或选中<b>一个组标题</b>时使用<b>应用到角色…</b>。请先在 Studio 中选择角色。
• <b>Male</b> / <b>Female</b> <i>pose</i> tags → next free character of that gender in list order. <b>Untagged</b> → list order top to bottom; each character gets at most <b>one</b> pose per apply (extras skipped; leftover chars may get a second-pass pose).=• <b>男</b> / <b>女</b> <i>姿势</i>标签 → 按列表顺序分给下一个该性别的空闲角色。<b>无标签</b> → 按列表从上到下；每次应用中每个角色最多获得<b>一个</b>姿势（多余的跳过；剩余角色可能在第二轮获得姿势）。
• Left-click thumbnail still applies <b>one</b> pose to <b>all</b> selected characters (separate from multi-apply).=• 左键单击缩略图仍会将<b>一个</b>姿势应用到<b>所有</b>选中角色（与多角色应用不同）。
• Each pose apply records <b>before</b> and <b>after</b> snapshots (absolute pose, position, rotation) per character. The pre-apply entry is skipped when unchanged from the current history point.=• 每次应用姿势都会为每个角色记录<b>应用前</b>和<b>应用后</b>的快照（绝对姿势、位置、旋转）。如果与当前历史点相比没有变化，则跳过应用前的条目。
• <b>Undo</b> / <b>Redo</b> (top bar or shortcuts) affect only <b>Studio-selected</b> characters. <b>History</b> pane lists the same selection, grouped by character, newest first.=• <b>撤销</b> / <b>重做</b>（顶栏或快捷键）只影响 <b>Studio 中选中</b>的角色。<b>历史</b>窗格列出相同的选择，按角色分组，最新的在前。
• Click a history line to jump to that snapshot; checkboxes choose pose / position / rotation. Data: <b>pose_browser_history.json</b>. Max entries per character: <b>Options</b> and BepInEx config.=• 点击历史行可跳转到该快照；复选框选择姿势 / 位置 / 旋转。数据：<b>pose_browser_history.json</b>。每个角色的最大条目数：<b>选项</b>和 BepInEx 配置。
A temporary pose clipboard (FK/IK only — not saved to the library). Distinct from <b>History</b> (automatic undo timeline).=临时的姿势剪贴板（仅 FK/IK —— 不保存到姿势库）。不同于<b>历史</b>（自动撤销时间线）。
<b>Stash</b> (top bar, or character row in <b>List</b> / <b>Mini</b>) — toggles the stash panel open or closed. If already open (docked or floating), it closes. If closed, it reopens in the last mode you used (<b>docked</b> beside the browser or <b>floating</b>).=<b>暂存</b>（顶栏，或<b>列表</b> / <b>迷你</b>中的角色行）—— 打开或关闭暂存面板。如果已打开（停靠或浮动），则关闭。如果已关闭，则以上次使用的模式重新打开（停靠在浏览器旁边的<b>停靠</b>模式或<b>浮动</b>模式）。
<b>Stash selected character</b> — capture from <b>exactly one</b> Studio-selected character. Each entry is labeled with character name and timestamp.=<b>暂存所选角色</b> —— 从 Studio 中选中的<b>恰好一个</b>角色截取。每个条目都标有角色名和时间戳。
<b>Click an entry</b> — apply that stashed pose to <b>all</b> currently selected characters (one or many).=<b>点击条目</b> —— 将该暂存姿势应用到当前选中的<b>所有</b>角色（一个或多个）。
<b>x</b> on a row — delete that entry (<b>Yes</b> / <b>No</b> confirm). <b>Clear entire stash</b> at the bottom (confirm required).=行上的 <b>x</b> —— 删除该条目（<b>是</b> / <b>否</b>确认）。底部的<b>清空全部暂存</b>（需要确认）。
<b>Auto-delete after apply</b> — remove an entry once applied.=<b>应用后自动删除</b> —— 条目应用后即将其移除。
<b>Docked pane</b> — side panel like <b>History</b>; closes when the main Pose Browser closes. <b>Float</b> undocks to a free window (drag title bar, resize ◢).=<b>停靠窗格</b> —— 与<b>历史</b>类似的侧面板；主 Pose Browser 关闭时随之关闭。<b>浮动</b>将其分离为自由窗口（拖动标题栏，◢ 调整大小）。
<b>Floating window</b> — <b>Dock</b> re-attaches beside the browser (or closes if the browser is hidden); <b>×</b> closes only the stash. Stays open when you close the main browser.=<b>浮动窗口</b> —— <b>停靠</b>重新附着到浏览器旁边（浏览器隐藏时则关闭）；<b>×</b> 只关闭暂存。关闭主浏览器时仍保持打开。
Hotkey <b>Toggle undocked pose stash</b> — open/close the <b>floating</b> stash only (Configuration Manager → <b>Pose Browser · Keyboard shortcuts</b>).=快捷键<b>切换浮动姿势暂存</b> —— 只打开/关闭<b>浮动</b>暂存（配置管理器 → <b>Pose Browser · 键盘快捷键</b>）。
Data: <b>pose_stash.json</b>. Floating window size/position and dock vs float preference: <b>pose_browser_options.json</b>.=数据：<b>pose_stash.json</b>。浮动窗口的大小/位置以及停靠还是浮动的偏好：<b>pose_browser_options.json</b>。
<b>View (…)</b> in the top bar (Window section) cycles <b>Full → List → Mini → Full</b>; choice is saved. Each mode remembers its own window size, position, and resize (stored in <b>pose_browser_options.json</b>).=顶栏中的<b>视图 (…)</b>（窗口部分）按<b>完整 → 列表 → 迷你 → 完整</b>循环切换；所选模式会被保存。每种模式各自记住窗口大小、位置和缩放（保存在 <b>pose_browser_options.json</b> 中）。
• <b>List</b> — optional folder tree (<b>Tree</b> toggle; separate saved window width with tree shown vs hidden, same position). Character row (<b>Chars</b>, Studio selection label, <b>Stash</b>). Scrollable list of <b>filtered</b> poses. <b>Prev / Next</b> applies in list order and wraps. Click a row for one pose; click a <b>▦ group</b> header for multi-character group apply.=• <b>列表</b> —— 可选的文件夹树（<b>树</b>开关；显示与隐藏树时分别保存窗口宽度，位置相同）。角色行（<b>角色</b>、Studio 选择标签、<b>暂存</b>）。可滚动的<b>筛选后</b>姿势列表。<b>上一个 / 下一个</b>按列表顺序应用并循环。点击一行应用一个姿势；点击 <b>▦ 组</b>标题进行多角色组应用。
• <b>Mini</b> — <b>Folder</b> arrows walk <b>Root only</b>, every subfolder in depth-first order, <b>All poses</b>, then <b>Favorites</b>, wrapping; the <b>first filtered pose</b> in the new scope is applied immediately. <b>Pose</b> arrows walk the filtered list, apply each step, and wrap. <b>Reapply</b> repeats the current pose.=• <b>迷你</b> —— <b>文件夹</b>箭头依次遍历<b>仅根目录</b>、按深度优先顺序的每个子文件夹、<b>全部姿势</b>，然后是<b>收藏</b>，并循环；新范围中的<b>第一个筛选后姿势</b>会立即应用。<b>姿势</b>箭头遍历筛选后的列表，每一步都应用并循环。<b>重新应用</b>重复当前姿势。
• <b>↻</b> — refresh tree.=• <b>↻</b> —— 刷新树。
• <b>All poses</b> — every subfolder, recursively (disabled while Move/Copy destination pick is active).=• <b>全部姿势</b> —— 递归包含所有子文件夹（选择移动/复制目标时不可用）。
• <b>★ Favorites</b> — all favorited poses library-wide (not a disk folder; saving while this row is active uses the pose root).=• <b>★ 收藏</b> —— 整个姿势库中所有已收藏的姿势（不是磁盘文件夹；在此行激活时保存会使用姿势根目录）。
• <b>Root only</b> — files in the pose root only; during Move/Copy, also picks <b>pose root</b> as destination.=• <b>仅根目录</b> —— 仅姿势根目录中的文件；移动/复制时也会选择<b>姿势根目录</b>作为目标。
• Click a folder name — browse that folder, or during Move/Copy sets <b>destination</b> without changing the grid.=• 点击文件夹名 —— 浏览该文件夹，或在移动/复制时设为<b>目标</b>而不改变网格。
• Footer: <b>New folder</b>, <b>Rename</b> / <b>Delete</b> (empty only); during Move/Copy/import, <b>Apply</b>/<b>Cancel</b> appear at the top of this footer.=• 底栏：<b>新建文件夹</b>、<b>重命名</b> / <b>删除</b>（仅限空文件夹）；移动/复制/导入时，<b>应用</b>/<b>取消</b>会出现在此底栏顶部。
• <b>Export branch…</b> / <b>Export library tree…</b> — <b>Full</b> layout only: folder footer when a folder is selected / at library root (v4 ZIP of that branch or the whole tree, includes pose groups when present).=• <b>导出分支…</b> / <b>导出姿势库树…</b> —— 仅<b>完整</b>布局：选中文件夹时 / 在姿势库根目录时显示在文件夹底栏（将该分支或整棵树导出为 v4 ZIP，存在姿势组时一并包含）。
• After <b>Import…</b>, the grid shows a preview: thumbnail click toggles inclusion (checkbox + Ctrl/Shift work). Use <b>Cancel import</b> in the bottom bar or <b>Cancel</b> in the folder footer to abort. <b>Tree branch</b> packs create a named subfolder under the destination you pick. v2–v5 packs still import.=• <b>导入…</b>后，网格会显示预览：点击缩略图切换是否包含（复选框 + Ctrl/Shift 均可用）。使用底栏中的<b>取消导入</b>或文件夹底栏中的<b>取消</b>来中止。<b>树分支</b>包会在所选目标下创建一个命名子文件夹。v2–v5 的包仍可导入。
• <b>Export…</b> in the <b>selection bar</b> saves checked library poses to a v7 <b>.zip</b> (tags/favorites; pose groups with offsets/rotations/heights/scales when fully selected).=• <b>选择栏</b>中的<b>导出…</b>会将勾选的库中姿势保存为 v7 <b>.zip</b>（标签/收藏；完整选中时还包括带偏移/旋转/身高/缩放的姿势组）。
• External tools must build <b>stored</b> (uncompressed) ZIP entries — see <b>Modules/PoseBrowser/POSE_ZIP_FORMAT.md</b> in the repo.=• 外部工具必须生成<b>存储</b>（不压缩）的 ZIP 条目 —— 见仓库中的 <b>Modules/PoseBrowser/POSE_ZIP_FORMAT.md</b>。
• Checkbox — select without applying; <b>Ctrl+click</b> and <b>Shift+click</b> work on the checkbox too (range \= filtered list).=• 复选框 —— 只选择不应用；<b>Ctrl+单击</b>和 <b>Shift+单击</b>在复选框上同样有效（范围 \= 筛选后的列表）。
• <b>Left-click</b> thumbnail — select one + apply pose.=• <b>左键单击</b>缩略图 —— 选中一个并应用姿势。
• <b>Ctrl+click</b> — add/remove from selection.=• <b>Ctrl+单击</b> —— 加入/移出选择。
• <b>Shift+click</b> — range select in the filtered list.=• <b>Shift+单击</b> —— 在筛选后的列表中范围选择。
• <b>Right-click</b> thumbnail — apply pose only (selection unchanged).=• <b>右键单击</b>缩略图 —— 仅应用姿势（选择不变）。
• With pagination (Options), use ◀ ▶; card width slider controls minimum size; extra width fills the row or adds columns.=• 开启分页时（选项），使用 ◀ ▶；卡片宽度滑块控制最小尺寸；多余的宽度会填满该行或增加列数。
• <b>Solo</b> / <b>▦ Group</b> / <b>▦ Pose</b> / <b>All</b> — ungrouped pose checkboxes; visible group headers (▦ entities); pose checkboxes inside groups; or everything (filtered results skip dimmed group members). <b>Invert</b> flips group headers if any ▦ group is selected, otherwise pose checkboxes (disabled when nothing selected). <b>None</b> clears both.=• <b>单个</b> / <b>▦ 组</b> / <b>▦ 姿势</b> / <b>全部</b> —— 未分组姿势的复选框；可见的组标题（▦ 实体）；组内姿势的复选框；或全部（筛选结果会跳过变暗的组成员）。<b>反选</b>在选中了任一 ▦ 组时反转组标题，否则反转姿势复选框（无选择时不可用）。<b>无</b>清除两者。
Select <b>exactly one</b> library pose, then <b>Items</b> in the bottom bar. The docked pane links Studio <b>workspace items</b> (props, accessories, etc.) to that pose.=选中<b>恰好一个</b>库中姿势，然后点击底栏中的<b>道具</b>。停靠窗格会将 Studio 中的<b>工作区道具</b>（道具、饰品等）与该姿势关联。
<b>Add</b> — select <b>one character</b> in Studio and one or more <b>workspace items</b> (tree or guide). The label above <b>Add selected item(s)</b> lists what will be registered. Layout is saved relative to that character (anchor position/rotation, object scale, body height).=<b>添加</b> —— 在 Studio 中选择<b>一个角色</b>以及一个或多个<b>工作区道具</b>（树或控制器）。<b>添加所选道具</b>上方的标签会列出将要登记的内容。布局以该角色为参照保存（锚点位置/旋转、物体缩放、身高）。
<b>Stored items</b> — each row: <b>☑</b> include in bulk load, <b>name button</b> loads that entry, <b>✎</b> rename label, <b>X</b> remove from the pose. Names in <b>bold</b> match an item currently selected in Studio (still a button).=<b>已存道具</b> —— 每行：<b>☑</b> 包含在批量读取中，<b>名称按钮</b>读取该条目，<b>✎</b> 重命名标签，<b>X</b> 从姿势中移除。名称为<b>粗体</b>的条目与 Studio 中当前选中的道具相符（仍是按钮）。
<b>Load options</b> — toggle <b>Position</b>, <b>Rotation</b>, <b>Scale</b> independently. <b>Load as free</b> spawns without workspace tree parenting even when the item was saved on a body part (same world layout, scaled with the character).=<b>读取选项</b> —— 分别切换<b>位置</b>、<b>旋转</b>、<b>缩放</b>。<b>作为自由物体读取</b>会在不设置工作区树父级的情况下生成，即使道具保存在身体部位上（世界布局相同，并随角色缩放）。
<b>Load Selection</b> / <b>Load All</b> — need one Studio character; unchecked rows are skipped. Items respawn from the catalog (bundle paths in <b>pose_items.tsv</b> v5). Attached items restore workspace tree parenting when not loading as free.=<b>读取所选</b> / <b>全部读取</b> —— 需要一个 Studio 角色；未勾选的行会被跳过。道具从目录中重新生成（资源包路径保存在 <b>pose_items.tsv</b> v5 中）。不作为自由物体读取时，附着的道具会恢复工作区树父级。
⚠ Yellow banner if the selected character does not have this pose applied (save/load still allowed). Orange ⚠ on a row \= warning from the last load (e.g. body part missing). Data: <b>pose_items.tsv</b> in Sandbox config.=⚠ 所选角色未应用此姿势时显示黄色横幅（仍可保存/读取）。行上的橙色 ⚠ \= 上次读取的警告（如缺少身体部位）。数据：Sandbox 配置中的 <b>pose_items.tsv</b>。
Card width, items per page (0 \= all on one scroll), <b>Apply stored relative positions when applying a group</b>, <b>Adjust relative layout for body height (saved per pose)</b> (requires relative positions), select/deselect all filtered, and a read-only list of <b>keyboard shortcuts</b>. Assign keys in BepInEx <b>Configuration Manager</b> → section <b>Pose Browser · Keyboard shortcuts</b> (next/previous pose; next/previous browse target; undo/redo; toggle undocked pose stash; no text field focused).=卡片宽度、每页项目数（0 \= 全部显示在一个滚动列表中）、<b>应用组时应用已保存的相对位置</b>、<b>按身高调整相对布局（每个姿势单独保存）</b>（需要相对位置）、全选/取消全选筛选结果，以及只读的<b>键盘快捷键</b>列表。在 BepInEx <b>配置管理器</b> → <b>Pose Browser · 键盘快捷键</b>部分中设置按键（下一个/上一个姿势；下一个/上一个浏览目标；撤销/重做；切换浮动姿势暂存；需无文本框获得焦点）。
Card width and page cap are mirrored in BepInEx under <b>Pose Browser</b>. Window positions, layout tier (<b>Full</b>/<b>List</b>/<b>Mini</b>), sort mode, and the group layout toggle live in <b>pose_browser_options.json</b> next to the other Sandbox config files.=卡片宽度和每页上限会同步到 BepInEx 的 <b>Pose Browser</b> 下。窗口位置、布局层级（<b>完整</b>/<b>列表</b>/<b>迷你</b>）、排序模式和组布局开关保存在其他 Sandbox 配置文件旁边的 <b>pose_browser_options.json</b> 中。
Pagination: max items per page (0 \= show all). Config: Items per page (grid).=分页：每页最大项目数（0 \= 全部显示）。配置：每页项目数（网格）。

In Studio, "Modify Selected" will affect only the items / characters that are currently selected in the Workspace, whereas "Modify ALL" will affect every single item and character in the entire scene.=在工作室中，“修改选中项”只影响当前在工作区中选中的物品/角色，而“全部修改”会影响整个场景中的每一个物品和角色。
Right-clicking "Modify Selected" or "Modify ALL" in either Maker or Studio will RESET the specified shader properties instead of setting them.=在角色编辑或工作室中右键点击“修改选中项”或“全部修改”，会重置指定的着色器属性，而不是设置它们。
Next to the property selector there is a dropdown arrow, which will let you restore previously used values and property names. The value/color histories will always be saved, but saving the texture history has to be toggled from the settings.=属性选择器旁边有一个下拉箭头，可以恢复之前用过的数值和属性名。数值/颜色历史总会被保存，但纹理历史需要在设置中开启保存。
If you use the same property name / value combination multiple times in succession, it will only get saved to the history once. You can also mark edits as favorites by clicking the star next to them to make sure they stick around while you make other edits.=如果连续多次使用相同的属性名/数值组合，历史中只会保存一次。你还可以点击编辑旁边的星标将其收藏，确保在进行其他编辑时它不会被挤掉。
On the "Tex" tab you can specify the texture in two ways: By selecting a file from the disk, or by copying an existing texture from a material. When copying, the first texture that matches the filters and the property name will be selected.=在“Tex”标签页中可以用两种方式指定纹理：从磁盘选择文件，或从材质复制现有纹理。复制时，会选中第一个符合筛选条件和属性名的纹理。
On the "Shader" tab you can swap shaders for other shaders by filtering similar to the other tabs and choosing a shader to be set. The shader name to be set has to match the shader name EXACTLY. For this reason it is recommended to use the dropdown, where you can also favorite shaders so they appear at the top of the list.=在“Shader”标签页中，可以像其他标签页一样进行筛选，然后选择要设置的着色器来替换着色器。要设置的着色器名称必须完全一致。因此建议使用下拉列表，还可以在其中收藏着色器，让它们显示在列表顶部。
In both Maker and Studio, you can set the UI Scale, which will modify the size of the plugin's UI. You can also toggle whether tooltips will be shown and whether you want to save texture edit history to disk.=在角色编辑和工作室中都可以设置界面缩放，以改变插件界面的大小。还可以切换是否显示提示，以及是否将纹理编辑历史保存到磁盘。
In Maker, you can choose whether to affect miscellaneous body parts such as eyes/tongue/noseline/penis/etc. If set to Yes, then they will only be affected when you're on the Face or Body tabs in the editor, and if you hit "Modify ALL".=在角色编辑中，可以选择是否影响眼睛/舌头/鼻线/阴茎等其他身体部位。设为“是”时，只有在编辑器的面部或身体标签页中点击“全部修改”才会影响它们。
The other setting in Maker is "Hair accs are hair". When using "Modify Selected', this setting has no effect, but when using "Modify ALL', and if this setting is turned on to Yes, then hair accessories will ONLY be modified if you're on the Hair tab in the editor.=角色编辑中的另一个设置是“头发饰品视为头发”。使用“修改选中项”时此设置无效；使用“全部修改”且此设置为“是”时，只有在编辑器的头发标签页中才会修改头发饰品。
In Studio, you can set whether to "Dive" folders and items. This modifies the behaviour of the "Modify Selected" button: If the selected items include an item which is set as diveable, then its children will also be modified. This is recursive, so if there are diveable items among the children, then their children are included as well.=在工作室中，可以设置是否“深入”文件夹和物品。这会改变“修改选中项”按钮的行为：如果选中的物品中有设为可深入的物品，它的子级也会被修改。此过程是递归的，子级中若有可深入的物品，它们的子级也会包括在内。
Furthermore, you can choose if you want characters to be affected at all, and if yes, then which parts of them. "Misc parts" refers to parts of characters such as tongue, eyebrows, and such, like in Maker, but unlike in Maker, "Modify Selected" will affect them as well.=此外，可以选择是否影响角色，以及影响角色的哪些部分。“其他部位”指舌头、眉毛等角色部位，与角色编辑中相同，但与角色编辑不同的是，“修改选中项”也会影响它们。
faces and bodies=面部和身体
hair, including hair accs=头发，包括头发饰品
accessories, excluding any hair=饰品，不含任何头发
held items (items parented to characters)=手持物品（设为角色子级的物品）
selected items'=选中物品的

Welcome to Mass Shader Editor! To get started, I first recommend checking out the Help section, which will tell you how to best use this plugin, and any specifics on what each of the buttons and options do.=欢迎使用 Mass Shader Editor！开始之前，建议先查看帮助部分，它会告诉你如何更好地使用本插件，以及每个按钮和选项的具体作用。
To access the help section, click the yellow '?' symbol in the top right corner of the plugin window.=要打开帮助，请点击插件窗口右上角的黄色“?”符号。
Afterwards, you should check out the various settings of the plugin, accessible either in the F1 menu or by clicking the cogwheel icon next to the help button. The available settings are different in Maker and in Studio!=之后可以看看插件的各项设置，可在 F1 菜单中打开，也可以点击帮助按钮旁的齿轮图标。编辑器和 Studio 中可用的设置不同！
Happy creating!=祝创作愉快！
 - Offset: ({0},{1}) Scale: ({2},{3})= - 偏移：({0},{1}) 缩放：({2},{3})
Mass Shader Editor can do four different things with shaders:=Mass Shader Editor 可以对着色器做四种操作：
- Edit numeric properties=- 编辑数值属性
- Edit color properties=- 编辑颜色属性
- Copy and paste textures=- 复制和粘贴纹理
- Swap shaders=- 更换着色器
It works on items and characters both, and it's possible to filter exactly which items and properties it will modify. It can also modify renderer properties.=物品和角色均可使用，并能精确筛选要修改的物品和属性。还可以修改渲染器属性。
To use the plugin, you have to choose one of the four tabs from the top bar, optionally set up filters, pick a property name to edit, and give it's value.=使用本插件时，需要从顶栏的四个标签页中选择一个，可选地设置筛选，选择要编辑的属性名并给出数值。
Once all of those are set up, you can click either "Modify Selected" or "Modify ALL". The way these buttons work is slightly different in Studio and in Maker.=全部设置好后，可以点击“修改所选”或“修改全部”。这两个按钮在 Studio 和编辑器中的作用略有不同。
In Maker, when you click "Modify Selected" the plugin will look at the currently selected item and look for shaders to edit in that item.=在编辑器中点击“修改所选”时，插件会查看当前选中的物品，并在其中查找要编辑的着色器。
For example, if you're editing the Legwear of a character, it will only affect that.=例如，如果正在编辑角色的袜子，就只会影响袜子。
In contrast, "Modify ALL" will affect the entire category of items you're currently editing: in the previous example, it would affect every piece of clothing on the current outfit.=相反，“修改全部”会影响当前正在编辑的整个物品分类：在上一个例子中，会影响当前服装的每一件衣物。
The different categories of items that can be affected are: Body, Hair, Clothing, Accessories.=可影响的物品分类有：身体、头发、服装、饰品。
Shift + "Modify ALL" will make the base list of items to edit be the whole character and current outfit.=Shift + “修改全部”会以整个角色和当前服装作为要编辑物品的基础列表。
There are three kinds of filters you can set up: Renderer, Material, Shader.=可以设置三种筛选：渲染器、材质、着色器。
They will filter which shaders to affect based on the names of their respective renderers and materials, and their own names.=会根据各自渲染器和材质的名称以及着色器本身的名称来筛选要影响的着色器。
To choose which shader to set up, click the R/M/S buttons next to the filter input.=要选择设置哪种筛选，请点击筛选输入框旁的 R/M/S 按钮。
An asterisk (*) after the letter indicates that the filter is active. All active filters will be applied when modifying.=字母后的星号（*）表示该筛选已激活。修改时会应用所有已激活的筛选。
To clear all filters, click the × button.=要清除所有筛选，请点击 × 按钮。
Filter syntax is the following:=筛选语法如下：
The filter string is broken into blocks along ";" and "|" characters, which are further broken down into elements along "," and "&" characters.=筛选字符串按“;”和“|”字符拆分成若干块，每块再按“,”和“&”字符拆分成若干元素。
Renderers' / materials' / shaders' names have to satisfy all elements of ANY block to be edited. An element beginning with "-" or "~" will be a negative filter.=渲染器/材质/着色器的名称必须满足任一块中的所有元素才会被编辑。以“-”或“~”开头的元素为反向筛选。
When setting up a shader name filter, a dropdown will appear with all MaterialEditor-available shader names to choose from.=设置着色器名称筛选时，会出现一个下拉菜单，列出所有可在 MaterialEditor 中使用的着色器名称供选择。
You can filter for different shaders as well, but you have to input their names manually. In non-shader filter cases, the dropdown will be of your previously used filters, where you can also favorite them, so they stick around.=也可以筛选其他着色器，但需要手动输入其名称。在非着色器筛选中，下拉菜单会列出你之前使用过的筛选，还可以将其收藏，使其一直保留。
Every filter is a wildcard, meaning that if you want to affect all "KKUTShair', "KKUTS', and "KKUTSeye" shaders you can simply input "KKUTS" or "UTS" in the shader filter.=每个筛选都是通配匹配，也就是说，如果想影响所有“KKUTShair”、“KKUTS”和“KKUTSeye”着色器，只需在着色器筛选中输入“KKUTS”或“UTS”。
You can auto-fill filters by clicking their names in Material Editor. For shaders you have to click the "Shader:" label to the left of the dropdown.=在 Material Editor 中点击名称可自动填入筛选。着色器则需要点击下拉菜单左侧的“Shader:”标签。
The property field specifies which property to affect. This is case-sensitive and you have to type the full name of the property here.=属性字段指定要影响的属性。区分大小写，且必须输入属性的全名。
These can also be auto-filled by clicking the appropriate labels in Material Editor. If you shift+click a "Shader:" label then its value will be put into the "Shader" field on the "Shader" tab, instead of the "Filter" field.=也可以在 Material Editor 中点击相应的标签自动填入。如果按住 Shift 点击“Shader:”标签，其值会填入“着色器”标签页的“着色器”字段，而不是“筛选”字段。
On the "Val" tab you can specify the value in two ways: Manual input or slider. The limits of the slider are configurable. You can also copy a value from the first matching property that would be edited.=在“值”标签页中可以用两种方式指定数值：手动输入或滑块。滑块的范围可配置。也可以从第一个将被编辑的匹配属性中复制数值。
After choosing a value, you also have to choose what way the value will be applied to the shader property.=选择数值后，还需要选择该数值以何种方式应用到着色器属性上。
There are five options for choosing what to do with the specified value:=对指定数值的处理方式有五种：
- "\=": Replace with the specified value=- “\=”：替换为指定值
- "+": Add to the current value=- “+”：加到当前值上
- "×": Multiply the current value=- “×”：乘以当前值
- "Min": Replace values lower than the specified=- “Min”：替换低于指定值的数值
- "Max": Replace values higher than the specified=- “Max”：替换高于指定值的数值
On the "Col" tab you can specify the color in three ways: Hex input, color picker, or value input. To open the color picker, click the color display.=在“色”标签页中可以用三种方式指定颜色：十六进制输入、取色器或数值输入。点击颜色显示可打开取色器。
You can manually set components above 1 or below 0, but if you do then the text input will be set to ########. You can also copy a color from the first matching property that would be edited.=可以手动将分量设为大于 1 或小于 0，但这样文本输入框会显示为 ########。也可以从第一个将被编辑的匹配属性中复制颜色。
There are five options for choosing what to do with the specified color:=对指定颜色的处理方式有五种：
- "Set": Replace the current color=- “Set”：替换当前颜色
- "Mix": Mix with the current color=- “Mix”：与当前颜色混合
- "Add1": Add to the color, capping at 1=- “Add1”：加到颜色上，上限为 1
- "Add2": Add to the color, no cap=- “Add2”：加到颜色上，无上限
- "Sub": Subtract from the color, floor 0=- “Sub”：从颜色中减去，下限为 0
The mix ratio can be set by the slider that pops up on the left. (Higher \= more effect)=混合比例可通过左侧弹出的滑块设置。（越高效果越强）
On this same tab you can also specify texture scales/offsets to be set when modifying a texture.=在同一标签页中，还可以指定修改纹理时要设置的纹理缩放/偏移。
Whether the texture or the scale/offset will be modified can be toggled with two checkboxes at the end of the respective lines.=是否修改纹理或缩放/偏移，可通过各行末尾的两个复选框切换。
Together with shader swapping, the plugin is also able to modify filtered shaders" render queue, which can be set on this tab. If left empty, 0, or non-numeric, it will not be modified.=除更换着色器外，本插件还能修改筛选出的着色器的渲染队列，可在此标签页中设置。如果留空、为 0 或非数字，则不会修改。
The render queue can also be modified on the "Value" tab by inputting "RQ', "Render Queue', "RenderQueue', or differently capitalised versions of those in the Property selector.=也可以在“值”标签页的属性选择框中输入“RQ”、“Render Queue”、“RenderQueue”或其不同大小写形式来修改渲染队列。
On the "Rend" tab you can edit some properties of renderers:=在“渲染”标签页中可以编辑渲染器的部分属性：
- Enable/Disable them=- 启用/禁用渲染器
- Change their shadow casting mode=- 更改其阴影投射模式
- Change if they receive shadows or not=- 更改其是否接收阴影
By default, all these properties will be edited, but you can uncheck the boxes on the right to skip editing any of these options.=默认会编辑所有这些属性，但可以取消勾选右侧的复选框来跳过其中任一项。
On the "Val" and "Col" tabs, clicking the "ℝ" button next to "Copy" will open the randomisation panel.=在“值”和“色”标签页中，点击“复制”旁的“ℝ”按钮会打开随机化面板。
While open, the specified kind and amount of random noise will be applied to the edited properties after every edit.=面板打开时，每次编辑后都会对被编辑的属性施加指定种类和数量的随机噪声。
In the case of colors, you can also choose what color space to treat them in, and which components to affect with the noise.=对于颜色，还可以选择在哪个色彩空间中处理，以及噪声影响哪些分量。
The settings tab can be opened via the cogwheel icon in the top right next to the "?" button, and it lets you toggle most settings of the plugin.=点击右上角“?”按钮旁的齿轮图标可打开设置标签页，可在其中切换插件的大多数设置。
These settings are different for Maker and Studio, but there are some common elements.=编辑器和 Studio 的设置不同，但有一些共同的元素。
MassShaderEditor Version 1.5.1.11=MassShaderEditor 版本 1.5.1.11
Plugin made by Starstorm=插件作者：Starstorm

// RSkoi_ComponentUtil.KKS.dll
ColorConversion.StringToColor() was provided incomplete color string, returning default=ColorConversion.StringToColor() 收到了不完整的颜色字符串，返回默认值
Toggle the UI of ComponentUtil.=开关 ComponentUtil 的界面。
Wait time after scene load=场景载入后的等待时间
How long ComponentUtil should wait in seconds after a scene is loaded before applying tracked changes. Try setting this higher if after loading a scene the changes saved with ComponentUtil seem to be overwritten.=场景载入后，ComponentUtil 在应用已追踪的更改前等待的秒数。如果载入场景后用 ComponentUtil 保存的更改似乎被覆盖了，请尝试调高此值。
Apply data on scene load=场景载入时应用数据
Whether ComponentUtil should apply related saved data on scene load. Set to false for debugging purposes or if a scene fails to load due to ComponentUtil.=ComponentUtil 是否在场景载入时应用相关的已保存数据。用于调试，或场景因 ComponentUtil 而无法载入时，可设为 false。
Save data on scene save=场景保存时保存数据

This window displays contents of game classes in real time. You can go deeper by clicking on the buttons with member names on the left (here it's 'Help1'), and in some cases you can edit the data (try clicking on this text and typing).To apply changes you have to click outside of the field.=此窗口实时显示游戏类的内容。点击左侧带成员名的按钮（这里是“Help1”）可以深入查看，某些情况下还可以编辑数据（试着点击这段文字并输入）。要应用更改，需要点击字段外部。
When you go deeper you can return to the original class by clicking on the history list above.=深入查看后，可以点击上方的历史列表返回原来的类。
On the far left you can see the type that a member holds. In this case this is a string. Only some types can be edited.You can copy the value even if it can't be edited.=最左侧显示成员所持有的类型。这里是 string。只有部分类型可以编辑。即使不能编辑也可以复制值。
You can run methods by clicking their names on the list. Try clicking on GetHashCode below to run it. Return value will appear. If a class is returned by the method click the method's name to open it (try S/Create method below).=点击列表中的方法名即可运行该方法。试着点击下面的 GetHashCode 运行它，会显示返回值。如果方法返回一个类，点击方法名即可打开它（试试下面的 S/Create 方法）。
WARNING - Running methods arbitraliy can have unforeseen consequences! You might even crash the game in some cases!=警告：随意运行方法可能产生无法预料的后果！某些情况下甚至可能导致游戏崩溃！

'IS ENUMERABLE' means that opening the member will give you anywhere from 0 to infinitely many values. They might even be generated as you view them. If the number of values is known, it will be displayed instead of this text.=“IS ENUMERABLE”表示打开该成员会得到 0 到无限多个值，甚至可能在查看时才生成。如果值的数量已知，会显示数量而不是这段文字。

This window lists breakpoints set on methods and on property getters/setters.=此窗口列出在方法以及属性 getter/setter 上设置的断点。
A breakpoint is tiggered whenever a method/property is called. The stack trace as well as any parameters and result value of that trigger are then stored in the list of hits.=每当方法/属性被调用时就会触发断点。该次触发的堆栈跟踪以及所有参数和结果值都会存入命中列表。
To add breakpoints right click on methods and properties, and look for the 'Attach breakpoint' options. It's easiest to do in inspector by right clicking on the member names.=要添加断点，请右键单击方法和属性，找到“附加断点”选项。最简单的方法是在检查器中右键单击成员名称。

Left click to open in Inspector=左键单击在检查器中打开
Right click to for more options=右键单击查看更多选项
Scene: =场景：
Multiple scenes can be loaded at the same time, in which case objects that belong to them all exist at the same time.=可以同时读取多个场景，此时属于这些场景的对象会同时存在。
The dropdown lists all currently loaded scenes. Select one of them to only show objects that belong to it.=下拉菜单列出所有当前已读取的场景。选择其中一个可只显示属于该场景的对象。
When a scene is unloaded, all of its objects are marked to be destroyed (in some cases they are not cleaned up until Resources.UnloadUnusedResources).=场景被卸载时，其所有对象都会被标记为待销毁（某些情况下要到 Resources.UnloadUnusedResources 时才会被清理）。
Show GameObjects from all loaded scenes.=显示所有已读取场景中的 GameObject。
Select a scene from the dropdown to only show GameObjects that belong to that scene (they will be destroyed if the scene is unloaded).=从下拉菜单中选择一个场景，可只显示属于该场景的 GameObject（场景卸载时它们会被销毁）。
Show GameObjects that are not part of any scene, but are marked as DontDestroyOnLoad.=显示不属于任何场景、但被标记为 DontDestroyOnLoad 的 GameObject。
These objects will not be destroyed when a scene is unloaded.=这些对象在场景卸载时不会被销毁。
Show GameObjects that are not part of any scene.=显示不属于任何场景的 GameObject。
These objects will not be destroyed when a scene is unloaded, but they are not visible in the hierarchy and cannot be selected.=这些对象在场景卸载时不会被销毁，但它们不显示在层级中，也无法被选中。
Object viewer - =对象查看器 -
Order: =顺序：
[Profiler] Failed to hook =[Profiler] 无法挂钩
Relative order of execution in a frame. Methods are called one by one on the main unity thread in this order.=一帧中的相对执行顺序。方法按此顺序在 Unity 主线程上逐个调用。
Methods that did not run during this frame are also included, so this number does not equal how many methods were called on this frame.=本帧中未运行的方法也会计入，因此此数字并不等于本帧调用了多少个方法。
Left toggle indicates if this method was executed in this frame (all Harmony patches were called, and the original method was called if not disabled by a Harmony patch).=左侧开关表示此方法在本帧中是否执行过（所有 Harmony 补丁都已调用，且原始方法未被 Harmony 补丁禁用时也已调用）。
Right toggle indicates if the original method was executed (original method being skipped is usually caused by a false postfix in a Harmony patch)=右侧开关表示原始方法是否执行过（原始方法被跳过通常是由 Harmony 补丁中返回 false 的前缀导致的）
Time spent executing this method (all Harmony patches included).=执行此方法所花的时间（包括所有 Harmony 补丁）。
By default it's shown in ticks (smallest measurable unit of time). Resolution of ticks depends on Stopwatch.Frequency, but usually 10000 \= 1ms.=默认以刻（可测量的最小时间单位）显示。刻的精度取决于 Stopwatch.Frequency，但通常 10000 \= 1ms。
High values will drop FPS. If the value is much higher on some frames it can be felt as the game stuttering.=数值高会降低帧率。如果某些帧的数值高很多，就会感觉游戏卡顿。
In methods running on every frame this should be as low as possible.=对于每帧都运行的方法，此值应尽可能低。
Bytes of memory allocated in the managed heap during this method's execution (all Harmony patches included). The value is approximate and might be inaccurate, especially if there is code running on background threads.=执行此方法期间在托管堆上分配的内存字节数（包括所有 Harmony 补丁）。此值为近似值，可能不准确，尤其是在有代码在后台线程上运行时。
High values (usually caused by constantly allocating and discarding objects, e.g. using linq queries) will trigger garbage collections, causing the game to randomly stutter. Magnitude of the stutters can be lowered by very fast CPUs and the incremental GC being enabled (only Unity 2019+).=数值高（通常由不断分配和丢弃对象引起，例如使用 linq 查询）会触发垃圾回收，导致游戏随机卡顿。非常快的 CPU 以及启用增量 GC（仅 Unity 2019+）可以减轻卡顿的程度。
In methods running on every frame this should be 0 (or as close to 0 as possible).=对于每帧都运行的方法，此值应为 0（或尽可能接近 0）。
Name format:=名称格式：
Name of GameObject that the component running this method is attached to > Full name of the component and name of the method (OnGUI event type)=运行此方法的组件所附着的 GameObject 名称 > 组件全名和方法名（OnGUI 事件类型）

Specify filters for which shaders (partial name) should be affected. Separate tokens with commas. Spaces and casedon't matter. A '-' sign in the front denotes a negative filter. Changes take effect on scene reload and on newlyadded or changed items (shaders).=指定哪些着色器（部分名称）会受到影响。用逗号分隔各项。空格和大小写无关紧要。前面加“-”表示排除。重新载入场景以及新添加或更改的物品（着色器）时生效。
