- Play, pause, and stop your animation with the buttons on the left of the main window. You can also use the shortcut <b>alt + t</b> to play/pause.=- 用主窗口左侧的按钮播放、暂停和停止动画。也可以用快捷键 <b>alt + t</b> 播放/暂停。
- You can move the cursor to a specific time by clicking on the <b>black bar</b> above the grid.=- 点击网格上方的<b>黑色条</b>可以把光标移到指定时间。
- You can also move the cursor to a specific time by changing the <b>time text</b> manually while paused.=- 暂停时也可以手动修改<b>时间文本</b>，把光标移到指定时间。
- You can <b>also</b> move the cursor by using the previous/next "frame" buttons (below play/pause/stop). Use the input field between them to set the "framerate". For example, with a value of 60, pressing the next button will move the cursor 1/60th of a second forward.=- <b>还可以</b>用上一“帧”/下一“帧”按钮（位于播放/暂停/停止下方）移动光标。用两者之间的输入框设置“帧率”。例如设为 60 时，按下一帧按钮会让光标前进 1/60 秒。
- You can zoom in and out by holding <b>left control</b> and <b>scrolling</b> on the grid or just scrolling on the top black bar.=- 按住<b>左 Ctrl</b> 并在网格上<b>滚动滚轮</b>，或直接在顶部黑色条上滚动，即可放大和缩小。
- You can scroll horizontally on the grid by holding <b>left shift</b> and scrolling normally.=- 按住<b>左 Shift</b> 并正常滚动，即可在网格上水平滚动。
- On the left of the window, you can see a list of <b>Interpolables</b>. In this context, an interpolable represents a variable that can be changed (interpolated between multiple values) over time.=- 窗口左侧是<b>插值项</b>列表。在这里，插值项代表一个可以随时间变化（在多个值之间插值）的变量。
- Each interpolable has a set of keyframes, left click on one to open the Keyframe window:=- 每个插值项都有一组关键帧，左键点击其中一个可打开关键帧窗口：
- You can copy/cut/paste keyframes by selecting one or more, using the traditional shortcuts <b>control + c</b> or <b>control + x</b>, changing the position of the cursor, and using <b>control + v</b>. When doing this, the leftmost keyframe will be copied on the cursor, and the other keyframes will be copied after that.=- 选中一个或多个关键帧后，可以用常规快捷键 <b>control + c</b> 或 <b>control + x</b> 复制/剪切，移动光标位置后用 <b>control + v</b> 粘贴。此时最左边的关键帧会被复制到光标处，其他关键帧依次排在其后。
- Holding the <b>alt</b> key while pasting keyframes will <b>insert</b> them. This is a special kind of pasting operation that makes all the keyframes on the right of the cursor move so that what you're pasting has enough space to be there.=- 粘贴关键帧时按住 <b>alt</b> 键会<b>插入</b>它们。这是一种特殊的粘贴操作，会让光标右侧的所有关键帧后移，为粘贴的内容腾出足够的空间。
- Keyframes can be dragged around by holding <b>alt</b> and using your mouse. Obviously you should be careful with this feature since it will change the time of the selected keyframe(s).=- 按住 <b>alt</b> 并用鼠标可以拖动关键帧。显然使用此功能时要小心，因为它会改变所选关键帧的时间。
- Selected keyframes can be scaled (i.e. you can make them closer or further apart) with <b>alt + scroll</b>=- 用 <b>alt + 滚轮</b>可以缩放所选关键帧（即让它们靠得更近或更远）。
- You can move the cursor to a specific keyframe by <b>right clicking</b> on it.=- <b>右键点击</b>某个关键帧，可以把光标移到该关键帧。
- Keyframes can also be deleted by using <b>control + middle click</b> on them. Adding <b>shift</b> to the mix will delete all the selected keyframes plus the one clicked on.=- 对关键帧使用 <b>control + 中键点击</b>也可以删除它们。再加上 <b>shift</b> 会删除所有选中的关键帧以及点击的那一个。
- Similar to <b>paste insert</b> described above, it's also possible to <b>void delete</b> keyframes. By holding the <b>alt</b> key while deleting keyframes, the ones on the right of the cursor will move back so it fills up the blank space that was left. This operation also work with <b>control + x</b>.=- 与上面说的<b>插入粘贴</b>类似，也可以<b>填空删除</b>关键帧。删除关键帧时按住 <b>alt</b> 键，光标右侧的关键帧会前移以填补留下的空白。此操作也适用于 <b>control + x</b>。
- Interpolables can also be deleted by using <b>control + middle click</b> on them.=- 对插值项使用 <b>control + 中键点击</b>也可以删除它们。
- The list of available interpolables is linked to the object you have selected in your <b>Workspace</b>, so make sure to try and select everything.=- 可用插值项的列表与你在<b>工作区</b>中选中的对象相关联，所以请尝试选择各种对象。
- A lot of operations can be performed on interpolables via a special context menu. Try using <b>right click</b> on one:=- 可以通过专门的右键菜单对插值项执行很多操作。试着对其中一个<b>右键点击</b>：
- Groups also have a dedicated context menu:=- 分组也有专门的右键菜单：
- Finally, groups can be collapsed/expanded via a single <b>left click</b> on them.=- 最后，<b>左键单击</b>分组即可折叠/展开它。
- Interpolables on an object can be saved through the <b>Single Files</b> menu. If a character is selected, its active animation will also be saved in the same file.=- 可以通过<b>单独文件</b>菜单保存某个对象上的插值项。如果选中的是角色，其当前动画也会保存在同一个文件中。
- Filter interpolables by their name with the text field below the list.=- 用列表下方的文本框可以按名称筛选插值项。
- Different "time signatures" can be used if you change the <b>Block Length</b> and <b>Divisions</b> fields. Block Length determines the length in seconds of a block, and Divisions is the number of smaller parts in a block. This can be useful if you want your grid to sync up with a specific tempo, for example.=- 更改<b>块长度</b>和<b>细分</b>字段可以使用不同的“拍号”。块长度决定一个块的秒数，细分是一个块中更小部分的数量。例如想让网格与特定节奏同步时会很有用。
- The whole UI can become semi transparent by holding <b>control</b> and using the scrollwheel on the word "Timeline" on the interface.=- 按住 <b>control</b> 并在界面上的“Timeline”字样上使用滚轮，可以让整个界面变得半透明。
That's it!=就这些！

    <b>Welcome to Timeline, a plugin used to make advanced custom animations, here are some things you might want to know:</b>=    <b>欢迎使用 Timeline，这是一个用于制作高级自定义动画的插件，以下是你可能想了解的一些内容：</b>
- Close this help by pressing <color\=#00A000FF><b>Help</b></color> again.=- 再次按 <color\=#00A000FF><b>Help</b></color> 即可关闭此帮助。
- The playback time is represented by a <color\=#FF0000FF><b>red bar</b></color> travelling through the window (called the <b>cursor</b>).=- 播放时间由在窗口中移动的<color\=#FF0000FF><b>红色竖条</b></color>表示（称为<b>光标</b>）。
- Values are represented by <color\=#FF0000FF><b>red diamonds</b></color> and are called <b>Keyframes</b>, do a <b>middle click</b> on the grid to add one. Protip; use <b>left shift</b> to snap a new keyframe to the grid. It can be used to snap things in other contexts too, try it!=- 数值由<color\=#FF0000FF><b>红色菱形</b></color>表示，称为<b>关键帧</b>，在网格上<b>中键单击</b>即可添加。小技巧：按住<b>左 Shift</b> 可将新关键帧吸附到网格上。在其他场合也能用来吸附，试试看！
    - Here you can see the time of the keyframe, its value, and a curve that represents how the value will be interpolated between this keyframe and the next.=    - 这里可以看到关键帧的时间、数值，以及一条表示该数值如何从此关键帧插值到下一个关键帧的曲线。
    - The keyframe's timing can be modified by typing something in the input field or by clicking on <color\=#2D9FA0FF><b>Use Current Time</b></color>, this has the effect to take the current playback time (where the cursor is) and set it to the keyframe. <color\=#2D9FA0FF><b>Drag At Current Time</b></color> is almost the same except when multiple keyframes are selected. In this scenario, Use Current Time will try to set the same time for all the selected keyframes (if possible) while Drag At Current Time will keep the shape of the selection intact (that's why it's called Drag). More information later on what dragging in this context means.=    - 可以在输入框中输入内容，或点击<color\=#2D9FA0FF><b>Use Current Time</b></color>来修改关键帧的时间，后者会取当前播放时间（光标所在位置）并设给关键帧。<color\=#2D9FA0FF><b>Drag At Current Time</b></color>几乎相同，只是在选中多个关键帧时有所不同。这种情况下，Use Current Time 会尝试为所有选中的关键帧设置相同的时间（如果可能），而 Drag At Current Time 会保持所选内容的形状不变（所以叫“拖动”）。稍后会详细说明此处“拖动”的含义。
    - The value can be changed by clicking on <color\=#2D9FA0FF><b>Use Current Value</b></color>, this has the effect to take the current value of what you're changing (the position of an object for example) and set it to the keyframe.=    - 点击<color\=#2D9FA0FF><b>Use Current Value</b></color>可修改数值，这会取你正在修改的对象的当前值（例如物体的位置）并设给关键帧。
    - The curve can be changed in multiple ways:=    - 曲线可以通过多种方式修改：
        - Add a point by doing a <b>middle click</b> somewhere.=        - 在某处<b>中键单击</b>添加一个点。
        - You can drag and drop this point around to change its time and value.=        - 可以拖放这个点来改变其时间和数值。
        - You can use the scrollwheel on the point to change the orientation of both tangents.=        - 在点上滚动鼠标滚轮可改变两条切线的方向。
        - For more precise changes, use the fields on the right.=        - 要更精确地修改，请使用右侧的输入框。
        - To remove a point, hold <b>left control</b> and <b>middle click</b> on the point.=        - 要移除一个点，按住<b>左 Ctrl</b> 并在该点上<b>中键单击</b>。
        - You can copy/paste curves between keyframes by using the two <b>Copy/Paste</b> buttons.=        - 可以使用两个 <b>Copy/Paste</b> 按钮在关键帧之间复制/粘贴曲线。
        - You can invert a curve by using the <b>Invert</b> button.=        - 可以使用 <b>Invert</b> 按钮反转曲线。
        - A few commonly used templates are available below those buttons, feel free to use them.=        - 这些按钮下方提供了几个常用模板，可随意使用。
    - Those parameters are saved automatically, so be careful when you're changing something.=    - 这些参数会自动保存，修改时请小心。
    - It's possible to select and <b>edit multiple keyframes</b> at the same time (however, some operations are limited, try it yourself and you'll see). Multi selection is done by holding <b>left control</b> or <b>left shift</b> or by simply using the selection box like you would select files in the explorer.=    - 可以同时选择并<b>编辑多个关键帧</b>（不过部分操作受限，自己试试就知道了）。按住<b>左 Ctrl</b> 或<b>左 Shift</b> 即可多选，也可以像在资源管理器中选择文件那样直接拖出选择框。
- Some interpolables are also linked to the selected <b>GuideObject</b> (that handle that allows you to move/rotate/scale an object or part of it). Which means that you can (for example) activate IK on a character and manipulate each node independently (each node will have a set of keyframes). =- 部分插值器还与所选的 <b>GuideObject</b>（用来移动/旋转/缩放物体或其局部的控制柄）相关联。也就是说，你可以（例如）为角色启用 IK，并独立操作每个节点（每个节点都会有一组关键帧）。
- If you're feeling hardcore, you can enable the "All" checkbox at the top of the interpolables list, it will display interpolables across all items in your scene. =- 如果你想挑战硬核操作，可以勾选插值器列表顶部的“All”复选框，它会显示场景中所有物品的插值器。
    - <b>Select linked GuideObject</b> <i>(single interpolable only)</i>: if you're confused and want to know the GuideObject linked to a certain interpolable, use this option and it will select the GuideObject (if applicable) or the character/item in the workspace. The same thing can be achieved by holding <b>alt</b> while clicking on the interpolable. The opposite can also be done when holding <b>alt</b> and selecting a GuideObject. This will highlight for a few seconds the linked interpolable (if any).=    - <b>Select linked GuideObject</b> <i>（仅限单个插值器）</i>：如果你弄不清某个插值器关联的是哪个 GuideObject，使用此选项即可选中该 GuideObject（如果适用），或在工作区中选中对应的角色/物品。点击插值器时按住 <b>Alt</b> 也能达到同样效果。反过来，按住 <b>Alt</b> 选择 GuideObject 时，会高亮显示与之关联的插值器（如有）几秒钟。
    - <b>Rename</b> <i>(single interpolable only)</i>: allows you to change the name of an interpolable. If you already have a custom name set, emptying the text field will make the interpolable return to its original name.=    - <b>Rename</b> <i>（仅限单个插值器）</i>：可以修改插值器的名称。如果已设置自定义名称，清空文本框会让插值器恢复原来的名称。
    - <b>Group together</b> <i>(multiple interpolables only)</i>: while having multiple interpolable selected (with either <b>control + click</b> or <b>shift + click</b>), you can group them together. This action will create a dedicated line that represents the group that can be collapsed or expanded (like a folder). More on that later.=    - <b>Group together</b> <i>（仅限多个插值器）</i>：选中多个插值器时（使用 <b>Ctrl + 单击</b>或 <b>Shift + 单击</b>），可以将它们编为一组。此操作会创建一行专门表示该组，可以折叠或展开（就像文件夹）。稍后会详细说明。
    - <b>Select keyframes</b>: selects all the keyframes of the selected interpolable(s).=    - <b>Select keyframes</b>：选中所选插值器的全部关键帧。
    - <b>Select keyframes before cursor</b>: selects all the keyframes of the selected interpolables(s) (only those before the cursor).=    - <b>Select keyframes before cursor</b>：选中所选插值器的全部关键帧（仅光标之前的）。
    - <b>Select keyframes after cursor</b>: selects all the keyframes of the selected interpolables(s) (only those after the cursor).=    - <b>Select keyframes after cursor</b>：选中所选插值器的全部关键帧（仅光标之后的）。
    - <b>Color</b> <i></i>: this allows you to change the background color of the interpolable, very useful for organisation.=    - <b>Color</b> <i></i>：可以更改插值器的背景颜色，便于整理，非常实用。
    - <b>Add keyframe(s) at cursor</b>: for each interpolable selected, this option adds one or several new keyframe(s) where the cursor is. A similar action can also be achieved through the shortcut <b>alt + middle click</b> on the grid. However, this one will add the keyframes where the <b>mouse</b> is, and not where the cursor is. This can also be combined with other shortcuts like shift.=    - <b>Add keyframe(s) at cursor</b>：为每个选中的插值器在光标处添加一个或多个新关键帧。在网格上使用快捷键 <b>Alt + 中键单击</b>也能实现类似操作，但它会在<b>鼠标</b>所在位置而不是光标处添加关键帧。还可以与 Shift 等其他快捷键组合使用。
    - <b>Parent to</b>: used to parent interpolables to a certain group, or unparent them altogether.=    - <b>Parent to</b>：将插值器设为某个组的子级，或完全解除其父级。
    - <b>Disable (all)/Enable (all)</b>: used to disable/enable all the selected interpolables.=    - <b>Disable (all)/Enable (all)</b>：禁用/启用所有选中的插值器。
    - <b>Move up/down</b>: moves interpolables up or down in the list, also very useful for organisation.=    - <b>Move up/down</b>：在列表中上移或下移插值器，同样便于整理。
    - <b>Delete</b>: removes interpolables and their keyframes.=    - <b>Delete</b>：移除插值器及其关键帧。
    - <b>Rename</b>: rename the group.=    - <b>Rename</b>：重命名该组。
    - <b>Select Interpolables under</b>: select all the interpolables under this group.=    - <b>Select Interpolables under</b>：选中该组下的所有插值器。
    - <b>Select keyframes</b>: select all the keyframes of all the interpolables under this group.=    - <b>Select keyframes</b>：选中该组下所有插值器的全部关键帧。
    - <b>Select keyframes before cursor</b>: select all the keyframes of all the interpolables under this group (only those before the cursor).=    - <b>Select keyframes before cursor</b>：选中该组下所有插值器的全部关键帧（仅光标之前的）。
    - <b>Select keyframes after cursor</b>: select all the keyframes of all the interpolables under this group (only those after the cursor).=    - <b>Select keyframes after cursor</b>：选中该组下所有插值器的全部关键帧（仅光标之后的）。
    - <b>Add keyframes at cursor</b>: same as above but for all the interpolables under this group.=    - <b>Add keyframes at cursor</b>：与上面相同，但作用于该组下的所有插值器。
    - <b>Parent to</b>: parent the whole group to another group, or unparent it altogether.=    - <b>Parent to</b>：将整个组设为另一个组的子级，或完全解除其父级。
    - <b>Disable/Enable</b>: used disable/enable all the interpolables under this group.=    - <b>Disable/Enable</b>：禁用/启用该组下的所有插值器。
    - <b>Move up/down</b>: same as the one for interpolables.=    - <b>Move up/down</b>：与插值器的同名选项相同。
    - <b>Delete</b>: removes the whole group and <b>all the interpolables within!</b>.=    - <b>Delete</b>：移除整个组以及<b>其中的所有插值器！</b>。
Left click to play/pause Timeline.=左键单击播放/暂停 Timeline。
Right click to open Timeline window.=右键单击打开 Timeline 窗口。
The button is yellow if there is Timeline data present in the scene.=场景中存在 Timeline 数据时按钮显示为黄色。

In the Chara Maker (accessory adjust panel open), press to toggle TOHYK on/off for that accessory. Kept as an explicit toggle instead of always-on so it doesn't steal Tab (or whatever you rebind this to) from other UI while you're not actively using TOHYK.=在角色编辑中（饰品调整面板打开时），按下此键为该饰品开关 TOHYK。之所以设计为显式开关而不是始终开启，是为了在不使用 TOHYK 时不占用其他界面的 Tab 键（或你改绑的按键）。
Clear Position=清除位置
Blender-style "clear transform": instantly resets the selection's local position to zero, same as Alt+G in Blender.=Blender 风格的“清除变换”：立即将所选对象的局部位置重置为零，与 Blender 中的 Alt+G 相同。
Clear Rotation=清除旋转
Blender-style "clear transform": instantly resets the selection's local rotation to identity, same as Alt+R in Blender.=Blender 风格的“清除变换”：立即将所选对象的局部旋转重置为初始方向，与 Blender 中的 Alt+R 相同。
Clear Scale=清除缩放
Blender-style "clear transform": instantly resets the selection's local scale to one, same as Alt+S in Blender.=Blender 风格的“清除变换”：立即将所选对象的局部缩放重置为一，与 Blender 中的 Alt+S 相同。
Chara Maker accessory mode only (while TOHYK is toggled on with Tab): recenters the Studio-style camera on the currently selected accessory (or the average position of several, if multi-selected via AccMover), same idea as pressing F on a selected object in Studio. The numpad '.' key (KeyCode.KeypadPeriod, Blender's own 'View Selected' key) always does the same thing alongside whatever this is rebound to.=仅限角色编辑饰品模式（用 Tab 开启 TOHYK 时）：将工作室风格的镜头重新对准当前选中的饰品（若通过 AccMover 多选，则对准它们的平均位置），与在工作室中选中物件后按 F 的思路相同。小键盘的“.”键（KeyCode.KeypadPeriod，即 Blender 自己的“查看所选”键）始终与此处绑定的按键执行相同操作。
X Axis=X 轴
Y Axis=Y 轴
